# Plan status archive — the full ledger

Relocated **verbatim** from `CLAUDE.md` `## Active work` on 2026-08-17. CLAUDE.md now carries a one-line index per plan; this file holds the full multi-paragraph status entries. **This is the LIVE full ledger, not a graveyard**: when a plan's status changes, amend its entry here exactly as it used to be amended in CLAUDE.md, and keep the CLAUDE.md one-liner in sync. Entries appear in the original list order (numbered ≈ execution order). Completed plan DOCUMENTS are archived at `done-plans/`; this file archives their status entries regardless.

Five file-less FINDING entries sat interleaved in this list; they now live in `FINDINGS.md` and are marked below by placeholders at their original positions. In-entry references like "the FALSE BINARY finding above" point to those relocated findings.

- `05-structural-integration-check-plan.md` — IMPLEMENTED 2026-05-24 on `develop-2.0-init`. Added Section 7 (Structural Integration / DIR check) + Output Format subsection + Rule 1 extension to `src/agents/code-reviewer.md` (§7 placed at end of checklist for monotonic numbering, not after §2 as drafted). Source: SLUMP/ProjectGuard paper. Catches duplicate-by-new-file gap. **Smoke tests (DoD) PENDING — manual:** DUPLICATE-flag case + INTEGRATED no-false-positive case. **AMENDED 2026-08-23 — the check was EXTENDED by plan 86's F1 (`d6f51be`), so this entry's "duplicate-by-new-file" framing is now only half of it.** It reads as two named arms. **File-level** — unchanged, and what this plan shipped: for each newly CREATED file, the intentional-parallel vs duplicate-rewrite classification, with a file-level `DUPLICATE` still **Critical** (the change rewrote what already existed). **Function-level** — new, and covering the case this plan's scope excluded: for each newly added function inside a file the changeset only MODIFIES, a verbatim or near-verbatim copy of ONE existing sibling — differing only in literals or a single argument — is `DUPLICATE` at **High**, while the same copy is `INTENTIONAL_PARALLEL` and PASSES when the task file, plan or spec declares and justifies it; an `INTENTIONAL_PARALLEL` asserted WITHOUT that justification is **High**. The check's closing sentence now carries the split — the file-level arm skips files that only edit existing modules, the function-level arm covers new-function duplication inside them — where it previously closed by skipping those files outright, which is exactly the gap plan 86 found: an undeclared clone of a sibling function inside a modified file was claimed by no layer, `/devforge:review` ruling single-task-contained findings out of scope by construction. The Output Format subsection this plan added grew the matching per-function verdict line alongside the per-new-file one. The finding is one sibling copied, never a count of repeated logic — the constitution's DRY rule still owns when a recurring pattern earns an abstraction. **Plan 05's own DOCUMENT is NOT edited and still describes the file-only scope** — read it as the file-level arm's origin, never as the check's current contract. **Neither observation is discharged:** the DoD smoke tests above are still PENDING, and plan 86's Phase 7 re-runs that same DUPLICATE-flag / no-false-positive pair at FUNCTION scope as a user-driven HARD GATE that has NOT run, scored as a PAIR because a rule that catches the clone by flagging everything fails the no-false-positive case.

- `10-AUDIT-COMMAND-PORT-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–7 SHIPPED 2026-06-01** on `develop-2.0-init` (working-tree, not committed); only **Phase 8 (testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven**. - `12-AUDIT-MULTI-PASS-UNION-PLAN.md` — **Steps 1–7 SHIPPED 2026-06-01** on `develop-2.0-init` (working-tree, NOT committed); only **Step 8 (testForge20 e2e A/B) remains, user-driven (HARD GATE)**. Adds an opt-in `--passes N` (int, default 1, clamped `[1,3]`) multi-pass union mode to the shipped `/audit`: at `--passes 1` byte-identical to single-pass (merger never invoked); at `--passes >= 2` the dispatch/consume/validate phase runs K times and a single tolerant location-based merge unions all 4×K agent outputs — `_audit/_merge.py` (the `(file, line ± 3)` union merger) + `--passes` / `merge-passes` / `render-report --passes-run` wiring + `pass_count` / `[MULTI-PASS:k]` schema/report/rank threading + main.md K-loop; `merge-passes` replaces `compute-consensus` at ≥2 passes (findings get `pass_count`, `[MULTI-PASS:k]` + `[CROSS-AGENT]` tags, a `Likely` confidence floor, and the conditional Summary `- Passes run: N | Multi-pass-confirmed findings: <count>` line). Recall goal: ~60% (1 pass) → ~79% (2) / ~92% (3) at K× cost. ~840 `_audit` tests passing. Step 8 is the e2e A/B (identical-vs-varied briefs; recall vs the 79%/92% band + merge-fidelity vs the 84-cluster baseline).

- `19-AUDIT-FALSE-POSITIVE-PRECISION-PLAN.md` — **Steps 1–6 SHIPPED 2026-06-15** on `develop-2.0-init`; only **Step 7 (testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven (HARD GATE)**. Recovers PRECISION on the shipped `/audit` (the recall plans 11/12 bought stays untouched) — `/audit` was structurally tuned to over-report (finder ORDERED to assume bugs exist, only filter checks GROUNDING not CORRECTNESS, every downstream stage AMPLIFIED). Four changes, **A the independently-shippable spine**: **A** = a **refutation / cross-examination stage** (new `references/refutation-preamble.md` + new `_audit/_verify.py` module + four verbs — `route-refutation`, `render-verify-brief`, `consume-verdicts`, `apply-verdicts` — in `_cli.py` + new PHASE 4.2.5 after consensus/merge, before recurring/rank) inverting the default from "assume bug" to "assume correct unless proven": `route-refutation` groups the working list by author and assigns each group a NON-AUTHOR finder (priority order `[code-reviewer, architect, qa-reviewer, security-reviewer]`, author-excluded, only present finders eligible; sole-finder self-refutes), each refuter cross-examines its routed subset and writes fixed-format markdown verdicts (`confirmed`/`dismissed`/`uncertain`, default-dismiss unless the defect is re-demonstrated from quoted code), `consume-verdicts` parses + merges, `apply-verdicts` partitions — NO 18th agent (cross-examination reuses the four finders; the single-non-finder-skeptic design was invalidated by the charter check — no generalist non-finder correctness reviewer exists, D2). **B** = reworded the explicit false-positive bias (`adversarial-preamble.md` + the coupled `_scope.py` `_CLOSING_REMINDER` constant + report `## Methodology`) to "demonstrate the defect or do not report it" — recall preserved (Confidence tiers stay; finder still surfaces Likely/Speculative), precision recovered downstream in refutation. **C** = multi-pass de-amplification — `_merge` no longer floors confidence to `"Likely"` on multi-pass recurrence; `_rank` `pass_bonus` neutralized; `pass_count`/`[MULTI-PASS:k]` are now descriptive only (correlated re-generation across passes is recall evidence, not correctness evidence). **D** = confidence-gated report — `## Top N Priorities` + `## Findings by File` draw from CONFIRMED ∪ high-stakes `[CONTESTED]` findings; dismissed + low-stakes uncertain render in a `## Dismissed / Worth a Glance` appendix (never deleted). **D7 high-stakes recall guard** (`apply-verdicts`) — `security` + `[CONSTITUTION-VIOLATION]` findings the refuter returns `uncertain` on are SURFACED in the headline flagged `[CONTESTED]` (never buried), and a "dismiss" on a grounded `[CONSTITUTION-VIOLATION]` also surfaces as `[CONTESTED]` (a grounded rule-break is never silently dismissed). **Step 5** deleted the stale `src/_pending/commands/audit.md` draft (superseded by the live command per plan 10 Decision 1). ~1026 `tests/lib/_audit/` tests pass. Anchored to plan 17's confirm-before-it-gates principle (different mechanism — the helper/orchestrator scratch-chain split, not `merge-review-panel`).

- `20-REVIEW-COMMAND-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–6 SHIPPED 2026-06-15** on `develop-2.0-init`; only **Phase 7 (testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven (HARD GATE)**. Redesigns `/review` from the stale pre-pivot `src/_pending/commands/review.md` draft into a live, emitted, pipeline-wired **feature-level emergent cross-task review** — the pipeline step after `/implement` drains a feature's tasks, before `/verify`. Its sole job: the emergent cross-task issues the `/implement` per-task panel structurally cannot see (cross-task security holes, assembled-data-flow perf, cross-task duplication, architectural drift), since the per-task panel reviews each task's diff in isolation. **Phase 0** extracted the roster-agnostic refutation engine (`findings_schema`, `_consume`, `_validate`, `_consensus`, `_verify`) out of `_audit/` into `src/devforge/lib/_shared/` (flat layout — OQ-2 lean) and parameterized `route_refutation`'s priority (`priority=None` → the existing audit constant) so `/audit` stays behaviorally identical — its `tests/lib/_audit/` suite is the regression net. **Phases 1–5** built the `_review/` helper subpackage (`_preflight` setup-chain gate, `_state` status-flip, `_scope` assembled-feature merge-base diff, `_brief` 5-finder brief assembly, the shared refutation verbs exposed via `_cli` importing from `_shared`, `_report` writing `specs/[feature]/review.md`) + `review_helper{,.py}` launcher + `src/commands/review/main.md` + 4 `references/` (anti-relitigation preamble, emergent-issue checklist, refutation preamble, report-format). **Phase 6** promoted `review` in the emitter `_PROMOTED` (`scripts/emitters/claude.py:51`), deleted the stale `src/_pending/commands/review.md` draft (no live consumer), and reconciled docs (`src/CLAUDE.md` `/review` entry + Workflow chain, `CHANGELOG.md`, this list). **Key facts:** 5 finders (`code-reviewer, architect, qa-reviewer, security-reviewer, performance-analyst`), 4 refuters (`/audit`'s priority `[code-reviewer, architect, qa-reviewer, security-reviewer]` — `performance-analyst` finds but NEVER refutes, by design; a specialist surfaces perf findings, a generalist refutes them; ZERO `route_refutation` logic change); scope = the assembled-feature git diff (`git merge-base <base>..HEAD` union of the feature's WIP commits, not completion-note prose); `/review` is code-independent of `/audit` (both depend only on `_shared/`, the engine never forks); output is **findings-only** (`specs/[feature]/review.md` — CONFIRMED headline + surfaced high-stakes `[CONTESTED]` + a Dismissed/Worth-a-glance appendix, NO verdict — the verdict is `/verify`'s) and feeds `/verify` (folds findings into its verdict, warns if missing) + `/audit`'s recurring-issue scan (globs recent `specs/*/review.md`). Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops; 267 `tests/lib/_review/` tests pass. Anti-relitigation is prompt-enforced only (D5 — `/implement` does not persist per-task findings, so a mechanical dedup is infeasible); the structured `review-handoff.json` for `/verify` is deferred (D4/OQ-3, built when `/verify` is refactored to consume it).

- `22-VERIFY-COMMAND-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–7 SHIPPED 2026-06-17** on `develop-2.0-init`; only **Phase 8 (testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven (HARD GATE)**. Redesigns `/verify` from the stale pre-pivot `src/_pending/commands/verify.md` draft into a live, emitted, pipeline-wired command at `src/commands/verify/main.md` + `references/report-format.md` + a `src/devforge/lib/_verify/` helper subpackage + a `verify_helper{,.py}` launcher, structurally modeled on `/review` (plan 20). Its defining job: **the verdict** — `/review` is findings-only, `/verify` owns APPROVED / NEEDS WORK / REJECTED. It is the pipeline step AFTER `/review`, BEFORE `/summarize`/`/finalize`, and adds three things on top of `/review`'s findings: AC conformance, assembled mechanical checks, and the verdict (NO finder ensemble, NO refutation reuse — D1). **Phase 0** extracted the assembled-feature scope resolver (`resolve_feature_scope` + git helpers + `_render_scope_block`) out of `_review/_scope.py` into `src/devforge/lib/_shared/feature_scope.py` (flat layout — OQ-3 lean) and parameterized the scope-block heading label (`/review` → "Review Scope", `/verify` → "Verification Scope"), re-pointing `/review` to import from `_shared` so it stays byte-behaviorally identical (`tests/lib/_review/test_scope.py` is the net). **Phases 1–5** built the `_verify/` subpackage (13 verbs: `check-status-and-flip`, `preflight` [setup-chain + populated-constitution gate + Source-Root/wrapper-mode; reads `.devforge/memory.md`, NOT the `_review` stale `.claude/memory/MEMORY.md` — finding F], `resolve-feature-scope` [wraps `_shared`], `read-ac-config`, `parse-acs`, `read-review-findings`, `merge-ac-results`, `check-hygiene`, `compute-verdict` [deterministic; constitution violations always block APPROVED — D7], `render-report` [writes `specs/[feature]/verification.md`], `render-inline-summary`, `flip-spec-status` [task cross-check → flip spec `**Status**:` → Complete + tick passed AC boxes], `file-bugs` [`bugs/NNN-*.md`, `Source: verify`]). **Phase 3** fixed `src/agents/ac-verifier.md`'s `## Input` contract to the live `ac_verification_mode` / `ac_runtime_*` config keys (the dead `AC_VERIFICATION*` keys + `auto`/`browser-only`/`api-only` modes gone) + defined the 4-mode mapping (D5: `runtime-assisted` probes the running app via Chrome MCP + API + CLI, MCP-probe first; `tests` runs the assembled test command as an independent PHASE-4 blocker [the agent code-reads — fine-grained test→AC mapping is OQ-1]; `code-only` code-reads; `off` is a code-reading floor noted advisory in the verdict) — the plan-15 skeleton was not reshaped. **Phase 6** wrote `main.md` (10 runtime PHASES, `forge-verify` scratch-chain, the `ac-verifier` Task dispatch, the report-only `verify-touched --iteration 0` call, the verdict/flip/bug-filing flow) + `references/report-format.md`. **Phase 7** promoted `verify` in the emitter `_PROMOTED` (`scripts/emitters/claude.py:51`), removed the stale flat-file `src/commands/verify.md` entry from `src/manifest.json` (folder layout is `_PROMOTED`-handled), deleted the stale `src/_pending/commands/verify.md` draft, reconciled docs (`src/CLAUDE.md` `/verify` entry, `CHANGELOG.md`, this list), and ran the cross-ref sweep. **Key facts:** reuses (does not re-implement) the `_shared` scope resolver + the installed `implement_helper verify-touched` binary (report-only, `--iteration 0`, NO self-repair loop, NO `/implement` change); writes back to the spec (the deliberate D4 departure — the only review/verify command that mutates its input, because it owns the Complete lifecycle transition `/summarize`+`/finalize` gate on); scratch literal `${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/forge-verify`. Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops; ~hundreds of `tests/lib/_verify/` tests pass. **Deferred:** OQ-1 (fine-grained tests-mode test→AC mapping), OQ-2 (structured `verify-handoff.json` for `/summarize`+`/finalize` — they gate on the spec `**Status**: Complete` flip for now). Two pre-existing bugs flagged to route separately (NOT plan-22's scope): the stale `src/manifest.json` `review.md` flat-file entry (a `/review`-side plan-20 inheritance) and `_review/_preflight.py`'s stale `.claude/memory/MEMORY.md` memory-path check (finding F).

- `23-ADVERSARIAL-GRILLING-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–6 SHIPPED in the working tree 2026-06-17** on `develop-2.0-init` (committed in pieces: Phase 0+1, 2, 3, 4, 5); only **Phase 7 (testForge20 e2e) remains — the deferred user-driven HARD GATE**. A standalone, **opt-in** `/grill` command — the design-level mirror of `/review` — positioned between `/plan` and `/breakdown`; it attacks the completed `plan.md` so a fatally-flawed design dies before `/breakdown` decomposes it (run for high-stakes plans; NOT a mandatory gate). ⚠ **The opt-in stance was AMENDED 2026-08-26 by plan 85 and the two bolded clauses above are superseded: the RUN is now required before `/devforge:breakdown`.** The amendment NARROWS and the rest of this entry survives it intact — the USER still owns every non-clean verdict, all four dispositions survive including KILL, the cross-pick and re-entry arms are unchanged, `/grill` still never modifies `plan.md` or `spec.md`, and what became mandatory is that the grill RAN, never that its disposition binds (the gate reads the report's presence plus the recorded `adversary_status`, never freshness and never the disposition). Plan 23's own FILE still carries the opt-in framing and is owed the same in-place amendment. **Single `devils-advocate` adversary** (roster 17 → **18**; read-only `tools:` allowlist + CBM graph + context7 + WebFetch/WebSearch) + an **architect-EXCLUDED refutation pass** `[code-reviewer, qa-reviewer, security-reviewer]` reusing the `_shared/` engine (the architect is not a finder here and must not cross-examine its own conceptual domain; the exclusion is the `priority=` arg to `route_refutation` — ZERO engine fork). **The adversary does its own three-ring blast-radius traversal** (Ring 0 read-in-full / Ring 1 capped one-hop via `trace_path` / Ring 2 query-only via `search_graph`/`search_code`/`get_architecture`) — the scope step hands it a STATIC manifest, not a pre-resolved radius, because a Python helper cannot call the CBM graph — plus **self-gated context7 web-verification** of the plan's external claims (VERIFY-not-rediscover: a "better option exists" hit routes upstream, never into a plan rewrite). Helper subpackage `src/devforge/lib/_grill/` (`seed_schema` / `_state` / `_preflight` / `_scope` / `_brief` / `_report` / `_cli` — 12 verbs: `check-status-and-flip`, `preflight`, `resolve-scope`, `render-brief`, `consume-tmp`, `validate-findings`, `route-refutation`, `render-verify-brief`, `consume-verdicts`, `apply-verdicts`, `render-report`, `write-seed`) + `grill_helper{,.py}` launcher. **Output** = `specs/[feature]/grill.md` (confidence-gated findings — CONFIRMED headline + surfaced high-stakes `[CONTESTED]` + a Dismissed/Worth-a-glance appendix) + a recommended **4-way disposition** PROCEED / REVISE-PLAN / RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM / KILL via the D9 two-question "does fixing it destroy the plan?" routing tree; on RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM it also emits a backward `specs/[feature]/grill-seed.json` (`ReEntrySeed` schema, `source = "grill"`, `target_stage ∈ {spec, discovery, research}`) consumed ADDITIVELY / CONDITIONALLY / INERTLY (D10 removability — a helper-free direct JSON read in a conditional re-entry block that no-ops when no matching seed exists) by `/research` / `/discover` / `/specify` (plan 36 extends this to a 4th consumer: REVISE-PLAN → `target_stage="plan"` → `/plan`). **The USER owns the verdict** at the `/breakdown` approval gate — `/grill` recommends, never decides. Emitter `_PROMOTED += grill`. ~hundreds of `tests/lib/_grill/` tests pass; full repo suite 8054 passed. Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops. **Decisions settled:** D1 standalone (not embedded in `/plan`); D5 full-engine reuse with architect-excluded priority; D7 the KILL / RE-ENTER design-time-REJECT is the new capability; D8/D10 opt-in + removable; OQ1 (dedicated `devils-advocate`), OQ2 (architect-absent, resolved-by-construction), OQ3 (polymorphic `Finding.file`), OQ4, OQ5 (standalone — the backward seed IS the handoff) resolved. **Note:** the `/research` + `/discover` consumer blocks are authored + reviewed in the working tree but commit alongside the uncommitted `18-SCOPE-FIDELITY-AND-PROMPT-INTAKE-PLAN.md` intake-gate work that shares those two files (the `/specify` consumer block + all other phases are committed).

- `24-SUMMARIZE-COMMAND-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE 2026-06-18** — all phases shipped on `develop-2.0-init` (committed `8f978e1`); Phase 5 testForge20 e2e VALIDATED (`/summarize` ran on testForge20's wrapper-mode the consumer's first feature, wrote `summary.md`, took AC status verbatim from `verification.md`, included wrapper-mode source-repo changes, rendered no verdict). Redesigns `/summarize` from the stale pre-pivot `src/_pending/commands/summarize.md` draft into a live, emitted, pipeline-wired **PR-ready feature synthesis** — the pipeline step after `/verify` approves, before `/finalize`. Its sole job: render the completed feature as a PR-ready narrative (what was built in user terms, change stats, key decisions, deviations, AC status). **Phases 1–3** built the `_summarize/` helper subpackage (`_cli`; `_preflight` [setup-chain + spec-`Complete` gate, NO constitution-populated guard, stateless — D8]; `_changes` [`gather-change-data`, reusing `src/devforge/lib/_shared/feature_scope.py` for the git change stats]; `_inputs` [`read-verification` consuming `/verify`'s authoritative AC table from `verification.md` — D3; `parse-completion-notes` over each task's `## Completion Notes`; `read-plan-decisions` lifting the key decisions from `plan.md` — D9]) + `summarize_helper{,.py}` launcher + `src/commands/summarize/main.md` (6 runtime PHASES, `forge-summarize` scratch-chain, orchestrator-inline synthesis — D1, agent-free, no verdict) + `references/summary-format.md`. **Phase 4** promoted `summarize` in the emitter `_PROMOTED` (`scripts/emitters/claude.py:51`), removed the stale flat-file `summarize.md` entry — AND the lingering `review.md` flat-file entry the plan-22 cross-ref sweep flagged — from `src/manifest.json` (folder layouts are `_PROMOTED`-handled), and reconciled docs (`src/CLAUDE.md` `/summarize` entry, `CHANGELOG.md`, this list). **Key facts:** pure synthesis — agent-free (D1), renders NO verdict, read-only on every input (D4, writes ONLY `specs/[feature]/summary.md`, mutates none of the spec/plan/tasks); AC status comes from `verification.md`, NOT re-derived from the spec (D3); reuses (does not re-implement) the `_shared` scope resolver `/review` + `/verify` already use; stateless (D8) — gates on the setup chain + the spec `**Status**: Complete` flip and is idempotent (a re-run overwrites `summary.md`), making a `[WIP]` commit that `/finalize` squashes. Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops; ~150 `tests/lib/_summarize/` tests pass. **Decisions settled:** D1 (synthesis, agent-free), D3 (consume `verification.md`), D4 (read-only on inputs), D8 (stateless), D9 (`plan.md` key decisions). `/finalize` (the pipeline's final step) is the sibling follow-on, redesigned separately.

- `25-FINALIZE-COMMAND-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE 2026-06-19** on `develop-2.0-init`.
