Its defining job: the surgical feature-completion docs plus the clean feature commit nothing else in the pipeline owns. **DISPATCHES an agent AND MUTATES — the inverse of `/summarize` on both axes** (D1/D4): it dispatches the `tech-writer` agent (Normal/surgical mode) AND rewrites local git history (the squash) AND writes `docs/`; it runs NO finder ensemble, NO refutation pass, renders NO verdict. **tech-writer KEPT but RETARGETED to live `docs/`** (D1) — the agent's surgical updates point at the LIVE Plan-F locations (`docs/<package>/<concern>/index.md` Hazards, `docs/<package>/architecture.md`, `docs/architecture.md`), NEVER the dropped per-feature `docs/features/` tier; **Phase 5 reconciled the dead-tier orphans** the redesign made coherent — `src/agents/tech-writer.md`'s internal contradiction (the dead `docs/features/` claims that survived alongside the Plan-F "dropped" notes — the Documentation Folder Structure tree, the helper-managed-files distinction, the conditional authoring trigger, and the LEGACY templates, all now framed reference-only), plus `src/docs/overview.md:15` + `src/devforge/storage-rules.md:157` (the dead-`docs/features/` claims retargeted to live `docs/<package>/`), plus the `src/CLAUDE.md` `/finalize` one-liner + Command-Details body + the `src/commands/summarize/main.md` next-step pointer. **History squash** (D3/D6) — `_finalize/_squash.py`'s net-new `squash` verb (+ `resolve-squash-base` / `check-pushed`) collapses the feature's `[WIP]`/`[checkpoint]` commits into one clean `feat(<feature-name>): <title>` commit (attribution per config); gated behind explicit user confirmation (D4) and refuses to rewrite already-pushed history (the `origin/<branch>..HEAD` guard skips + warns). **Wrapper-mode dual squash, source repo traceless** (D5) — in wrapper mode BOTH repos are squashed; the source (product) repo squash uses the `[TICKET-ID] - Description` format with NO `Co-Authored-By` / NO AI traces / NO conventional-commit prefix, REGARDLESS of `COMMIT_ATTRIBUTION`. **Docs `[WIP]`-committed BEFORE the squash** (D8) so they fold into the single clean commit. **Reuses (does NOT fork) the assembled-feature scope resolver** — `src/devforge/lib/_shared/feature_scope.py` (`heading_label="Finalize Scope"`, the same resolver `/review` / `/verify` / `/summarize` use) supplies both the changed-file list for the tech-writer brief and the squash merge-base (D2); `/finalize` does NOT reuse the `_shared` refutation engine. **Stateless** (D7) — no run-state file; the squash is a single idempotent op (re-running on a finalized feature no-ops "Nothing to finalize"). Emitter `_PROMOTED += finalize`; the stale flat-file `src/commands/finalize.md` entry removed from `src/manifest.json` (folder layout is `_PROMOTED`-handled). Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops. **Phase 6 SHIPPED** (the `/implement` source-WIP attribution reconciliation — `cmd_wip_commit` now suppresses `COMMIT_ATTRIBUTION` on wrapper-mode source-repo WIP commits so the product repo is traceless even in the already-pushed-skip edge the squash can't cover; standalone unchanged). **Phase 7 e2e VALIDATED 2026-06-19** — surgically delivered into testForge20 (no reinstall) and ran `/finalize` on the consumer's first feature: install repo squashed to one `fix(…)` commit, source/product repo `the consumer product repo` squashed to one traceless `[TICKET-ID] - …` commit (D5 holds — empty body, no `Co-Authored-By`), no `docs/features/` created (D1; tech-writer justifiably skipped docs for the small fix, so the positive live-`docs/<package>/` write path was not exercised). Plan COMPLETE. (P6 was not delivered to testForge20 — older install, 870-line `_cmds_commit.py` divergence — but the squash erases WIP commits so the persisted source history is traceless regardless.) **Decisions settled:** D1 (dispatch tech-writer, retargeted to live `docs/` — the inverse of `/summarize`'s agent-free D1), D2 (reuse `_shared` scope, NOT the refutation engine), D3/D6 (squash-base strategy + `_squash.py` mechanics), D4 (MUTATES, confirmation-gated), D5 (wrapper dual squash, source traceless), D7 (stateless), D8 (docs `[WIP]`-committed before the squash), D9 (Phase 6 separable). `/finalize` is the pipeline's final step; `/summarize` is the sibling that precedes it.

- `26-REINTRODUCE-FIX-PLAN.md` — **DONE (build) 2026-06-19** on `develop-2.0-init` — Phases 0–4 built + committed (`efa16e4`..`26ae580`); install ride passed. **Phase 5 (testForge20 e2e) WAIVED by the maintainer 2026-06-19 — NOT executed** (`/fix` is build-verified — helper unit tests + install ride — but not runtime-e2e-validated; opportunistic e2e deferred). Reintroduces a `/fix` command as a **proposal-only gated pipeline-remediation** command — a thin CALLER over `/implement`'s already-shipped back-half engine at `src/commands/fix/main.md` + `references/` + a new `src/devforge/lib/_fix/` helper subpackage + a `fix_helper{,.py}` launcher. Its defining job: remediate a known, already-diagnosed, already-scoped defect the pipeline itself surfaced — WITH `/implement`'s full per-task gates — WITHOUT re-running spec → plan → breakdown. **Supersedes ONLY plan 21's D1** ("no fast-path command or tier"); every other plan-21 decision stands (the `/refactor` drop + D2–D6 untouched). The reversal is deliberate (`## Why supersede plan 21 D1`): the framework converged after plan 21 on "shared engine underneath, distinct command surface per distinct workflow moment" (`/review` vs `/verify` over `_shared/`; `/verify` reusing `verify-touched`) — a pattern plan 21 D1 did not anticipate; and the staleness fear D1 cited was a property of the deleted v1.28 COPY draft, not of a thin CALLER. Plan 21's D1 carries a Phase-0 back-pointer so the reversal is not mistaken for an oversight. **7 settled decisions:** D1 reintroduce (supersede plan 21 D1 only); **D2** the two-arm fix-or-file OFFER fires in exactly THREE in-window situations (`/review` findings / `/verify` NEEDS WORK / a user-raised + model-code-confirmed conversational defect, all post-`/implement`/pre-`/summarize`), everywhere else file-only — never auto-invoked (every forge command sets `disable-model-invocation: true`), never a cold/free-text fixer; D3 full `/implement` back-half reuse (verify-touched + self-repair → four-reviewer panel → forcing-functions gate → hard gate → `wip-commit`); **D4** findings-only — `/fix` writes NO `bugs/` file and closes none (the `Open → In Progress → Fixed` lifecycle stays MANUAL; `src/devforge/storage-rules.md` re-pointed); D5 `/refactor` stays dropped; **D6** thin caller, no copied machinery (single-source-of-truth `implement_helper` binaries can't drift); D7 scope-escalation bounce to `/specify`. The ONE `implement_helper` change is the **additive, backward-compatible task-less `wip-commit` mode** (`--task-file`/`--index`/`--number` made optional so `/fix` commits a `[WIP] fix:` without a task; `/implement` keeps passing them and stays byte-identical). `_fix/` is LEAN (no verdict, no finder ensemble, no AC re-derivation, no bug-filing): `_cli` registry, `_preflight` (setup-chain + feature resolution + source-root/wrapper-mode, `.devforge/` paths), `_findings` (`read-findings` — parse `review.md` + `verification.md` NEEDS-WORK issues), `_scope` (`resolve-scope` — finding→file set for `verify-touched`), `_window` (`in-fix-window` — the case-3 window gate). The case-3 conversational offer lives as a TIGHT always-on rule in `src/CLAUDE.md` (the only host for a non-command behavior), gated on `fix_helper in-fix-window`; the `src/CLAUDE.md` catalog carries a PURPOSE one-liner + a conditional-loop workflow annotation (`/fix` is NOT a linear step — modeled on `[/grill]`'s optional note, OQ-3). Emitter `_PROMOTED += fix`. Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops. **4 OQs resolved at build:** OQ-1 (persisted findings), OQ-2 (narrow `_fix/_scope`), OQ-3 (a `[/grill]`-style conditional-loop annotation), OQ-4 (`fix_helper in-fix-window` verb). Phase 5 (testForge20 e2e — `/implement` → `/review` → `/fix` → `/verify`) is WAIVED — NOT executed (maintainer 2026-06-19); its recipe stands as the validation procedure if/when an opportunistic e2e is run.

- `28-GENERATE-DOCS-INCREMENTAL-MODE-PLAN.md` — **CLOSED 2026-08-07 — OUTDATED (maintainer disposition; do not build).** Core cost substantially addressed by the shipped cbm_sync `detect_changes` drift path + `source_stamp` compose skip. Original record: Records the decision to add an incremental MODE to `/generate-docs` (NOT a standalone command, NOT a `--module` flag) for the mixed-team case where non-framework devs' commits drift `docs/` and a full `/generate-docs` is too expensive (every run pays a full CBM `index_repository` reindex + a whole-tree `source_stamp` scan regardless of delta — the LLM compose is already stamp-gated, so the waste is fixed preflight overhead). Design: scope to files changed since last index — read `.devforge/cbm-last-indexed-sha`, `git diff <sha>..HEAD --name-only`, regenerate only affected concern/package docs, swap the full `index_repository` preflight → `detect_changes`. A standalone command was REJECTED (a second doc producer would drift docs from the CBM/tier/glossary/cite-back model — one source of truth). The pre-pivot `src/_pending/commands/refresh-docs.md` draft is retained as reference only, superseded as a seed.

- `32-GENERATE-DOCS-GREENFIELD-LANE-PLAN.md` — **CLOSED 2026-08-07 — OUTDATED (maintainer disposition; never ratified, do not build).** The stub-passes-the-gate greenfield behavior stays the accepted workaround. Original record: Recorded 2026-06-23 on `develop-2.0-init`. Documents the greenfield asymmetry in the 4-command setup chain: `/constitute` HAS a greenfield lane (keyed on `INIT_JSON.project_state`, `constitute/main.md:430-431`) but `/generate-docs` does NOT — it is a pure code-reader that no-ops on a fresh scaffold, yet its `docs/architecture.md` output is a hard `/specify` precondition (`specify/main.md:36,211`). The chain survives only because the install stub passes the gate (the gate's populate-guard literal is constitution-specific, absent from the architecture stub). Lays out four options; **RECOMMENDS Option 1** — a greenfield short-circuit in `/generate-docs` off the SAME `project_state == "empty"` signal `/constitute` uses (skip the concern/package/project/glossary tiers, leave the substituted stubs, emit a "nothing to document yet" message, exit 0), and DECLINES Option 2 (relaxing the `/specify` gate would weaken the brownfield guarantee). No winner picked unilaterally — Phase 0 is the maintainer-ratification gate; build phases authored only after. Open: OQ-1 (`init.yaml`/`project_state` availability at step 2), OQ-2 (CBM near-empty corroborating signal), OQ-3 (detection home: helper vs orchestrator), OQ-4 (preflight overlap with the deferred plan 28 incremental mode). The sibling `/configure` docs-stub placeholder-substitution fix it references as out-of-scope SHIPPED separately (`dbf0dd5`).

- `33-FINALIZE-STAGES-SPEC-ARTIFACTS-PLAN.md` — **SUPERSEDED by 37** (per-step commits replace finalize-only) — **NOT STARTED — decisions drafted, awaiting Phase-0 sign-off.** On `develop-2.0-init`. Closes a confirmed framework gap: the spec-driven pipeline NEVER git-commits a feature's planning artifacts (`spec.md`/`plan.md`/`research.md`/`*-handoff.json`/`review.md`/`verification.md`/`*-state.json`) — verified that `/implement` stages only code + task files + `tasks/README.md` (never `git add -A`, `_cmds_commit.py:39,97`), `/finalize` PHASE 2 stages only `docs/` (`finalize/main.md:143`), the PHASE-3 squash is `git reset --soft` (`_squash.py:521`, never picks up untracked), and `/specify`/`/plan`/`/breakdown`/`/research`/`/discover`/`/review`/`/verify` contain ZERO `git add`. So the PR after `/finalize` ships code with no spec/plan/review and `git clean -fdx` would silently delete them. Fix extends `/finalize` ONLY (D1) to stage the whole `specs/<feature>/` dir (D2 — no cherry-pick; enumerates the optional `grill.md`/`grill-seed.json`/`data-model.md`/`contracts.md` + `summary.md`-already-committed) in an unconditional `[WIP]` commit before the squash (D5 — explicit `git add`, since the artifacts aren't in `gather-change-data`'s committed diff), install-repo-only in wrapper mode so the source repo stays traceless (D4 — guards plan 25 D5); runtime state (`memory.md`/`session-state.md`/`cbm-sha`) is EXCLUDED (D3 — global, not feature-scoped); inline git step vs helper verb is the one debatable call (D6/OQ3); back-fill of already-orphaned artifacts is a NON-GOAL (OQ4). Phase 1's `finalize/main.md` edit MUST route through instruction-author → instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide (ships into `.claude/`). Drafted behind the instruction-author → instruction-reviewer loop (4 findings fixed). Discovered while committing feature-002 devForge artifacts in a consumer install (the consumer install) that the pipeline had left untracked.

- `34-VERIFY-HYGIENE-FALSE-POSITIVE-PLAN.md` — **BUILD DONE 2026-06-23** on `develop-2.0-init` (code committed `0be91f0`); only **testForge20 e2e (reinstall) remains, user-driven**. Fixes a `/verify` false-positive NEEDS WORK on clean features — the verdict was driven by hygiene NOISE, not real defects. Four root causes: RC1 — `check_hygiene` (`_verify/_hygiene.py`) ran its leftover-artifact regexes over EVERY file in the assembled diff (no file-type gate), so `specs/*.md`/`design/*.html`/JSDoc prose flagged (e.g. `// print()` in HTML, `### Expects:` spec headers); RC2 — `scope_creep` = whole diff MINUS the src-only `breakdown-handoff.json` baseline, so pipeline-written `specs/<feature>/*.md` + `design/*.html` + review-driven test files flag as creep forever; RC3 — both fed `_verdict.py` as a HARD BLOCKER (`hygiene_flags` → NEEDS WORK); RC4 — a negative-grep AC (`! grep 'style=[{][{]'`) self-matched the JSDoc documenting the forbidden pattern, and `ac-verifier.md` had no guidance to read WHAT a grep matched. Four fixes (D1 — file-gate is a DENYLIST of prose extensions + forge-artifact dirs, NOT a code-extension allowlist, per the polyglot stance of `dd1c29c`; D2 — hygiene is ADVISORY, removed from the `_verdict.py` blocker set, user-confirmed; D3 — no test-file name special-casing): `_hygiene.py` `_is_code_file` gate on both scans + `files_skipped`, `_verdict.py` hygiene→advisory, `_report.py` advisory labels, `_cli.py` fallback dicts, `ac-verifier.md` rule 13 (read negative-grep matches; doc-only → PASS), `verify/main.md` + `references/report-format.md` reconciled. 496 `_verify` tests pass. Built behind python-engineer → python-reviewer + instruction-author → instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops by a parallel session; surgically delivered to the consumer install's install (no reinstall). Renumbered from a draft-time `33` collision with the finalize plan above.

- `35-GRILL-REFUTER-ROUTING-FIX-PLAN.md` — **✅ CLOSED 2026-06-24** (committed `06b78be` on `develop-2.0-init`); testForge20 e2e **DEFERRED by maintainer** (not blocking — empirically validated this session). Fixes a `/grill` PHASE 4 refuter-routing bug: the spec told the orchestrator to pass ONLY the finding's author to `route-refutation --finders`, but the helper requires every eligible refuter to be present in `--finders` — so with `devils-advocate` as the sole present finder, all findings self-refuted to their own author (no cross-examination). Spec-side fix only (`src/commands/grill/main.md` PHASE 4): a present-refuter determination step now composes `--finders "devils-advocate,<present-refuters>"` so the architect-excluded refuter priority `[code-reviewer, qa-reviewer, security-reviewer]` actually routes; the `_shared` refutation helper is UNCHANGED. Empirically validated this session.

- `36-GRILL-UNIVERSAL-REENTRY-PLAN.md` — **✅ CLOSED 2026-06-24** (all build phases shipped, committed `06b78be`); Phase 6 testForge20 e2e DEFERRED by maintainer (not blocking). Adds `/plan` as the **4th consumer** of `/grill`'s `ReEntrySeed`: the REVISE-PLAN disposition now emits a backward `specs/[feature]/grill-seed.json` with `target_stage="plan"`, consumed by a new `/plan` **PHASE 0a.7** re-entry block (mirrors `/specify`'s Phase 0.5 re-entry consumer) so a re-`/plan` addresses the grill's confirmed findings instead of re-deriving the plan. `_grill/seed_schema.py` `SEED_TARGET_STAGES` grew `3 → 4` (`spec`/`discovery`/`research` + `plan`); a `render_report` `_UPSTREAM_STAGES` (the 3 upstream stages) guard keeps RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM rendering distinct from REVISE-PLAN (Design X — RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM targets the 3 upstream stages, REVISE-PLAN targets `plan`; distinct dispositions, never conflated). OQ-1 resolved (a): `carried_findings` stays `List[str]`. Built behind python-engineer → python-reviewer + instruction-author → instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops.

- `39-GRILL-SEED-VERDICT-GATED-PLAN.md` — **✅ CLOSED 2026-06-24** (committed `a908e91` on `develop-2.0-init`); Phase 4 testForge20 e2e DEFERRED by maintainer (not blocking). Closes the "orphan seed on override" case left open by plan 36's D3: `/grill` wrote `grill-seed.json` in PHASE 6 from the RECOMMENDATION, before the PHASE-7 human gate where the user can override — an overridden seed (recommend Revise/Re-enter, user picks Proceed) became an orphan a later `/plan`/`/specify` run silently obeyed. Fix (Option A): `write-seed` + its `commit-artifacts` moved from PHASE 6 into PHASE 7's matching re-entry arm, gated on the user's verdict — the seed is written ONLY when the user's PHASE-7 pick MATCHES the recommended seed-disposition (`Revise plan` on a REVISE-PLAN rec → `target_stage="plan"`; `Re-enter upstream` on a RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM rec → an upstream stage); a cross-pick or `Proceed`/`Kill` writes no seed. Reuses `write-seed`/`commit-artifacts` UNCHANGED (no helper-logic change; only the `_cli.py` self-doc label reworded `(Phase 5)` → `(human-gate re-entry arm)`); `render-report --re-entry-target` + `SEED_TARGET_STAGES` untouched. OQ-1/OQ-2 resolved = matching-pick. Built behind instruction-author → instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide + python-engineer loops.

- `37-PER-STEP-ARTIFACT-COMMIT-PLAN.md` — **✅ DELIVERED 2026-08-18 — closed by maintainer statement, with no per-phase verification performed under that closure. Every status claim in the body below predates that and is superseded by the STATUS AMENDMENT that closes this entry — read it first.** **Phases 0–1 + 2–5, 7–9 SHIPPED 2026-06-23** on `develop-2.0-init` (the shared `artifact_helper commit-artifacts` verb + 7 of the 8 command edits committed); **Phase 6 (`/grill`) done-in-tree but uncommitted/entangled with concurrent grill work**; **Phases 10/11/13 in progress; Phase 12 (e2e) user-driven.** Supersedes plan 33: closes the same gap (the spec-driven pipeline never git-commits a feature's planning artifacts — `spec.md`/`plan.md`/`research.md`/`*-handoff.json`/`review.md`/`verification.md`/`*-state.json` accumulate untracked, so the PR after `/finalize` ships code with no spec/plan/review and `git clean -fdx` would silently delete them) but inverts plan 33's mechanism — instead of a single finalize-only inline-git stage, EVERY pipeline command WIP-commits its OWN artifacts (md + handoff JSON + per-feature `*-state.json`) the moment it writes them, install-repo-only + fail-soft, folding into `/finalize`'s squash so the final PR is byte-unchanged. **Mechanism = a single SHARED, TESTED helper verb** `commit-artifacts` (`--paths <json-array>` of file-or-dir paths + `--label <str>` + `--root <path>`; resolves the install root via the same `_implement/_workspace.py` resolver `wip-commit` uses; stages ONLY explicit named paths `git add -- <path>`, NEVER `git add -A`; commits `[WIP] <label>`; FAIL-SOFT — a git error warns + returns non-fatal, never blocks the caller; "nothing to commit"/path-absent is a benign no-op). **Decisions:** D1 — a shared tested verb, NOT inline git per spec (reverses plan 33 D6: ~8 new call sites + the safety-net mandate DRY); D2 — wrapper split: the verb targets `workspace.install_root` ALWAYS, never the source/product repo (the source repo stays code-only + traceless `[TICKET-ID]`, plan 25 D5); D3 — `/research` + `/discover` pre-feature reports + handoffs INCLUDED (committed per-step to the install repo, fold into the squash when on-branch after the base, harmless separate upstream WIP otherwise); D4 — the `/finalize` `git add specs/<feature>/` safety-net is RETAINED (carried from plan 33 D2/D5) as a belt-and-suspenders catch for any artifact a fail-soft per-step commit skipped — that redundancy is DELIBERATE, not an oversight. **OQs:** OQ1 RESOLVED to a single new `artifact_helper{,.py}` launcher under `src/devforge/lib/` (mirrors the `audit_helper`/`review_helper`/`summarize_helper` shim pattern, rides the full-install `cp -R`); OQ2 — research/discover included (= D3); OQ3 — per-command WIP labels (`[WIP] spec: NNN-slug`, `[WIP] plan: NNN-slug`, …) for readable pre-squash history (the squash erases them, so the choice is invisible in the final PR); OQ4 — keep the safety-net (= D4). **14 phases:** 0 ratify (user sign-off gate), 1 the shared verb (`_artifact/` + `artifact_helper` launcher, REAL git-fixture test standalone + wrapper arms), 2–9 the eight command edits (2 `/research`, 3 `/discover`, 4 `/specify`, 5 `/plan`, 6 `/grill`, 7 `/breakdown`, 8 `/review`, 9 `/verify` — each adds ONE per-step commit call to its `main.md` after the artifact is written + approved), 10 the `/finalize` safety-net (D4), 11 supersede plan 33 (its own file + this list), 12 testForge20 + wrapper e2e (user-driven), 13 docs reconcile. Each `main.md` edit routes through instruction-author → instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide (ships into `.claude/`); the verb through python-engineer → python-reviewer. **Phase 7 (`/breakdown`) is NOT redundant in wrapper mode** — there `/implement` stages only source `touched_files` in the source repo and leaves the task files + `tasks/README.md` untracked in the install root, so Phase 7's commit is the FIRST to track them there. **STATUS AMENDMENT, 2026-08-18 — this supersedes every status claim in this entry, its opening marker included: the plan is ✅ DELIVERED, closed by maintainer statement (*"plan 37 delivered"*).** That statement is the entire closure authority. **No per-phase verification was performed under it and no commit SHA is recorded here for Phase 6 or for Phases 10/11/13 — do not read this amendment as a report that any phase was inspected.** One supporting fact WAS checked on the day: a live `git status` reported `src/commands/grill/` and `src/devforge/lib/_grill/` clean (verified 2026-08-18), so **the working tree no longer carries the uncommitted Phase-6 grill work described above** — an observation about the tree, NOT an inspection of what the grill commits contain. **The pre-closure phase notes above (Phase 6 done-in-tree but uncommitted, Phases 10/11/13 in progress, Phase 12 e2e user-driven) are retained deliberately as history**; they describe the state before this closure and are not live claims. Repo-root `CLAUDE.md`'s plan-37 index line was flipped to match on the same day.

- `38-BREAKDOWN-AGENT-ROSTER-VALIDATION-PLAN.md` — **Phases 1–5 SHIPPED 2026-06-24** on `develop-2.0-init`; only **Phase 6 (testForge20 / consumer e2e) remains, user-driven.** Fixes a confirmed `/breakdown` bug surfaced in a consumer install (a consumer desktop Electron app with no backend stack): `/breakdown` assigned each task an agent from the static Agent Assignment table with NO check that the agent was actually generated for the project, and the split-or-escalate rule was prose-only — so `backend-engineer` got assigned to an Electron main-process file and `/implement` halted far downstream on the missing agent (`handoff_schema.py` documented the gap verbatim: "the roster is NOT validated here"). Two halves (user-chosen A+B scope). **A — mechanical gate:** new `breakdown_helper verify-agent-roster <tasks-dir>` forcing-function (sibling to `verify-contract-chain` / `verify-ac-coverage`; shared `_validate_agent_roster` fn) globs the installed `.claude/agents/*.md` roster and HARD-halts at `/breakdown` **PHASE 3.5** on any task whose `**Agent**:` isn't installed (lists offenders + `Available agents:`, mirroring `/implement`'s halt); the same check folds into `finalize-handoff` as a chokepoint so a poisoned `breakdown-handoff.json` cannot be written. **Fail-closed** on absent/empty roster (a `.md`-named *directory* is excluded via `is_file()`); the default `--agents-dir .claude/agents` is correct in BOTH standalone and wrapper mode because the roster lives in the install/wrapper root (where the helper is invoked from), NOT the Source Root — so the spec passes NO `--agents-dir`. PHASE 5's best-effort-continue clause was split by channel so a roster violation reaching `finalize-handoff` (offender block on **stdout**, `no agent roster found` on **stderr**) is a HARD halt back to Phase 3.5, never swallowed; the Phase 3.5 preamble's "record in Risk Assessment" bypass was carved out (roster gate = HARD, no bypass). **B — table row:** the Agent Assignment table gained a row for non-server host/runtime-entrypoint code (Electron main, desktop-app `main`, CLI entrypoint, Tauri core) → the owning package's stack implementer per `PACKAGE_STACKS`, explicitly NOT `backend-engineer` by default (the first table row whose right cell is a `PACKAGE_STACKS`-lookup rule rather than a literal agent name). The `/implement` missing-agent guard is UNCHANGED — this moves the failure left, `/implement` → `/breakdown`. NO agent-name enum added (`TaskRow.agent` stays non-empty-only — the roster is runtime/per-project; the schema docstring now points at the helper-layer check). Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops; 974 breakdown/implement tests pass. Extends plan `14` (which left the absent-agent check as deferred prose; this plan adds the mechanical gate).

- `40-DESIGN-FIDELITY-FORCING-FUNCTION-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–8 SHIPPED 2026-06-24** on `develop-2.0-init` (built + per-phase review-clean); only **Phase 9 (testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven (HARD GATE).** Makes visual drift from a `design/reference.html` structurally gate-blocking for any frontend task, given the framework cannot reliably "see pixels." Root-caused from a consumer install (a consumer Electron shell drifted from `design/reference.html`): the drift was CONTRACT-licensed (the spec said "semantic equivalence, not pixel-exact"; fidelity was deferred to a `/review` → design-auditor pass that **was dispatched by no command** — `design-auditor` was an orphan agent; hardcoded hex + `var(--token,<literal>)` fallbacks passed `/implement` unchecked; scope was conflated with defects). **Mechanism = TWO ORTHOGONAL gates + a required pre-code disposition manifest** (the orthogonality is load-bearing — a runtime compare reads resolved values with no provenance so it can't catch token-bypass; a static grep can't catch right-token-wrong-spacing; neither is redundant). **The contract** (Phase 1) is a persisted universal constitution section **§3.8 Design Fidelity** (`_UNIVERSAL_SECTIONS` + `verify-universal-defaults`-tracked, `*Backed by* constitute_helper verify-design-tokens`; narrow reference-present⇒1:1 carve-out, markup rebuilt freely) + the `design-auditor.md` Rule 5 carve-out. **The disposition manifest** (Phase 2/3) — `src/devforge/lib/_design/` helper (`design_helper`: `resolve-reference` / `init-manifest` / `validate-manifest` / `extract-spacing-scale`; `data-ref`-keyed per OQ-5) produces `specs/[feature]/design-manifest.json` at `/breakdown` **PHASE 2.5** (conditional on a `design/reference.html`); an UNCLASSIFIED element or an unresolvable value HALTS intake and escalates BEFORE any task is written (the 4-way taxonomy MATCH / DEFER-EMPTY / STATIC-PLACEHOLDER / DEVIATE). **The static provenance gate** (Phase 4/5) — a new `_constitute/_forcing_functions/_design_tokens/` detector + `constitute_helper verify-design-tokens` (registered in `_schema.py::FORCING_FUNCTION_RULES` + `_setters.py::RULE_TO_VERB` + `_cli.py`) runs at `/implement`'s per-task forcing-functions gate + the opt-in `pre-commit-forcing-functions.sh` (no hardcoded color/border/spacing literals, no `var(--x,<literal>)` fallbacks, undefined-token-fails-loud, token-binding on MATCH elements, `:hover`+`:focus-visible`); checks 1–4 are manifest-independent, Check 5 GLOBS `specs/*/design-manifest.json` at runtime (OQ-2 "option d" — global config is `{enabled, token_source_css}` ONLY, the per-feature manifest is never a config value). **The runtime conformance gate** (Phase 6) — `design-auditor` re-equipped (`tools:` += `evaluate_script`+`list_pages`; `## Approach` rewritten to the OQ-1 HYBRID mechanism = computed-style diff for tokenizable axes + screenshot-diff scoped to MATCH regions; Rule 1's prose fallback REPLACED by a structured `CHROME_MCP_AVAILABLE` probe; OQ-3 MCP-absent = provenance-floor-only + DECLARE-not-covered) + WIRED into `/review` as a NEW conditional PHASE-2.5 dispatch (this is the first time the runtime check is invoked — resolves the 3 `design-auditor` orphan sites). **Activation** (Phase 5) — `/constitute` Section 3.5 now offers the `design_token_provenance` rule for UI projects (`set-forcing-functions --rule design_token_provenance --enabled true --token-source-css design/styles.css`; optional `--allowlist-paths`). **Producer alignment** (Phase 7) — `frontend/mobile-engineer` briefs gained a `### Design Fidelity` subsection (bind tokens incl. typography, declare hover/focus-visible, carry `data-ref` anchors, never silently fill a gap — escalate). **D8 orphan reconcile:** the `breakdown/main.md` Agent-Assignment row mis-assigning the read-only `design-auditor` as a per-task IMPLEMENTER was DE-ASSIGNED standalone (routed to the owning stack engineer); the charter + Boundaries orphans are made TRUE by the Phase-6 wire-in. Also fixed a pre-existing `ac-verifier.md` bug (referenced `list_pages` without allowlisting it). Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops; `_design`/`_design_tokens`/`_constitute`/`_review` suites green + full suite clean. 6 OQs ratified 2026-06-24 (hybrid mechanism / extend design-auditor / floor-and-declare / manifest at `/breakdown` / `data-ref` required / spacing relaxes when CSS absent).

- `41-AGENT-EXECUTOR-REACHABILITY-PLAN.md` — **Work Items 1–3 SHIPPED 2026-06-24** (working tree) on `develop-2.0-init`; only **Phase 5 (testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven.** Closes the "orphaned declaration" class found in a 2026-06-24 declarations→executors survey: a spec/table/agent names a responsibility but nothing runs it. Three orphan types — type-1 ORPHANED AGENT (named responsible, no command dispatches), type-2 ORPHANED STEP ("verified at stage Y" but Y doesn't), type-3 ORPHANED FINDING (runs in an ensemble but gates nothing). **THREE work items** (design-auditor was the survey's canonical orphan but is OWNED BY plan 40 — explicitly OUT here; the consumer-runtime roster check is plan 38's `verify-agent-roster` — complementary, not folded). **WI-1 (type-1 fix):** wired `qa-engineer` — a chartered test-writer that was an orphan (named only in the `/breakdown` consult-relay list, no Agent-Assignment row, so no task ever routed to it) — a real executor: a "Dedicated test-authoring / coverage-gap task → qa-engineer" row + rule in `src/commands/breakdown/main.md` (ratified D2 model (a): inline tests stay each engineer's default; a dedicated qa-engineer task is created only on an architect/decomposition coverage-gap or test-heavy-AC flag — no double-coverage). **WI-2 (type-3 fix):** made CONFIRMED Medium-severity non-constitution `/review` findings gate the `/verify` verdict — they were rendered to the human but never appended to `blockers[]`, so a finder producing only Medium output ran and gated nothing. `src/devforge/lib/_verify/_verdict.py` gained `_is_medium`/`_MEDIUM`/`medium_confirmed` + an `if medium_confirmed:` `medium_finding` blocker → NEEDS WORK (ratified D3: any-confirmed-Medium; **never REJECTED**; **contested Medium does NOT gate** — confirmed-only per OQ-3; Info stays advisory). **Hygiene stays advisory (D4, plan 34 untouched)** — the mandatory plan-34 false-positive regression test (clean feature + only hygiene flags → APPROVED) passes; `verify/references/report-format.md` + `verify/main.md` PHASE 9 reconciled to name the Medium tier; 511 `_verify` tests pass. **WI-3 (the permanent gate — stops new orphans being born):** a maintainer-side agent-executor-reachability checker — `scripts/lib/agent_reachability.py` (`find_orphans(repo_root) -> {orphan_agents, unknown_assignments, relay_only}`) + `scripts/verify-agent-reachability.py` CLI + `tests/lib/test_agent_reachability.py` (36 tests). Parses the `src/agents/*.md` roster and every real dispatch path — literal `subagent_type:` in `src/commands/**`, the `/breakdown` Agent-Assignment table (the data-driven `/implement` engineer-agent path), and helper ensemble/refuter lists via **AST** (`_AUDIT_AGENTS` flat list in `_audit/_preflight.py`, `_FOCUS_BLOCKS`/`_REVIEWER_VOCAB` **dict KEYS**, `_REFUTER_PRIORITY`/`_GRILL_REFUTER_PRIORITY` lists) — and FAILS on a roster agent with zero executor, an unknown assignment-named agent, or a hard-fail relay-only agent (functional `RELAY_ONLY_ALLOWLIST`, empty now). Live-src GREEN (all 18 roster agents reachable). The repo has no CI/Makefile, so the pytest test against live `src/` IS the permanent gate. **Scope honesty (D5/D8): the gate mechanizes type-1 + unknown-agent ONLY — type-2 forward-prose and type-3 finding-inertness are NOT mechanized** (they stay human review conventions; a passing gate is not full orphan coverage). **Ratified decisions:** D1 wire-not-retire qa-engineer; D2 model (a); D3 any-confirmed-Medium → NEEDS WORK; D4 hygiene advisory; D5/D8 honest non-coverage; D6 the 4-rule check-set; D7 plan-38/plan-41 maintainer-vs-consumer split; OQ-1 standalone `scripts/`; OQ-4 hard-fail relay-only. Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops (a HIGH python-reviewer catch fixed mid-build: the AST extractor missed `ast.AnnAssign`, silently not-reading the annotated `_AUDIT_AGENTS` — a latent false-negative). Supersedes nothing; extends the survey discipline into a standing gate.

- `42-DESIGN-MANIFEST-TRIGGER-FORCING-FUNCTION-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE 2026-06-25** — Phases 1–5 shipped + committed (`f0a9e95`) on `develop-2.0-init`; **Phase 6 (testForge20/consumer e2e) DEFERRED by maintainer** (not blocking — build-verified via 296 breakdown tests + dual review loops). PREVENTIVE hardening of plan 40's design-fidelity apparatus: plan 40 made visual drift gate-blocking through a manifest (`specs/[feature]/design-manifest.json`, produced at `/breakdown` PHASE 2.5) that TWO downstream gates (`/implement`'s static `verify-design-tokens` Check 5 + `/review`'s `design-auditor` dispatch) depend on — but the manifest's load-bearing TRIGGER was skippable PROSE with a silent-failure cascade (a skipped PHASE 2.5 writes tasks + a green `breakdown-handoff.json` with no manifest; Check 5 globs `specs/*/design-manifest.json` project-wide so the current feature either no-ops OR checks ANOTHER feature's MATCH refs; the `design-auditor` dispatch never fires). Same shape as the gap plan 38 closed for the agent roster. **WI-1 (mechanical backstop):** new `breakdown_helper verify-manifest-present <tasks-dir>` forcing-function (sibling to `verify-contract-chain`/`verify-ac-coverage`/`verify-agent-roster`; shared `_validate_manifest_present` predicate composing `_design`'s `validate_manifest` via a single import) asserts `design/reference.html` present ⇒ `design-manifest.json` present-and-valid, HARD-halt no-bypass at `/breakdown` **PHASE 3.5** (the 4th integrity gate) AND folded into `finalize-handoff` as a chokepoint (a poisoned handoff cannot be written). **WI-2 (defense-in-depth tripwires):** loud-WARN non-blocking tripwires at `/implement`'s forcing-functions gate site + `/review` PHASE 2.5 for the legacy/tampered `reference.html`-present-but-manifest-absent case (the PHASE 3.5 gate guards fresh decompositions; the tripwires surface the void guarantee for already-decomposed features). **Decisions:** D1 mechanical PHASE 3.5 gate mirroring plan 38; D2 reuse `validate-manifest` (no fork); D3 verb + finalize chokepoint, single shared fn, two intra-file call sites; **D4 — PHASE 2.5 detect made mechanical via bash `test -f design/reference.html` (NOT a shared helper call): `--scope-only` can't run at PHASE 2.5 because no tasks-dir exists yet at intake; the same file-existence predicate + the PHASE 3.5 backstop guarantee agreement, and the reference-path literal is already duplicated in PHASE 2.5's `resolve-reference` call regardless** (maintainer-ratified Option B 2026-06-25); D6 verb homed in `breakdown_helper.py` (gate-family cohesion); D7 tripwire severity = loud WARN non-blocking. **Non-goals:** consumer manifest backfill; NON-FILE design sources (Figma/URL/screenshot — needs a structured `design_source` declaration, tracked in plan 43); any change to plan 40's apparatus. Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer (Phase 1–2 helper, 296 breakdown tests) + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide (Phase 3–4 `/breakdown`+`/implement`+`/review` main.md) loops. Extends plan 40 (the apparatus) + plan 38 (the gate-shape precedent).

- `45-SEAM-TRUST-PROPAGATION-PLAN.md` — **Steps 1–3 SHIPPED 2026-06-27** on `develop-2.0-init` (`8390b62` Step 2, `97e0417` Step 3, `cfe48dd` plan). Closes two handoff trust-mismatch seams found in a design critique of the framework's own gate architecture (where one stage validates a value for SHAPE only and a downstream stage SCOPES ITS OWN WORK by trusting that value as correctness). **Seam E** (caveat propagation): `PlanSeeds.correctness_vetted: bool = False` added to the research→plan handoff carrier (`_research/handoff_schema.py`) + `plan_helper._render_research_plan_seeds` renders an adjacent "shape-checked (token-overlap), NOT correctness-vetted" caveat, so `/plan` stops silently treating a token-overlap-validated `recommended_approach` as authoritative. Deterministic, no model call; back-compat proven as the honest pair (old JSON→default + current-producer-stable, NO impossible byte-identity claim). **Seam A** (design-fidelity spacing circularity): `verify-design-tokens` Check 5's spacing sub-check (`_design_tokens/_scanner.py` + `_cmd.py`) re-anchored to scope on the `data-ref`s present in `design/reference.html` (stdlib `html.parser`, which the LLM cannot mistag for this purpose) minus DEVIATE-with-recorded-reason exemptions, instead of the LLM-authored MATCH `match_refs` — so a mistagged-DEVIATE element can no longer silently escape the ONLY hardcoded-spacing check; reference absent/unreadable/anchorless → byte-identical `match_refs` fallback; Checks 1–4 + Check 5 color sub-check unchanged. **Step 1** was a live trace (no code) confirming the circularity is partial-by-axis (spacing-only; color is re-covered by the manifest-independent Check 1). **D8:** Step 2 deliberately did NOT flag the manifest `disposition` on the assumption Step 3 re-anchors that path — Step 3 shipped, so the exclusion is retroactively valid (a future session must not read "disposition unflagged" as "disposition safe"). Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer (3 reviewer findings fixed incl. a silent fail-open regression on empty `reference.html`) loops; every load-bearing claim shipped with its raw proving command (the engagement's governing discipline, after one fabricated "byte-identical" assertion was caught early). **Off-critical-path Golden Regression Catalog SHIPPED** as repo-root `REGRESSION-ANCHORS.md` (pins three fixed meta-bugs — orphaned design-auditor, skippable design-manifest trigger, import-handoff dedup — to their guarding tests; records the tier-1.5 "probe misroute" as a rejected phantom).

- `48-REVIEW-MANDATORY-GATE-PLAN.md` — **SHELVED 2026-06-30 — keep the warning, NOT built.** On `develop-2.0-init`. Records (but does NOT implement) a hard gate making `/review` mechanically mandatory before `/verify` renders a verdict — anchored to the plan-38/42 forcing-function shape (mechanical verb → exit non-zero → `main.md` halts on stderr). The gap is real but structural-and-theoretical: `/review` is documented mandatory (unbracketed in the `src/CLAUDE.md` Workflow arrow-chain) but only WARNED on (`src/commands/verify/main.md:153` "proceed weakened"), and `disable-model-invocation: true` means the model can't auto-run it — yet it was NOT a reproduced incident (unlike 38/42's the consumer install root-cause). **Maintainer shelved it:** the loud warning already nudges a human operator, OQ-1 means even a full build guarantees review *presence* not *freshness* (a `/fix` → re-`/verify` loop satisfies the gate with a stale `review.md`), and the full verb is disproportionate to an un-hit gap. **Revival trigger:** a `/review` skip OBSERVED in practice — especially a UI feature reaching APPROVED `/verify` while `design-auditor` (runs ONLY at `/review` PHASE 2.5) never fired. On revival the proportionate first cut is D1 **alt-(a)** (flip the `:153` warning into a halt — instruction-loop + docs only, no new verb), not the full preflight verb. Plan written + instruction-reviewed (7 findings applied) so the analysis + collateral-scope map (Rule 6 `verify/main.md:370`, `summarize/main.md:139`, `report-format.md:55,69`, helper `_report.py:209,245`) is preserved against re-derivation.

- `49-DEVFORGE-RUNTIME-STATE-DISPOSITION-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–5 SHIPPED 2026-07-04** on `develop-2.0-init` (working tree); only **Phase 6 (consumer/testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven.** Makes a consumer install's git tree CLEAN automatically after a full pipeline cycle — it was left dirty every cycle with `.devforge/` runtime-state churn the maintainer hand-committed. Root cause: `.devforge/` mixed THREE storage classes under one dir with no per-file git disposition, AND the one ignore rule for session state pointed at a DEAD path (`.claude/session-state.md`, the pre-plan-22 location — moved to `.devforge/session-state.md` in plan 22 finding F) that the `union_lines` merge had been propagating from the forge repo's OWN root `.gitignore` (`update.sh` `TEMPLATE_DIR/.gitignore`) into every consumer. Fix = a 3-class disposition (VERSIONED / EPHEMERAL / FEATURE-SCOPED, D1) for every `.devforge/` file. **EPHEMERAL** (`session-state.md`, `specify-state.json`, `research-state.json`, `research-report.json`, `discover-scope.json`, `discover-report.json`, `.preflight-stamp`, `cbm-last-indexed-sha`, `.generate-docs-trace.log`, `cbm-usage.log`, `*.lock`) → a NEW dedicated `src/files/devforge.gitignore` template (D5) merged by a `manifest.json` `mergeFiles[".gitignore"].templateSource` + an `update.sh` `merge_src()` resolver (re-points the union source off the forge-root `.gitignore`; `.mcp.json` `union_keys` fallback preserved) + an `install.sh` inline union block (fresh-install coverage, OQ-5) + a fail-soft / tracked-only / install-repo-only `git rm --cached` migration shared via `scripts/devforge-state-migrate.sh` (sourced by BOTH `install.sh` + `update.sh`) that also DELETES the dead `.claude/session-state.md` line (`union_lines` only ADDS). **VERSIONED** `memory.md` + `spec-stamps.jsonl` deltas → folded into the `/finalize` PHASE-2 safety-net `commit-artifacts --paths` so they ride the squash (D3 — a NARROW, deliberate file-level revisit of plan 33/37's blanket runtime-state exclusion, back-pointer inline in `finalize/main.md`; OQ-4 RESOLVED = feature-squash). **FEATURE-SCOPED** (`specs/<feature>/*`, dated `research/`+`discover/`) already plan-37-committed. `storage-rules.md` documents the 3-class table + the scratch-vs-record trap (`.devforge/research-report.json` ≠ `research/<slug>/handoff.json`, D7). Reuse-only, no new helper (D4); install-repo-only / wrapper-safe by construction (D8, plan 25 D5). 50 scratch tests green (Phase 1 union fresh/existing/stale + Phase 2 migration tracked→untracked / VERSIONED-preserved / fresh-no-op / non-git-skip + shared-fn as-sourced). Plan written + inline-reviewed (3 findings fixed in a loop); the `finalize/main.md` edit routed through `instruction-reviewer`. Discovered when a `/finalize` in the consumer install left 9 `.devforge/` runtime files uncommitted. **Version NOT bumped** — release is deferred to the maintainer.

- `53-DESIGN-ANCHOR-FIRST-CLASS-PLAN.md` — **Phases 1–8 SHIPPED 2026-07** on `develop-2.0-init` (working tree); only **Phase 9 (the consumer install/testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven HARD GATE.** **SUPERSEDES plan 52** (which is now marked SUPERSEDED, retained for rationale only — do not build it). Makes design intent a **first-class captured pipeline input** — a `design_anchor` — instead of something every stage re-guesses (font drift was the symptom). Two independent axes (D2/D17): the DECLARED anchor `kind` (source of intent — `html` implemented; `figma`/others = open NOT-COVERED slots) × the DETECTED build platform (from stack metadata, never declared — `web` implemented). **Anchor vs binding split (D4):** the anchor `{kind, file, selectors}` = immutable INTENT, captured at `/research`|`/discover` via a new supplementary rubric dimension (`set-design-anchor`/`set-scope-design-anchor`, reusing `parse_design_source`), carried ONE hop via the intake handoff `SpecSeeds.design_anchor`, persisted by `/specify` to `specs/[feature]/design-anchor.json` (+ a `source_hash` staleness baseline, D13; the structured upgrade of plan 43's `**Design source**:` frontmatter, D14) — then **park-once, read-in-place** (NOT re-carried through handoffs, D5); the binding `{route, pairs:[{anchor_selector, built_testid}]}` = built-side wiring authored at `/breakdown` PHASE 2.5 (the `design-manifest.json` disposition-manifest SUCCESSOR — same filename, new shape; container-floor pair + opt-in pairs, D7). **Auditor engine (D8/D9):** the `design-auditor` runs a two-tier deterministic check at `/review` — an always-on anchor-FREE sanity floor (overflow / clip / font-not-loaded — D10) + an anchor-gated value+geometry fidelity diff (built computed vs the ACTUAL design source read via the intent reader — the **D12 REVERSAL** of plan 52 D11). OQ-A resolved: all DECISION logic is Python (`_design/_bag`+`_floor`+`_fidelity`+`_comparator`+`_cmds_compare` + the `compare` verb), the JS assets (`_design/js/{built,intent}_reader.js`) are thin measurement collectors (verified at Phase 9 e2e, not unit-tested — no JS infra added). **Visual (D11):** computed + geometry-NUMBER diff GATES; a VLM screenshot pass is ADVISORY (never gates); pixel-diff-as-gate rejected. **7 honesty invariants** (non-vacuousness structural; #7 platform-mismatch is the outermost preflight, from stack metadata). `/review` embeds the auditor's fidelity report as a distinct `## Design Fidelity` section via `render-report --design-section` (OUTSIDE the refutation partition — deterministic probe output is not cross-examined); `/plan` reads the anchor (light passive). **Retired:** the `data-ref`/`_DataRefCollector`/`resolve_reference`/`resolve-reference`/`init-manifest` + `ElementRecord`/disposition (MATCH/DEFER-EMPTY/STATIC-PLACEHOLDER/DEVIATE) model, `_design_tokens` Check 5 (kept Checks 1–4, manifest-INDEPENDENT), and the `/implement` design-manifest tripwire; `validate-manifest`→`validate-binding`; `verify-manifest-present` retargeted to the binding; `_reference.py`→`_css_parse.py`. `_design/` added to the `install.sh`+`update.sh` surgical `--only` always-copy set (cross-cutting runtime dep of `/breakdown`+`/review`+`/implement`). Constitution §3.8 reworded. **Figma is the fast-follow SIBLING plan** (open `kind`, its intent reader normalizes live MCP node data against the same comparator — name no other platform). Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer+claude-code-guide loops; full suite green throughout.

- `56-DEVFORGE-CODE-GITIGNORE-PLAN.md` — **Phases 1–3 SHIPPED (working tree) + Phase 4 = this docs pass, 2026-07-10** on `develop-2.0-init`; only **Phase 5 (consumer/testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven.** A SCOPE-EXTENSION of plan 49 — it adds a fourth, CODE, class to plan 49's `.devforge/` runtime-state disposition. **Problem:** the forge ships its runtime helper code into every consumer under git-TRACKED `.devforge/lib`/`bin`/`templates`, and the consumer's codebase-memory-mcp (CBM) graph indexes it — measured at **37% of a real consumer's graph** (300 files / 3898 of 10628 nodes), burying the 78 `src/` files of actual product code. CBM 0.6.1 has no path-exclude knob and does NOT honor `.ignore` (proven on throwaway repos), but DOES honor `.gitignore` even for git-tracked files — so the only durable fix is `.gitignore`, and (mirroring plan 49) gitignore ALONE leaves a fragile tracked-and-gitignored half-state, so the fix ALSO untracks. **Mechanism (the node_modules model):** 3 lines appended to `src/files/devforge.gitignore` (`.devforge/lib/`, `.devforge/bin/`, `.devforge/templates/`) + a recursive-dir untrack loop added to the shared `scripts/devforge-state-migrate.sh` (`forge_migrate_devforge_state`, which both installers already source) — fail-soft / tracked-only / install-repo-only, never touching the VERSIONED root files. **NO `manifest.json` / `install.sh` / `update.sh` change was needed** (the `union_lines` merge auto-carries the new lines — Phase 3 confirmed mechanically). The three dirs are regenerated by `install.sh` / `update.sh` `cp -R` on every run, so they are never git-tracked; a fresh clone that skips a forge install/update lacks the helpers until one runs (OQ-1 → assumed install step, like `npm install`; no missing-helpers guard built **at the time — plan 72 later closed this on the `update.sh` path: a target whose `.devforge/lib` holds no executable `*_helper` now auto-enters repair mode. A clone that runs neither installer is still on its own**). **`.devforge/template/` (SINGULAR) is DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED** (Phase 0.5 decision) — it stays fully tracked because it is `update.sh`'s agent three-way-merge baseline + NEW/REMOVED agent enumeration source; untracking it would risk blind agent overwrite + skipped pruning. (Note `templates/` plural IS untracked; `template/` singular is NOT — they differ by one char.) Untracks all of `lib`/`bin`/`templates` (316 of the 335 git-tracked `.devforge/` code-dir files; `lib` alone is 314); only `.devforge/template/`'s 19 files stay tracked/indexed by design. Phases 1–2 built behind python-engineer → python-reviewer (a scratch-git-fixture test asserting the three dirs untrack while VERSIONED root files stay tracked); docs (Phase 4) in `src/devforge/storage-rules.md` (new CODE class) + `CHANGELOG.md` + this list. **Version NOT bumped** — deferred to the maintainer (matching plan 49).

- `62-SMT-REQUIREMENTS-CONSISTENCY-PLAN.md` — **Phases 1–7 SHIPPED 2026-07-15/16** on `develop-2.0-init` (working tree, not committed); Phase 8 (docs, this pass) + **Phase 9 (consumer/testForge20 e2e) remain, user-driven HARD GATE.** A new opt-in, spec-tier **`/spec-check`** command — the requirement-level mirror of `/grill`, run BETWEEN `/specify` and `/plan` — that proves whether a feature spec's acceptance criteria are self-consistent via the **Z3 SMT solver** (the framework's first requirement-tier consistency check; the one capability where AWS Kiro was architecturally ahead). Neurosymbolic: the read-only `spec-formalizer` agent translates each AC → a typed constraint IR (helper owns the shape, LLM fills values), Z3 proves the conjunction, and `unsat_core()` names the exact conflicting AC set. Helper subpackage `src/devforge/lib/_spec_check/` (`ir_schema`/`_solve`/`_consume`/`_brief`/`_report`/`_preflight`/`_seed`/`_quorum`/`_cli`; ~369 tests) + `spec_check_helper{,.py}` launcher (8 verbs) + `src/commands/spec-check/main.md` + `references/{report-format,formalization-guidance}.md` + `src/agents/spec-formalizer.md` (roster 18→19; `model_tier: think`, read-only `tools: Read, Grep, Glob`) + emitter `_PROMOTED += spec-check`. Output `specs/[feature]/spec-check.md` (**D4 two-layer surface — the human checks the TRANSLATION, not the proof**) + a **verdict-gated** backward `spec-check-seed.json` (`source="spec-check"`, `target_stage="spec"`, consumed by `/specify`) written ONLY on a REVISE-SPEC-matching human pick (plan-39 pattern). **Honest scope (D9/D11/D13 — mandatory under-promise, baked into every user-facing string):** consistency prover NOT mind-reader (catches ACs contradicting EACH OTHER, not intent — a single coherent-but-wrong AC passes); permission clashes caught ONLY when a permitting case is asserted reachable (**D9 — pure `z3.Implies`, NO reachability injection**; the formalizer's recovery lever formalizes a permission-GRANT as a reachable-case assertion; NOT "permission/role logic" generally); the Z3 proof is deterministic over a **human-checked, quorum-stable** formalization — a **fixed 2-pass quorum (D13)** surfaces a contradiction only if its `ac_id`-core reproduces across a majority (the verdict-reproducibility guard — the English→IR formalization is a stochastic LLM step, so "deterministically prove" is honest only about the SOLVER, not the verdict; an `unstable` non-reproducing one-off is surfaced low-confidence, never REVISE-SPEC). **Opt-in ADVISORY, never blocking (D14)** — blocking belongs to DETERMINISTIC gates (forcing-functions), advisory to the NEUROSYMBOLIC; a future session must not "strengthen" it into a gate. **(AMENDED 2026-08-19 — D14 was REVERSED IN PART by plan 82's ratified D6, by a dated in-place amendment block under D14 itself; the sentence above records the 2026-07-16 decision and is superseded on the pipeline-position question only.** `/devforge:spec-check` is now RUN-mandatory — `/devforge:plan` PHASE 0a.8 fails closed until a fresh `spec-check.md` exists for the resolved spec — while the command is still typed by the USER and never auto-invoked. **The narrow reading is load-bearing: what is mandatory is that the check RAN and its report is FRESH; the VERDICT never binds**, a `REVISE-SPEC` report satisfies the gate exactly as `CONSISTENT` does, the human owns every non-clean disposition, and a clean verdict auto-accepts. The standing constraint that replaced the absolute last sentence: no verdict condition, no override flag, no skip arm. **Everything else in this entry stands** — D3, D4, D9, D11, D13 and D10 are all explicitly unchanged by that amendment, and OQ-9's nudge stays unbuilt, its premise discharged by the reversal rather than by the nudge.**)** **`_shared/` relocations:** seed schema `_grill/seed_schema.py` → `_shared/seed_schema.py` (multi-source `SEED_SOURCES=("grill","spec-check")`, `_grill` re-pointed byte-behaviorally); AC parser `parse_acs` `_verify/_ac.py` → `_shared/spec_acs.py` (so `_spec_check` reuses it without cross-command coupling; back-compat shim in `_verify/_ac.py`); `/specify` Phase-0.5 consumer generalized to consume BOTH seed sources (`specs/*/*-seed.json`, source-aware narration, multi-match branch). **D9 reconciled + D13/D14/OQ-9 added post-ratification 2026-07-16** — a design critique caught a decision-record-vs-build drift (D9's original "assumed reachable" prose contradicted the shipped pure-`Implies` code — the exact consistency-drift a `/spec-check` plan must not have) and the stochastic-formalizer verdict-reproducibility hole. Package renamed `_smt` → `_spec_check` (convention/`--only` fix). Built behind python-engineer→python-reviewer (test-first) + instruction-author→instruction-reviewer+claude-code-guide loops; plan-41 reachability gate GREEN. **OQ-9 (deferred, NOT v1):** a deterministic `/specify` nudge ("N conditional/numeric ACs over shared vars → consider `/spec-check`") to make opt-in cover the specs the author was confident about.

- `65-STATUS-FLIP-ARTIFACT-COMMIT-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–4 DONE (working tree) 2026-07-17** on `develop-2.0-init`; only **Phase 5 (consumer/the consumer install e2e) remains, user-driven HARD GATE.** Fixes a dirty git tree at `/implement` start, found in the consumer install. **Root cause:** the per-step artifact commit (plan 37) stages only each command's OWN new artifacts, but a pipeline command also MUTATES its upstream input's `**Status**:` line on consume (`check-status-and-flip <path>` in `/plan`+`/breakdown`; `flip-spec-status` in `/verify`) and never re-committed that flip — so `spec.md` (Draft→Approved by `/plan` PHASE 0b), `plan.md` (Draft→Approved by `/breakdown` PHASE 0b), and `spec.md` again (→Complete by `/verify` PHASE 6, EXPLICITLY excluded from its commit per the old `verify/main.md:376`) each sat modified-uncommitted until `/finalize`'s `git add specs/<feature>/` safety-net swept them. The final PR was already clean; the bug was the confusing mid-pipeline dirty tree — a hole in plan 37's "every command commits what it writes," now closed to "…what it writes OR mutates." **Fix (instruction-only — reuses the shipped `artifact_helper commit-artifacts` verb, NO Python, NO new commit call, D1/D3):** `/plan` adds `spec.md` to its Phase-4 commit path-set; `/breakdown` adds `plan.md` to its Phase-5 path-set; `/verify` appends `spec.md` to its Cleanup path-set CONDITIONALLY — only when PHASE-6 `flip-spec-status` returned `flipped:true` (APPROVED, no task-completion blocker), omitting it on NEEDS WORK/REJECTED/blocked (D2). Reconciled the `verify/main.md` line-29+376 exclusion notes and the `src/CLAUDE.md` `/plan`+`/breakdown`+`/verify` one-liners. **Out of scope, documented:** `.devforge/spec-stamps.jsonl` (plan 49 D3 defers its commit to `/finalize`); the `research/…/handoff.json` `outcome.spec_path` back-fill (called by NO command — not systematically pipeline-triggered, OQ-2). Note: `check-status-and-flip` is overloaded — only the `/plan`+`/breakdown` positional-path form is a dirtying artifact-status flip; the `--to <phase>` form in `/verify`/`/grill`/`/review`/`/audit` just advances an EPHEMERAL run-state JSON and is not at issue. Phase 0 ratified by the maintainer 2026-07-17 (D1–D4 + scope). Built behind three parallel instruction-author → instruction-reviewer + one claude-code-guide loops (the `/verify` review verified inline after two infra-stalled reviewer dispatches; every reviewer finding fixed — sentences broadened to cover PHASE 0b's full `flipped`/`inserted`/no-op state space, line-21 commit-set summaries reconciled). Version NOT bumped (deferred to the maintainer). Extends plan 37 (per-step artifact commit).

- `66-PROPERTY-BASED-TESTING-AND-NARROWING-RULE-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–4 SHIPPED 2026-08-03** on `develop-2.0-init` (working tree); only **Phase 6 (testForge20 e2e) remains, user-driven (HARD GATE)**; Phase 5 (IR-stub generation) DELIBERATELY ABSENT (follow-on plan gated on plan-62 IR persistence — the `/spec-check` IR is scratch-only, verified). layer-wide must name every affected caller; explicit anti-KISS tie-breaker) — parsed as rule label "Narrowing" by `_split_design_principles` (8 §3.6 rules now; `verify-universal-defaults` needs no code change, it parses the live canonical file); `/plan` 1.3 sub-question 7 + PHASE 2.5 step-7 Narrowing-restriction trace + `architect.md` Rule-9 Narrowing forcing step; mechanical detector deliberately deferred (prose-first v1). **WI-1 (property lane, Phases 2–4):** trigger = ARCHITECT-NAMED targets at `/plan` (NOT `/spec-check` IR — opt-in + scratch-only, the v1 premise inverted at rewrite): 1.3 sub-question 8 → `### Pure-Builder Targets` plan-template table (`Target | File | Why pure`, conditional-include) → `_plan/handoff_schema.py` `PureBuilderRow` + `BreakdownSeeds.pure_builder_targets` (default-empty, back-compat byte-proven) → `plan_helper` `_parse_pure_builder_targets` in `finalize-handoff` → `/breakdown` `read-plan-handoff` renders the `### Pure-Builder Targets (property-test lane)` sub-block → PHASE 3 `### Property-test tasks` emission rule (dedicated `qa-engineer` task per the plan-41 D2 row, `render-task-file --property-targets` emits the new optional `**Property targets**:` task field, stack matrix fast-check/hypothesis + ≥100 iterations + special-char/unicode/empty arbitraries + pinned-seed/counterexample + honest-skip for unsupported stacks; - `72-UPDATE-SH-REPAIR-GUARD-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE 2026-08-12 (closed by maintainer — all phases complete, no pending e2e)** on `develop-2.0-init` (commits `5790d73` `update.sh` + `src/manifest.json`, `b96735e` `install.sh`); **Phase 4 reproduction PASSED mechanically** — a deliberate departure from this repo's user-driven testForge20 gate, since the incident reproduces fully in a local fixture (transcript in the plan under `## Reproduction (post-fix)`). Fixes the incident where a consumer eval install — a fresh checkout of a committed "forge setup" state, so plans 56 + 63's gitignored-and-untracked CODE dirs were absent — carried every VERSIONED artifact and **zero helper code**, and the `update.sh` run meant to provision it restored nothing: both `read -r confirm` gates (the equal-version bail and the apply confirmation) are unguarded simple commands under `set -euo pipefail`, so `read` on EOF killed the script at whichever it reached first (exit 1, no `Aborted.` line, a dangling `[y/N]` as the only diagnostic). **D1 repair guard** — a lib-only completeness sentinel (`.devforge/lib` present AND ≥1 top-level executable `*_helper` **file**, `-f` and `-x` both, written errexit-safe inside an `if`), evaluated after the plan-44 drift check, sets **`FORCE=true`** — the one switch that already clears BOTH gates and nothing else reachable there; deliberately **version-independent** (an incomplete install with an OLDER version dies at the second gate, so coupling repair to version equality would miss it) and needing no new copy logic (the `templateOwned` loop already creates missing files, the exec-bit blocks re-chmod, the emit rewrites `command-refs/`). **D2** — a complete, up-to-date, non-tty run without `--force` is now a clean exit-0 no-op instead of an exit-1 crash at the prompt, nested inside the existing `if [ "$FORCE" != true ]` so `--force` is never swallowed. **OQ-1 → (b)** — the surviving non-tty apply refusal prints on **stderr** before the `read` can fail (same exit 1, now legible). **OQ-2 → folded into Phase 1** — `.devforge/bin/` gained a `templateOwned` manifest pair + a `bin/*.sh` chmod block, so a repair restores an executable `chrome-devtools-mcp.sh` instead of leaving `src/mcp.json`'s server entry dead (the D1 sentinel was NOT widened to require it — the same run restores it). **D3** — `install.sh` now stamps `.claude/template-version` at the end of a FULL install (read via `$PY_CMD`, not `jq`; fail-soft; `--only` excluded by construction), so a fresh install no longer reports `(unknown)` to its first `update.sh`. Closes plan 56's OQ-1 **on the `update.sh` path only** — a fresh clone that runs neither installer still has no helpers. Built behind python-engineer → python-reviewer (4 findings). **Version NOT bumped** — deferred to the maintainer; the `CHANGELOG.md` entry sits under `## [Unreleased]`.

- `73-LITERAL-ARCHAEOLOGY-EVIDENCE-ARM-PLAN.md` — **Phases 0–6 SHIPPED 2026-08-12** on `develop-2.0-init`; **Phase 7 (consumer e2e, user-driven HARD GATE) WAIVED by the maintainer 2026-08-13 — NOT executed** (the mechanism is build-verified — Phases 1–5 each closed behind both review loops — but **NOT consumer-validated**; the recipe stands in the plan as the validation procedure if an opportunistic e2e is ever run, the same disposition as plan 26's waived Phase 5). **Commit state is SPLIT:** the code is committed — `bac6008` carries this plan's `src/` + `tests/` work — while the plan DOCUMENT is deliberately NOT committed and stays untracked, because this repo is PUBLIC and that file carries private-client identifiers, so committing it would publish fresh client data (plans 74 and 75 are untracked for the same reason; a planned orphan-branch migration that drops those files is the intended route, not a completed step). Read the plan's "shipped" claims accordingly: released for the code half, untracked for the record of it. Origin: a two-install A/B on the same maintenance ticket in a consumer install — one run traced a hardcoded literal's git history and found a long-standing user-visible regression, the other examined the identical code, ran no history query, concluded "live, out of scope", and hardened that wrong call three more times through machinery that all fired correctly. **GAP 1 (mode gate)** — `/research`'s Phase 2.5b archaeology lane (`git log -S` → `git show --stat` → `git blame` → a 6-value `intent` enum → a per-intent recovery rule) was bug-mode-gated at THREE sites: the spec trigger, verify **check 17** (`bug_mode_17`), and the presence gate `finalize-handoff` enforces in `_research/handoff_schema.py`. Removal/cleanup tickets classify as enhancements, so the lane went inert exactly where literal provenance is load-bearing. **D1** drops the mode conjunct at all three together (plan 67's check-8 decouple shape); **checks 15/16 + Phase 2.4d stay bug-gated** — they trace runtime symptom VALUES an enhancement has none of, whereas 2.5b traces COMMIT HISTORY, identical in both modes. This corrects a for-the-record assumption in plan 69 D6, back-annotated at `69-…-PLAN.md:41` (a correction of a recorded assumption, NOT the overturning of a ratified decision — that clause was never a ratification item). **GAP 2 (trigger shape — the deeper half)** — both existing arms cover a literal that IS the defect or is ABOUT TO BE REPLACED; neither covers a literal reasoned FROM, where the plan is to DELETE or KEEP and check 17's replacement-prose detector cannot match. **So GAP 1 alone would NOT have caught the incident.** **D2** adds trigger **arm (3)** (literal-as-evidence: a literal's value cited as grounds for a dead/live · in/out-of-scope · keep/delete call); **D3** adds a separately labelled per-intent EVIDENCE recovery block **beside — never merged into** — the fix-layer rules (`{placeholder, forgotten, inherited-refactor}` ⇒ the value is NOT evidence of intent, re-derive or downgrade; `deliberate` ⇒ the call may stand but must cite the introducing SHA + subject); **D4** supplies the mechanical carrier — an OPTIONAL `--rests-on-literal <file:line>|none` on `record-finding` forced at verify by a new mode-independent **check 20** (sub-option (b): `record-finding` has 74 subprocess call sites across four out-of-package test files, so a required argparse arg was impossible — the plan-69 D1/D5 frozen-signature precedent). **OQ-5** (found at drafting, resequenced INTO Phase 1 because the widening made the collision live) added a REQUIRED `--use <fix-layer|evidence>` to `record-literal-archaeology` (7 in-package call sites — that precedent does not bind it), defaulting to `fix-layer` on deserialization, and scoped the escalation-cite validator to `fix-layer` rows only; without it an evidence row recording `forgotten` on a deletion-shaped recommendation hard-`ValueError`ed at `finalize-handoff`. Two follow-on repairs shipped with Phase 2: **`classify-finding-literal`** (check 20's original remedy told the user to re-call the append-only `record-finding`, which appends a DUPLICATE and leaves the violation standing — the SECOND occurrence of that trap after check 19's; the class is narrow and needs no forcing function, per a recorded negative-result audit of every check's remedy message) with its blanket update opt-in behind an explicit **`--all`** guard, and a **TOCTOU** fix. **Phase 3 (D7)** — the evidence-lane declaration: a fixed four-bool `EvidenceLanes` (`static_graph`/`text_search`/`runtime_probe`/`history`) on `Handoff`, a `set-evidence-lanes` setter, `None` per-lane defaults (so "never declared" ≠ "declared false"), a declaration-EXISTS guard at `finalize-handoff` — **a call-happened check, never a per-lane-value check; it fires identically for all-true and all-false, so a future session must NOT read it as a D7 violation and delete it** — and an unconditional `## Evidence Lanes Consulted` render. **Phase 4 (D6 — maintainer-ratified BUILD, overriding the plan's own shelve recommendation and the plan-48 precedent it cited)** — `/discover`'s absence-claim provenance lane: `_cmds_absence.py` + `record-absence-probe` + `requires_absence_probe`, keyed on the NEGATIVE claim ("nothing exists ⇒ build new") as the exact complement of `cmd_verify`'s Rule G, with sentinel canonicalization ON STORE and a conditional `## Absence Probes` render. **Phase 5** carries archaeology to `/plan` — a `**Literal provenance**` block in `plan_helper._render_research_plan_seeds` reading `spec_seeds.literal_archaeology` as a SIBLING read (the carrier already existed, so OQ-2's "which carrier?" was mis-posed) + architect-consult sub-question **10**, appended so nothing renumbers. Built behind python-engineer → python-reviewer + instruction-author → instruction-reviewer + claude-code-guide loops throughout (Phase 2 alone: four passes of each); ~1395 tests green at Phase 4. **DO NOT read the closure as a clean slate — EIGHT items remain OPEN; Phase 6 was docs-only and closed none, and the Phase 7 waiver closes none either, because a decision not to run a check is not a fix** — seven of them fully stated in the plan's `## Context for next session` and the eighth (item 8 below) in its `## Non-goals`: (1) **`record-literal-archaeology`'s `--use` is UNREPAIRABLE** — first-write-wins on `(literal, file_line)`, a re-call is a silent no-op at exit 0, only `reset-report` recovers; the twin of what `classify-finding-literal` fixed, and worse, since a wrong `use` silently exempts the row from the escalation-cite validator (unfixable AND invisible). Still unscoped; fix shape by analogy is a `classify-archaeology-use` sibling or extending the existing repair verb; (2) **the G-mirror trap** — `/discover`'s Rule G violation message prescribes citing the BARE path, while the mirror in the handoff schema demands the full prefixed string, so following the gate's own instruction is what a later gate rejects (pre-existing since plan 03, proven reachable in Phase 4; fix either the mirror or the message — leaving them mismatched does not); (3) **the wrapper-repo Source Root gap — needs its own plan** — `/discover` + `/research` are the only command surfaces without the Source Root resolver, and in wrapper mode a git query against the wrong root returns an empty history **indistinguishable from a genuine absence**, whose recorded `--found false` satisfies the guard exactly as a real one does (caveats now ship in both files; the real fix spans two helper subpackages); (4) the caller-scope justification is free text no check ties to archaeology rows — the exact carrier of the reasoning class this plan exists for; (5) the evidence-lane declaration is carried but never surfaced by `/plan`; (6) **an ACCEPTED LIMITATION, not a pending item** — divergent duplicate `findings[]` rows have no correct-and-safe repair short of `reset-report`; `--all` makes the fabrication VISIBLE, not avoidable (candidate real fixes: an ordinal selector or a `retract-finding` verb, neither in scope); (7) a parked footgun — a `return _die(...)` inside a `with _state_transaction(...)` block still runs the post-`yield` write; harmless at the one pre-existing site, a trap for any count-check-then-reject added inside a transaction; (8) pre-existing plan-number citations shipping into consumer specs in **TWO** files (`src/commands/plan/main.md` and `src/commands/research/main.md`), unresolvable in a consumer install — routed to a future surgical cleanup. The waived Phase 7 was designed as a **known-answer regression anchor** (the correct output is already known from the better run); it never ran, so **no anchor was added to `REGRESSION-ANCHORS.md`** — adding one on a successful run is part of the retained recipe, not a completed step. The plan's `## Build discipline` section — organized by the moment each rule fires (DRAFTING / BUILDING / REVIEWING / CLOSING) — is arguably a more durable output than its code; read the group for the moment you are in.
