(3) plans 05 and 10 call the structural-integration check **§7** while the shipped file numbers it **8**, recorded so no session "fixes" it by renumbering the live file. **Plans 62 / 82 / 85 are NOT dependencies and were untouched: this plan built no gate at all, so nothing it did engages plan 62's D14 or the reversals plans 82 and 85 propose — and a future session must not cite this plan as precedent in either direction.** `FINDINGS.md` finding 1 (the §3.6 Narrowing FALSE BINARY) is **untouched**: D2 added a block to §3.6 alongside Narrowing and neither amends, weakens nor resolves that finding.

- `87-ARTIFACT-LANGUAGE-GUARD-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE (build) 2026-08-23 (Phases 0–3; Phase 0 ratified in-session the same day — D1–D5, nothing left open). Phase 4 consumer observation DEFERRED — a user-driven HARD GATE that has NOT run; build-verified, NOT consumer-validated.** Created 2026-08-23 on `develop-2.0-init`. The plan document carries no private-client identifiers and is **committed normally**, unlike the deliberately-untracked plans 73/74/75; its `## Evidence constraint` records that the two untracked evidence files at repo root are neither read nor cited, and that **this plan needs neither** — its motivation is a configuration change plus a mechanical consequence, not an incident. **Origin.** The maintainer reads English as a second language and on 2026-08-23 set Claude Code's user-level `language` key to `uk` so terminal prose renders in Ukrainian — a legitimate, documented use of an official settings key (*"Have Claude respond in a language other than English"*, topic *"Model and responses"*, scope *"Any file"*, verified live at `https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings-reference`). **The consequence is mechanical and is the whole problem: that key is an INSTRUCTION TO THE MODEL, not a filter over what the model writes**, and nothing in Claude Code partitions terminal prose from file writes — so `spec.md`, `plan.md`, task files, research reports, code comments and commit messages all sit in the blast radius at a nonzero, unquantified rate. **The plan is written so a ratifier can see that there is NO incident, NO failing run and NO observed leak behind it** rather than inferring an evidentiary base that does not exist. **Why an English-only corpus is worth defending**, recorded once so no phase re-derives it: the corpus is a MACHINE INPUT before it is a human one (every downstream command re-reads these artifacts, and every prompt, persona, checklist and rule name is English); mechanical checks match English tokens (dead-code anchor tokens matched verbatim, `/devforge:verify`'s memory-bucket rubric matching `## What Failed` / `## What Worked` / `## Known Pitfalls`, the constitution splitter recognising bold English headers); and commits and specs are read later by people who never set the key. **Two separable layers, and only the first shapes behavior.** (1) **Instruction guard, zero Python** — the emitted `CLAUDE.md` gained `### Always` item **15, "English in files"**: all file content and commit messages stay in English (specs, plans, code, comments, docs) regardless of any operator response-language setting, which applies to conversation only; **verbatim quotes of user-reported words may keep their original language.** Three wording properties are deliberate and must survive any later edit: it **names no settings key** (a consumer-facing rule outlives any particular Claude Code surface, so a hard-coded `language` would rot), it **names the artifact classes explicitly** (a rule saying only "files" invites the reading that a code comment is not a file), and **its final clause exists to make the rule and the detector tell the same story** — plan 83's `conversational (in-window user report; no report file)` seed evidence legitimately quotes a non-English user, and without the clause the rule would forbid exactly what the framework's own design requires an artifact to carry. It is a **pure append** — the list ended at 14, `### Never` was untouched, nothing renumbered — and a repo-wide grep confirmed **nothing counts the `### Always` items**, the only citations in being by item NUMBER (`Key Rules #4`), which an append leaves true. `README.md`'s `## Install` section gained the matching operator note. (2) **Advisory detector** — `artifact_helper commit-artifacts` scans, after staging succeeds and before the commit, the set enumerated by `git diff --cached --name-only -z` (the staged set, never `--paths`, which may name a directory whose whole subtree is staged), decoding each file with `errors="replace"` and scanning it plus the commit label for U+0400–U+052F, writing one `commit-artifacts: warning: …` line per offending file to **stderr**. **Ratified decisions: D1** ADVISORY WARN that never blocks — precedents are plan 34's hygiene flags (*"ADVISORY and … intentionally NOT in the blocker list"*) and plan 44's WARN-only fail-soft drift check; **fail-closed was refused because a known legitimate non-English class already exists** (the quoted-user evidence above), so a hard fail would block correctly-authored content on day one and force a carve-out the zero-escape-hatch policy forbids by name; **the strengthening trigger is the FIRST CONFIRMED LEAK the warning failed to stop**, and until one is observed this plan may not be cited as evidence the warning works. **D2** Cyrillic-only over U+0400–U+052F (Basic + Supplement, covering і/ї/є/ґ) via stdlib `re` — plan 62's stdlib-clean stance holds, `install.sh` is untouched, the historic Cyrillic Extended blocks are deliberately out of range, and general non-Latin detection is **recorded as possible but NOT built** (Python's `re` has no `\p{Script=…}`, so it would mean a range table or a third-party dependency); **the asymmetry is accepted rather than smoothed — the rule is universal, the tripwire is scoped to the one script anybody has configured.** **D3** stderr plain lines in the `commit-artifacts: warning:` shape the function already emits one branch away, with **no stdout-JSON key**: the happy-path test asserts the exact stdout object, and the two stdout shapes ARE the machine contract, so an advisory field there would invite a future caller to gate on it — the fail-closed behavior D1 refuses. ⚠ **The detector must never append to `stage_errors`**, which is the exit-1 channel; that was the single most likely implementation defect and is pinned by a Verify criterion. **D4** the `### Always` item-15 landing site (rejected: `## Artifact Storage`, whose bullets scope where files live, not what they contain, and which says nothing about code comments or commit messages). **D5** the `README.md` `## Install` note naming the two PERSONAL carriers — `~/.claude/settings.json` (*"You, in every project on this machine"*) and `.claude/settings.local.json` (*"You, in this one project only"*) — and steering OFF `.claude/settings.json`, because `install.sh` unconditionally overwrites that file, so a key placed there is destroyed on the next install ride. **The note quotes fact 3 VERBATIM** — Claude Code *"keeps it out of git when it creates the file; if you create it by hand, add it to `.gitignore` yourself"* — because that fact's entire content is a conditional and every paraphrase collapses it into "it's gitignored," which is false for exactly the operator most likely to be reading it; the other two facts are paraphrased because each is a single low-nuance scope statement, and quoting all four would have pushed the paragraph past its section's register. **An `install.sh` closing-message `echo` is the recorded NAMED FALLBACK** if the README note proves unseen — rejected for this plan only because `install.sh` was recently touched by plan 72 and a docs-only phase with a zero-script diff is trivially reviewable. **⚠ Coverage bound — do not over-read the plan's title.** *"Every byte written to a file"* is the GOAL; the detector rides `artifact_helper commit-artifacts` ONLY, and **`implement_helper wip-commit` is a second, independent commit path that is deliberately NOT covered**, so a leak inside a per-task or `[WIP] fix:` code commit is invisible to this guard. **Zero new checks, zero validators, zero gates** — plan 75's tripwire, both halves; the detector is a warning, which is neither. **Phases:** 0 ratification (closed same day) · 1 the two instruction edits · 2 the detector + its tests (four CLI cases incl. the pure-ASCII false-positive floor and a fail-soft exception case, plus direct predicate tests) · 3 sweep + records · **4 consumer observation, user-driven HARD GATE, NOT RUN** — two known-answer cases scored **as a PAIR** (one Cyrillic word ⇒ warning on stderr, exit 0, stdout unchanged; a real ASCII pipeline artifact commit ⇒ no language warning at all), because a detector that passes the first by warning on everything fails the second. **⚠ A clean Phase 4 is NOT evidence the guard works** — it is evidence the detector does not fire on clean input, and D1's strengthening trigger is an observed LEAK, which that phase cannot produce. **Discovered while drafting, NOT owned and not fixed:** (1) `src/manifest.json`'s `projectOwned` list is honoured by `update.sh` and **ignored by `install.sh`**, which reads the manifest for `version` only while unconditionally overwriting `.claude/settings.json` — a file that list names; the overwrite is DOCUMENTED behavior in `src/CLAUDE.md`, so this is a scope-wording observation about the manifest description, not a defect in either script, and D5 works around it by naming the two files neither script touches; (2) **no repo-wide convention previously existed for what language artifacts are written in** — the requirement was implicit in every English prompt and English-token mechanical check and stated nowhere, so item 15 **establishes precedent for the `### Always` list rather than following one**. `src/devforge/storage-rules.md` was checked and deliberately NOT edited: this plan creates no artifact and changes no artifact's shape, and **recording that no-op is what stops a later session from "harmonizing" it in**.

- `88-COLD-FIX-BUGS-LANE-PLAN.md` — **✅ CLOSED 2026-08-27 (build) — closed by maintainer statement (*"the test is a separate story — mark it as done"*), and that statement is the whole closure authority, not a fresh per-phase verification. Phases 0–4 complete 2026-08-26 (Phase 0 ratified 2026-08-26 by maintainer statement, D1–D7 + OQ-1–OQ-7 all decided, every item AS RECOMMENDED, nothing left open; commits `92592c5` Phase 1 / `bf0c4b9` Phase 2 / `7ec8f69` Phase 3, `d09b7e2` Phase 4). ⚠ Phase 5 consumer e2e DEFERRED AS A SEPARATE EFFORT — user-driven HARD GATE, NOT WAIVED and NOT run. The close covers the BUILD and is NOT a pass: build-verified, NOT consumer-validated, and the cold lane has never been observed end to end; the three known-answer anchors below remain that effort's recipe, anchors 1 and 2 scored as a PAIR.** Created 2026-08-25 on `develop-2.0-init`; the document carries no private-client identifiers and is committed normally. **Origin, two independent halves both verified before drafting.** (1) A **cold** bug — noticed outside any feature's post-`/devforge:implement`/pre-`/devforge:summarize` window — had exactly two routes: hand-fix it (bypassing every gate) or the full ~10-command chain (disproportionate for a one-file defect); `/devforge:fix`'s own empty-list STOP said so in its shipped text. **The maintainer confirmed on 2026-08-25 that this friction was already OBSERVED**, so it is not a predicted cost — but it is also **not a measurement** (see the plan-55 note below). (2) `bugs/NNN-*.md` had **NO consumer**: two producers (`/devforge:report-bug` `Source: manual`, `/devforge:verify` PHASE 9 `Source: verify`), a three-state status field, and nothing that ever read or advanced one — `src/commands/research/main.md` returned **zero** `bugs/` hits while `/devforge:report-bug`'s forward pointer named it. **The rejected alternative, recorded with reasoning so it is not re-proposed:** a "light lane through the main pipeline" (a `minor` classification thinning artifacts across research/specify/plan/breakdown), declined on two independent grounds — ceremony cost lives in **GATE COUNT, not artifact size**, so thinner artifacts leave all ten gates standing; and an "if minor" branch inside the main chain is the escape-hatch shape the zero-escape-hatch meta-rule forbids by name. Containing the lane in one command keeps the main chain's rules absolute. **Ratified decisions.** **D1** the trigger is an explicit `bugs/NNN-<slug>.md` argument — MECHANICAL, no judgment, no flag, no severity threshold — and plan 26's D2 free-text ban is **EXTENDED, not reversed** (a bug FILE is a written finding with title/severity/evidence/file-table, so *"consumes findings, never invents them"* stays literally true); the **code-confirmation duty is mandatory and not droppable** because a bug file is a CAPTURE, not a confirmation. **D2** three feature-bound sub-phases are bypassed on the cold path — feature resolution, the `in-fix-window` gate, **and the `read-findings` call** (all three need a `--feature` that does not exist; `in_fix_window` would return `no_tasks_dir` and gate a cold run out by construction, and keeping a call whose answer the spec tells you to ignore is not a check) — as an explicit dated amendment to plan 26's in-window rule, narrow because that rule's subject is a sealed FEATURE UNIT a cold run does not have. ⚠ **One protection IS genuinely lost and is recorded, not argued away:** the window gate also refused to run mid-`/devforge:implement` (`not_all_tasks_complete`) and the cold lane has no such interlock. **D3** the lane ends in ONE clean `fix(<scope>): <title>` commit — no `[WIP]`, because no `/devforge:finalize` will ever squash it — via fork 1 **(a)** extending `implement_helper wip-commit` with a `--final` mode (re-affirming plan 26's own 2026-06-19 "extend the one binary, never a second composer" note) and fork 2 **(i)** wrapper keeping `[TICKET-ID] - <title>`; ⚠ the existing full-branch-name fallback means a cold wrapper fix on `develop` reads `[develop] - <title>`, **a string seen at ratification and ACCEPTED**. The `--final` + `--scope` shape stayed a recommendation Phase 1 could diverge from with a stated reason (it did not). ⚠ **`--final` must NOT clear `.devforge/wip.md`** — a cold run wrote no marker and clearing one it did not write destroys another command's crash-recovery state; this was the single most likely Phase-1 defect and is pinned by tests. **D4** exactly ONE `bugs/` write on the ONE consumed file (`**Status**` → `Fixed`, the empty `**Fixed**:` line dated, `## Fix Notes` filled), amending `/devforge:fix` Rule 7 NARROWLY — **the CREATION ban is absolute in both lanes and `/devforge:report-bug` remains the sole creator** — via fork **(b)**, a `close-bug` verb over a new function in `_shared/bug_file.py` on helper-owns-shape (⚠ a recorded divergence from the drafting brief's "only D3 is Python" framing, so **Phase 1 carried TWO deliverables** and remained the only Python phase). It **COLLECTS plan 26's own chartered `close-bug` deferral**, and refuses the close — writing nothing — on a missing file, a status that is not `Open`/`In Progress`, or a `## Fix Notes` body that is no longer the placeholder, so **hand-written notes are never overwritten**. ⚠ The schema is `**Fixed**:` and `## Fix Notes` — **there is no `Fixed date` field and no bold `Fix Notes` field**. **D5** the cold bounce recommends **`/devforge:research`**, never `/devforge:specify` (which blocks until a research or discover handoff exists in a feature directory, so naming it would name a command that refuses to run), writes **NO seed** (plan 83's seed model is feature-scoped by construction — `write_seed` joins onto `feature_dir` and `--feature-dir` is required), asks nothing, and leaves the bug `Open`; ⚠ **the diagnosis is spoken and then lost** — the same loss plan 83 fixed for the in-window bounce — recorded as an accepted bound, with a feature-less seed carrier plus a `/devforge:research` consumer named as the future plan an OBSERVED repetition would justify. **D6** the emitted `CLAUDE.md` fix-or-file offer went two arms → three (in-window repair / cold repair / a change needing the full chain from `/devforge:research`), discriminator = **defect-repair vs change, explicitly NEVER file count**, ADVISORY and never a gate (precedent: `/devforge:plan`'s stakes-hint). Ratified as **reading (i)**: the rubric lives in `src/CLAUDE.md`, `/devforge:report-bug` Rule 8 stays **byte-unchanged** and gains **no forward pointer**. ⚠ **The rubric is ADVISORY model judgment with nothing checking it — the ONLY mechanical net in this plan is D5's in-command bounce, which fires after the user has already typed the command.** ⚠ **Its counter-argument is retained UNRESOLVED**: the command's own PHASE-4 pointer (naming `/devforge:research`/`/devforge:specify`) and the rubric can give a user two different answers in one session, and the command's answer is the one printed in the transcript — **a Phase-5 observation of that contradiction is the named trigger to re-open as reading (ii)**. **D7** NO numeric scope cap — v1's ≤5-files guard deliberately not reproduced, since file count is a proxy for the wrong quantity (plan 27's D3 *"routing state, not a size metric"* is the precedent). **OQ answers:** OQ-1 confirmation SUPPLIES `files_cited` and an unlocatable defect STOPS the run; **OQ-2 YES**, the full back half runs unchanged (declining it would have collapsed the proportionality argument, not trimmed it); OQ-3 mode-dependent vehicle — standalone rides the same commit, wrapper rides `artifact_helper commit-artifacts` into the install repo with its `[WIP] ` label prefix **ACCEPTED** rather than given a second composer; OQ-4 memory read UNCHANGED; OQ-5 cold-fixing a sealed feature's CODE is acceptable (the seal covers artifacts, not source) ⚠ **with the honest residual that the sealed feature's `verification.md` keeps asserting ACs proved against a since-changed diff, and nothing updates it**; OQ-6 plan 63's 13/7 counts UNCHANGED, `/devforge:fix` stays `disable-model-invocation: true`; OQ-7 scratch literals reused, the serial-runs assumption recorded as part of the answer. **A D4 ↔ OQ-3 conflict surfaced during Phase 1 and was resolved 2026-08-26**: D4's "flip after the commit lands, fix-notes carry the SHA" and OQ-3's "standalone rides the same commit" cannot both hold, because a SHA cannot be written into a file riding the commit that mints it. **OQ-3 controls the VEHICLE; D4's SHA clause is narrowed BY MODE** — standalone calls `close-bug` FIRST (notes carry the commit SUBJECT line) then commits both together; wrapper commits the code first (notes carry the returned source SHA) then commits the flip separately. ⚠ **Standalone therefore has a non-atomic window**: a `wip-commit --final` failure leaves the bug flipped but uncommitted, which is not a clean rollback point, and re-running `close-bug` exits 2 — the emitted recovery is to resolve the failure and re-run ONLY the commit. **⚠ SUPERSEDES `55-STANDALONE-BUG-FIX-LANE-PLAN.md`** (DEFERRED 2026-07-10; marked superseded 2026-08-26), which designed this same lane and was NOT in this plan's own sweep list — found by the Phase-4 `bugs/` sweep. Its **D-command-surface (Option 1, overload `/devforge:fix`)** and **D-batch (single)** leans were followed; its **D-writeback lean (manual, to preserve plan 26 D4) was REVERSED**, the deciding evidence being the no-consumer finding it did not have; its **D-diagnosis-agent** was taken only half — the inline code-read shipped, **the `runtime-debugger` escalation arm did NOT**. ⚠ **Two things plan 55 designed are NOT built and must not be read as shipped:** the **CBM blast-radius tripwire** (plan 88's bounce is a judgment classification, not a caller/cross-layer measurement, and D7 refuses any numeric bound) and **decoupled standalone firing of stage-coupled floor gates** (sidestepped rather than solved — the cold lane runs the gates that were never stage-coupled and simply has no design-anchor or AC gate to fire; the general decoupling problem stays open). ⚠ **Plan 55's revival trigger was NEVER satisfied as written** — it demanded 3–4 instrumented bugs with file counts, gated-surface hits and reviewer-count evidence, and **no such measurement was ever taken**; this plan was authorized by the maintainer statement plus the no-consumer finding, a weaker base, so **plan 88 must NOT be cited as proof the middle band was measured**. **Zero new gates, zero new `verify-*` check numbers, zero hard-fail validators** — plan 75's tripwire, both halves; the cold lane adds a MODE, and `close-bug` is a writer with an input precondition, which is neither. **Python is Phase 1 ONLY**; Phases 2–4 are instruction- and docs-only. **Not covered, stated so it is not assumed:** plan 87's language detector rides `artifact_helper commit-artifacts` only, so the cold lane's `wip-commit --final` code commit is **outside** it, exactly as per-task commits already are. **Phase 5 = user-driven HARD GATE, NOT run**, three known-answer anchors — happy path (one clean `fix(scope):` commit; the bug file diffed to confirm **only the three D4 fields changed**; the exact commit subject recorded as a STRING, since fork 2(i)'s `[<branch>] - <title>` is where a ratifier first sees it) · scope-change bounce (names `/devforge:research`, NO seed written anywhere, bug stays `Open` and otherwise byte-unchanged) · the three-arm offer observed in conversation (**recording whether `/devforge:report-bug`'s own pointer contradicted the rubric — that observation is D6's re-open trigger, not a nit**) — with **anchors 1 and 2 scored as a PAIR**, since a triage that bounces everything passes one and fails the other. ⚠ **A clean run is NOT evidence the lane reduces ceremony**: the friction claim rests on the 2026-08-25 maintainer statement and on nothing this phase can produce. **As of 2026-09-03 (plan 93): the flag is gone from `/devforge:fix`; 16/4.** **AMENDED 2026-09-04 by plan 95 (its D6), narrowly: D6's arm 3 gained ONE clause offering `/devforge:report-ticket` as the file-it-for-later variant; the discriminator, arms 1–2 and the three-arm single-axis shape are byte-unchanged, `/devforge:report-bug` Rule 8's reading (i) is NOT reopened, and no build record was edited.**

- `89-TEST-FOUNDATION-HARDENING-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE (build) 2026-08-27 — Phases 0–5 complete (`3521e34` Phase 0 close / `1ead52d` Phase 1 / `8f4ffec` Phases 2+2b / `27cb30e` Phase 3 / `77beba1` Phase 4, plus the Phase-5 docs commit this entry rides in; python-reviewer + instruction-reviewer both SHIP-READY; full `tests/lib` suite green — 11124+ passed, `test_breakdown_helper` 426). Phase 5 closed with BOTH halves landed: D6's note in `src/commands/configure/main.md` and the docs/reconciliation sweep (status flip, the dated build amendments, `CHANGELOG.md`, the `CLAUDE.md` index line and this entry); its Verify criterion passes — `grep -rn "REGRESSION_GATE" src/commands/` now returns hits in BOTH `verify/main.md` and `configure/main.md`. ⚠ Phase 6 consumer e2e DEFERRED 2026-08-27 by explicit maintainer decision — the maintainer INTENDS to run it (NOT waived; build-verified, NOT consumer-validated), and the five known-answer anchors recorded below are the recipe for that run, with anchors 1+2 and anchor 5's two plants each scored as a PAIR. No anchor has been run, and nothing in Phases 1–5 may be read as evidence any of them would pass.** Created 2026-08-26 on `develop-2.0-init`; the document carries no private-client identifiers and is committed normally. **Origin: a review question, not an incident.** One maintainer question on 2026-08-26 — does the framework cover the code it generates with tests — triggered an `src/` sweep, and **the sweep's split IS the plan**. Test RUNNING was already solid at three independent layers (`/devforge:implement` PHASE 5's scope-aware `verify-touched` with its helper-owned 3-iteration self-repair cap; `/devforge:verify` PHASE 4's assembled suite plus the baseline-diff regression gate; `qa-reviewer`'s AC→test mapping) and **nothing in this plan touches any of it** — a future session must not read this plan as a claim that test execution was broken. Test OBLIGATION was soft everywhere it could have stood: constitution §3.4 was a bare `{{TESTING}}` placeholder, the task-file skeleton's four standing Done-When lines had no test analog, `### Always` item 8 was *"Lint everything"* with no counterpart, and a package whose `test_command` is `null` ran no tests SILENTLY while every Done-When box still ticked. **Linting was obligated in three places and testing in one — that asymmetry is the finding.** ⚠ **There is NO consumer incident, NO failing run and NO measurement behind this plan** — a PREDICTED-GAP plan in plan 87's class, and the plan requires that this be said wherever it is summarized. **Three testing-adjacent surfaces were checked and deliberately left alone, each with its reason recorded so they are not re-opened:** AC→test-outcome mapping (`--test-anchor` is captured at `/devforge:specify` and `/devforge:verify`'s tests mode explicitly defers consuming it — **chartered to the testForge20 e2e**, not collected here); plan 86's F3 regression-net declaration (**strengthening waits on plan 86's own Phase-7 data**, which does not exist); and `qa-engineer`-only-on-a-flagged-gap (deliberate plan 09/15 design, not a hole). **Ratified decisions — Phase 0 closed 2026-08-27, D1–D7 + OQ-1–OQ-6 ALL as recommended, the five explicit picks answered in-session.** **D1** a universal `**Tests are part of the change.**` block appended to `src/constitution.md` **§3.5, NOT §3.4** — and the choice is MECHANICAL, not editorial: `_UNIVERSAL_SECTIONS` is a CLOSED eleven-entry tuple omitting `§3.4`, so `verify-universal-defaults` never looks there, while §3.5 parses as ONE whole-body rule and needed no parser change. §3.4 was declined ON THE RECORD for its zero drift detection; a new numbered §3.9 was out of bounds as Python. ⚠ Its counter-argument is ACCEPTED as a real cost, not answered — a reader hunting testing rules still meets §3.4's placeholder — and routed to **OQ-3, resolved arm (i): accept it, add nothing.** **D2** a fifth standing Done-When line `Tests pass on changed files (see Development Commands section)` in `_DONE_WHEN_FIXED_LINES` — **the plan's ONLY Python**, one list literal plus its tests — deliberately UNCONDITIONAL, the drafting brief's *"skip only when the package has no test command"* phrasing REJECTED as the escape hatch the meta-rule forbids by name. **Ordering resolved APPEND-LAST** by orchestrator ruling on three reasons (byte-stability of existing skeleton lines; no invented type-check/lint/test grouping smuggled in as formatting; house append precedent) — ⚠ recorded because the build initially inserted mid-list and reversed, so a session reading only the final diff does not re-litigate it. Blast radius **EIGHT verified sites: FIVE edited, THREE recorded no-ops** (the plan's own headline/enumeration mismatch was reconciled before Phase 0 closed — do not propagate either original number). ⚠ **`test_four_fixed_done_when_lines_verbatim` still PASSED with a fifth line — only its NAME was falsified, so a green suite was never evidence that site was handled**; it was renamed at Phase 1, and python-reviewer's finding 2 was closed in-build by a NEW durable test, `test_done_when_fixed_lines_match_storage_rules_skeleton`, which **turns the plan's manual `storage-rules.md` ↔ `_DONE_WHEN_FIXED_LINES` diff criterion into a standing regression.** **D3** a sixteenth `### Always` Key Rule, **Test behavior changes**, a PURE APPEND with items 1–15 byte-identical (plan 87 D4's precedent) — ⚠ its bound stated in the vendor's own words, *"context, not enforced configuration"*: presence in every session, compliance in none; Phase 4 had **zero test blast radius**, no test pinning the item count. **D4** arm (b), **INSTRUCTION-ONLY**: a `verify-touched` `pass` whose `test_commands_run` array is EMPTY — a field already on the wire that no spec had ever named — makes `/devforge:implement`'s approve path pass the Tests box as `--unverified-box`, leaving a durable `_(unverified — see Completion Notes)_` annotation instead of a falsely green tick. The stderr-WARN arm is the NAMED strengthening arm with an observable trigger (**the first observed run where a package with no configured test command completed a task with the box ticked**), and `_cmds_verify.py` was NOT edited. ⚠ **D4 DEPENDS ON D2 and Phase 0 took BOTH, recording the pairing in writing** — D4 alone is unbuildable, D2 alone ships a box that can read `[x]` over a run in which nothing ran. **D5** NO numeric coverage threshold at any layer — DECLINED, its counter-argument **ACCEPTED as a standing bound, not answered**: without a number "coverage" is unfalsifiable and this framework still cannot answer *"is this codebase tested?"* with evidence, so **a future plan wanting one owes a reported, NON-GATING measurement first and must argue a threshold only from the distribution it produces.** **D6** arm (b), documentation-only, SHIPPED at Phase 5: `/devforge:configure` never named `REGRESSION_GATE`, a live key with an enum (`off` / `full`), a built-in `"full"` default, a `configure_helper set-regression-gate` setter and a documented `/devforge:verify` **PHASE-4.3** consumer — **so the model running setup never surfaced it and the user never learned it existed.** The note landed at the END of **Phase 5.1 — Render config**, the site where `render-config` writes `project-config.json` and therefore the moment a reader first meets a key no phase set; it states the default, the emission, the PHASE-4.3 reader (which treats an absent value as `"full"`), the two accepted values, and the setter-plus-re-render route to change it, and it closes by forbidding a Phase-4 prompt for it. **No new wizard question, no count edits**; arm (a)'s seventh Phase-4 prompt was declined and remains the recorded escalation. ⚠ **A live number checked while writing the note CONFIRMS fact 11 rather than contradicting it**: the file's Phase-5 preamble says `configure.yaml` is *"fully populated (29 fields set)"* while `_configure/_schema.py`'s `FIELD_SCHEMA` holds **30** — the difference is exactly `regression_gate` (23 set in Phase 3 + 6 in Phase 4 = 29, and the 30th is set by no phase), so **the documented gap is visible in the file's own arithmetic** and the note cites that split rather than introducing a competing count. **D7** (ADDED 2026-08-26 by maintainer directive — the obligation must not be satisfiable by tests written merely so tests exist, nor by invented ones; RATIFIED IN FULL with **clause 1 KEPT**) three FALSIFIABLE clauses appended to D1's block and mirrored in `qa-reviewer`, **each naming an observable SHAPE and never a quality** — the words *meaningful* / *reasonable* / *adequate* appear in no emitted text: expected-value provenance **scoped inline to the changed behavior** (running the implementation and recording its output is not a derivation; **a test pinning existing behavior the change does not alter is explicitly outside the clause**, so characterization tests before a restructuring stay legitimate — the scope is carried in the emitted sentence itself, which is what let clause 1 be kept rather than dropped); *"for every test there is a change to the code under test that makes it fail"* with **THREE named vacuous shapes** (a mock's own configured return asserted back; a literal or just-assigned value asserted against itself; a call asserting nothing about result, raised error or observable effect); and no restating what the type checker already proves. **DEFENCE IN DEPTH FOR D2, not an independent idea — the checkbox is a target and a vacuous test ticks it** (the same shape as plan 82's D8 with plan 81's F3: admission at authoring, refusal at review). The detection layer is a lettered **Phase 2b** that **EXTENDS** `qa-reviewer`'s pre-existing *"Flag weak, vacuous, or implementation-coupled assertions"* sentence at Approach step 4 plus Rule 3 — ⚠ **so what shipped is FALSIFIABILITY, not the concept: that agent already said "vacuous" and left it undefined**, and a record claiming the check is new has misdescribed the diff. No new section, no new agent, no `tools:` change, the fenced `yaml` meta-block byte-identical (which is why no claude-code-guide pass was owed), and the vocabulary still exactly `ADEQUATE / GAPS FOUND`. **`src/agents/qa-engineer.md` is a VERIFIED NO-OP** — its philosophy already carries four of the clauses, and the one it lacks belongs in the constitution anyway because fact 15's inline tests are the per-engineer MAJORITY path that agent is never dispatched on, while the panel hands `qa-reviewer` every test in the change regardless of author. **OQ-6** floors a clause-2 finding at **≥ High, and High forces `GAPS FOUND`**; clauses 1 and 3 deliberately take NO floor (a type-restating assertion is redundant rather than false; a provenance violation can be a legitimate characterization test). ⚠ Its counter-argument is accepted as the cost: a contested call re-fans the whole four-reviewer panel and can burn its three-round cap. **⚠ THE HONEST BOUNDS, which ratification did NOT strengthen.** **NOTHING MECHANICAL CHECKS meaningfulness** — the check is an LLM reviewer judging LLM-written tests, and **weaker than plan 86's F3 in one specific way: F3's reader is a human opening a task file, D7's is a model in an autonomous loop**; clause 2's *"there is a change that makes it fail"* is a counterfactual the reviewer reasons about rather than executes. **Vacuous tests do not become impossible and no phase measured how many get through.** **Mutation testing** (`mutmut` / `Stryker` / `cargo-mutants`) is the MECHANICAL answer to clause 2 and is **deliberately NOT built** — recorded as the NAMED strengthening path with a trigger (the first observed vacuous test matching a clause-2 shape that passed a `qa-reviewer` panel), never as a refusal. **Zero new gates, zero `verify-*` numbers, zero validators, zero new agents, zero new verdict values** — plan 75's tripwire, both halves. **Build findings recorded 2026-08-27, each an amendment to the plan rather than a silent fix:** the fact-4a paragraph in `implement/main.md` **contradicted D4 in TWO sentences, not the one the plan named** (its closing *"On a clean `pass`, pass NO `--unverified-box` arguments"* AND an opening enumeration asserting a clean `pass` confirms ALL conditions) — **a build that greps only for the closing sentence passes its own Verify while shipping the contradiction**; a **THIRD site at `:36`** was falsified by OMISSION and gained the second-trigger clause, while `:321` and the `:165`-area bullets are verified no-ops; two pre-existing drive-by stalenesses were fixed in-band and logged as such rather than as scope creep (`_implement/_cli.py:83`'s `verify-touched` help string, which contradicted its own module docstring and had been stale since plan 17's Phase 2, gained `+ test`; `references/agent-brief.md:13` gained `+ tests`); `/devforge:fix` needs **no D4 mirror**, verified — it has no `scope-and-approve` path; and Phase 2b's Verify wording *"the same two tokens in the same places"* was corrected to *"the same two-token vocabulary"*, having become count-false when OQ-6's floor legitimately added a third inline USAGE of the existing `GAPS FOUND` token. **⚠ CONSUMER-INSTALL DRIFT IS EXPECTED, BY DESIGN AND NOT A REGRESSION**: canonical §3.5 gained a rule, so `constitute_helper verify-universal-defaults` reports a §3.5 mismatch against any install whose `.devforge/constitute.json` predates this build and the WARN-only update-time drift check fires — **and shipping in the same release as plan 86 shows consumers TWO drift findings, §3.5 and §3.6, not one.** Back-porting into shipped installs is an explicit NON-GOAL. **Plans 86 and 87 were CHECKED at Phase 5 and neither is edited — both no-ops recorded as deliberate:** plan 86's F3 governs behavior-PRESERVING surfaces at decomposition while D7's clauses bind tests asserting CHANGED behavior and clause 1 explicitly excludes F3's subject, **so the two are disjoint by construction rather than by luck**; plan 87's advisory-WARN precedent is cited by D4's arm (a), which was not taken. **Coordination with plan 90** (drafted the same day; execution order 89 → 90, and **plan 89 shipped first, so plan 90 owes the LIVE re-read**): THREE surfaces shared unconditionally — `src/CLAUDE.md`'s `### Always` list, `src/devforge/storage-rules.md`'s Done-When skeleton, and ⚠ **the newest and least obvious, added 2026-08-26 by D7**, `src/agents/qa-reviewer.md`, where this plan's Phase 2b edited Approach step 4 + Rule 3 while plan 90 edits a DIFFERENT sentence beside step 6 — **different sentences in one file, so the hazard is a CLOBBERED EDIT on a stale read, not a contradiction** (step 6 was verified byte-unchanged by this build) — plus `src/agents/qa-engineer.md` as a CONDITIONAL FOURTH that **did NOT join**, OQ-3 having resolved to arm (i). ⚠ **`D7` is an overloaded token across the pair: plan 89's D7 is test-meaningfulness, plan 90's D7 is a wall-clock cost line — every cross-reference must name the plan.** **Phase 6 = user-driven consumer e2e HARD GATE, NOT run**, FIVE known-answer anchors: configured-test-command task (box ticked clean) · `null`-test-command task (box UNTICKED and annotated, the exact line recorded as a STRING) · the §3.5 drift finding, with the helper's exact finding kind recorded · the Key Rule shipped and loaded, **whose part (b) rests on a Claude Code tool-behavior claim and owes a `claude-code-guide` check BEFORE the run**, part (a) scored independently · and **a planted vacuous test beside a genuine one in the same task**, recording the finding's severity and the returned verdict token as STRINGS. **Anchors 1+2 are scored as a PAIR, and so are anchor 5's two plants** — a reviewer that catches the plant by flagging everything fails the other half — and **anchor 5 is the ONLY place D7's false-positive rate will ever be observed; nothing shipped here has measured it.** ⚠ **A clean Phase 6 would be evidence the obligation is present and the no-test case is visible — never evidence that generated code is better tested**, since this plan measures no coverage at all.

- `90-E2E-TEST-LANE-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE (build) 2026-08-27 — Phases 0–5 complete (`1a06463` Phase 0 close / `c1718ee` Phase 1 / `9cee99c` Phase 2 / `182f514` Phase 3 / `d4d7928` Phase 4, plus the Phase-5 docs commit this entry rides in — Phase 1 landed fourth, the phases being independent, so the chain reads `1a06463` → `9cee99c` → `182f514` → `c1718ee` → `d4d7928`). ⚠ Phase 6 consumer e2e DEFERRED 2026-08-27 by explicit maintainer decision — the maintainer INTENDS to run it (NOT waived) — user-driven HARD GATE, NOT run: build-verified, NOT consumer-validated, and the lane has never been observed end to end. Its three known-answer anchors below are that run's recipe, with anchors 1 and 2 scored as a PAIR and anchor 1 obligated to record the wall-clock delta as a NUMBER.** Created 2026-08-26 on `develop-2.0-init`; the document carries no private-client identifiers and is committed normally. **Ships SECOND of the 89/90 pair, so this plan owed the LIVE re-read of every shared surface — and did it.** **Origin: a question and a sweep, not an incident.** One maintainer question on 2026-08-26 — are end-to-end tests needed in the emitted pipeline, and what would adding them take — triggered an `src/` sweep whose three findings are the plan. (1) The emitted instruction set contained **no web-e2e concept at all**: a `playwright|cypress|selenium|puppeteer|webdriver` grep over `src/**/*.md` returned nothing, and the only E2E vocabulary anywhere was mobile-only and advisory, in `qa-engineer`'s `## Mobile Testing` bullet and its `qa-reviewer` step-6 mirror. (2) **There was no test-tier taxonomy to hang one on** — one `test_command` per package plus a flat `TEST_COMMANDS`, with nothing distinguishing unit from integration from e2e; **this is the plan's central structural problem.** (3) e2e detection already existed in `init_helper`, was **never run by any command spec**, and **could not have been used if it were** (its only reader filters bucket values through a schema-valid set that excludes playwright and cypress) — write-only end to end, and **left exactly that way by this build.** ⚠ **There is NO incident behind this plan and none is claimed** — a PREDICTED-GAP plan in plan 87's class, and it must be described that way wherever it is summarized. **The rejected alternative, recorded with its reasoning so it is not re-proposed:** putting the e2e suite in `test_command` or `TEST_COMMANDS[0]`, declined on two independent CORRECTNESS grounds rather than on cost — it would run per task under `/devforge:implement` and be re-paid up to three more times by the helper-owned self-repair cap, and it is broken by construction in `regression-gate`'s baseline half, a detached worktree with nothing built and nothing served, where an app-dependent suite fails for reasons unrelated to the feature and **the gate then reads its own failure as `baseline-failing` and stops gating — a gate that silently disarms itself is worse than no gate.** **Ratified decisions — Phase 0 closed 2026-08-27, D1–D8 and OQ-1–OQ-7 all answered, four explicit picks made in-session.** **D1** one top-level `E2E_COMMAND` key (`e2e_command` in `configure.yaml`), whose load-bearing constraint is that it stays **INVISIBLE to `verify-touched` and to `regression-gate`** — a mechanical consequence, since both read only `PACKAGE_STACKS[].test_command` and `TEST_COMMANDS[0]`, not a promise. ⚠ **Its seeding fork was picked and does NOT match the decision's drafted sentence:** `/devforge:configure` composes the default from the `MANIFESTS_JSON` manifests it already parses, so **`init_helper` is untouched, its detector stays unreachable from every command, and `.devforge/init.yaml`'s `test_infra.e2e` is read by nothing this build produced** — honest rather than tidy. The per-package arm (b) stays the recorded widening path; arm (c), no key at all, stays rejected. **D2** the declaration is plan-side: architect **sub-question 12** feeding a conditional `### E2E Scenarios` subsection, carried to `/devforge:breakdown` with **ZERO Python** by reading the table from `plan.md` directly, exactly as the change-induced dead-code table already is. It gives `architect.md`'s `qa-engineer` consult row its first consumer. ⚠ **Its honest bound is in the emitted bracket text: nothing checks the declaration — the claim is visibility, not enforcement**, the same claim in the same words plan 86's `Regression net:` line makes. **D3** the execution seat is a new fail-soft `/devforge:verify` **PHASE 4.5** running the suite ONCE at HEAD in the real `source_root` — never a worktree, never the merge-base, never a second run — always exiting 0, with statuses `off` / `inconclusive` / `e2e-clean` / `e2e-failing`. **Fork (i) ratified: NO status gates the verdict in v1**, and the reporting sub-question resolved **reasons-only** (`compute-verdict` gains an `--e2e` input adding one `reasons` line and NO blocker; `render-report` unchanged; `verification.md` gains no dedicated section — the shape the regression gate already has). ⚠ **The strongest counter-argument against this plan is ACCEPTED, not answered: a lane that runs a suite, watches it go red and approves the feature anyway prints a green verdict beside a red suite in the same run.** The strengthening trigger is named and is an OBSERVATION — the first e2e-catchable defect that reaches a consumer DESPITE an `e2e-failing` status having been surfaced — and **a build that flips the arm on reasoning has left this plan.** **D4** NO seventh PHASE-3.5 gate: the sequence stays at six and **plan 75's tripwire holds in both halves**; `verify-e2e-coverage`'s shape and its OBSERVED-dropped-scenario trigger are recorded, not built. **D5** a dedicated `qa-engineer` task under the Agent Assignment table's existing test-authoring row (**no new row**, which would create two rows matching one task), scenarios riding free-form `## Change Details` with **NO flag and NO header field**, plus an escape-hatch-free framework rule (write in the framework the configuration names; empty configuration ⇒ the task is BLOCKED and says so; never a framework the project has not adopted) and — **both forks picked** — the **dedicated-e2e-directory placement rule**, with the runner-config exclude arm reaching NO emitted text, and section arm **(a)**, rename and widen. ⚠ Two costs accepted, not answered: **consumer-merge friction** from renaming a shipped agent file, and **nothing checks the placement rule**, so a misplaced e2e spec produces a confusing red run at the next `/devforge:implement` task rather than an error. **D6** the SUITE owns the application lifecycle — the framework starts nothing, waits on nothing, tears down nothing — and `ac_runtime_url` / `ac_runtime_api_base` / `ac_runtime_cli_command` stay a **separate, uncoupled channel** PHASE 4.5 neither reads nor writes; a future unification belongs in `ac_runtime_cli_command`, never in `E2E_COMMAND`. **D7** the wall-clock cost line plan 85's D7 makes obligatory: **ONE suite run per `/devforge:verify` run** — not per task, not per commit, not doubled — bounded by `off` costing a config read, by the advisory arm never triggering a re-run cycle, and by a timeout being `inconclusive` rather than a hang. ⚠ **The cost line is STRUCTURAL and carries no number, which is exactly the gap plan 85's D7 was written to close** — accepted and not argued away, because plan 70's Phase-2 numbers do not exist; **Phase 6's anchor 1 is the first datum this plan family will ever have, and it is owed even if it turns out uninteresting.** **D8** a greenfield or no-e2e-infra install is a clean SILENT no-op at every seat: the field is seeded empty and nothing is asked, the plan subsection is omitted heading-and-table, `/devforge:breakdown` writes no table, no task and **no "no e2e scenarios" line anywhere**, and `/devforge:verify` reports `off` with no user-facing note and no report line. ⚠ The counter-argument is accepted: a web project that genuinely should have e2e coverage is never told, and the reply is sub-question 12, which fires on the ACs rather than on the config. **OQ answers:** no CI claim, and the framework writes and reads no CI files (OQ-1); the mobile text survives BYTE-IDENTICAL in both agents (OQ-2); **the AGENT runs the suite while writing it and the HELPER does not** — no new flag, no `verify-touched` branch, no per-task e2e execution — bounded by D5's placement rule, so *"the per-task path is unchanged"* is true of the CONFIG KEY by construction and of the FILES only by authoring discipline, **two different kinds of true this build does not merge** (OQ-3); flake handling explicitly OUT OF SCOPE and **load-bearing for D3** — a future plan proposing to gate on `e2e-failing` owes a flake policy in the same document (OQ-4); a separate timeout constant defaulted higher than `_regression._TEST_TIMEOUT`, never an import of it, and a timeout produces `inconclusive`, never `e2e-failing` (OQ-5); the result reaches `/devforge:summarize` for free through `verification.md`'s reasons, with no `summarize_helper` change, no `/devforge:finalize` change, no widening of `/devforge:verify`'s frontmatter `description` and **no plan-63 13/7 delta** (OQ-6); `E2E_COMMAND` over a tier-neutral name, on consistency with the only test-tier vocabulary the codebase already uses (OQ-7). **What each phase built.** **Phase 1 (Python)** — `e2e_command` across the six-file config surface: `FIELD_SCHEMA` (30 → **31**), a `FIELD_DEFAULTS` baseline of `""` rather than a copied `_AC_RUNTIME_FIELDS`-style conditional exemption (**`e2e_command` has no analogous discriminator, so inventing one would have meant inventing a gating field this plan does not create** — and a non-`None` default keeps it out of `_cmds_verify.py`'s null-scalar loop with no exemption at all), the `set-e2e-command` setter and its verb registration, `E2E_COMMAND` appended LAST to `_PROJECT_CONFIG_KEY_ORDER` (38 → **39**), and the `AC verification` display group per the `regression_gate` sibling precedent rather than a new single-field group. **Phase 2 (instruction-only)** — sub-question 12 in the voice sub-questions 8 and 9 already use, with EXPLICIT empty answers (`no full-stack-only flows`), and the conditional subsection in its neighbours' bracketed-conditional shape whose text states the no-gate bound; **two extra same-file consistency edits were ruled in by the orchestrator as cross-check discipline** (the architect-authored-list entry and the approval-summary line), and a reviewer's sub-question-8-idiom nit was skipped in favour of matching the file's dominant form. **Phase 3 (instruction-only)** — PHASE 0a.5's read-from-`plan.md` sub-block modelled on the dead-code paragraph, PHASE 3's `### E2E scenario tasks (declared full-stack flows)` carrying the framework rule and the ONE ratified placement arm, `qa-engineer.md`'s section renamed **`## Mobile Testing` → `## End-to-End Testing`** with a web block beside the mobile block (**every mobile bullet byte-identical**), and `qa-reviewer.md`'s step-6 web mirror. ⚠ **Build finding: the plan's fact 3 claim that `## Mobile Testing` occurs *"exactly once repo-wide"* was `src/`-scoped truth** — a repo-wide grep also hits the maintainer ledgers, whose mentions are HISTORICAL; the Verify criterion's zero-stale-references reading is `src/`-scoped. **Phase 4 (Python + instruction)** — `verify_helper e2e-gate` over a new `_verify/_e2e.py` built in `_regression.py`'s always-exit-0 mould, the `--e2e` reasons-only fold, and `verify/main.md`'s `### 4.5` plus its `$WORKDIR/e2e.json` scratch entry and PHASE-4 heading-line update. ⚠ **Its build findings are recorded rather than smoothed:** a semantics FIX distinguishing a missing-or-corrupt config (`inconclusive`) from a cleanly-parsed config whose key is absent or blank (`off`), so `off` means *"nobody configured a suite"* and never *"the config could not be read"*; shell exit codes 126/127 documented and test-pinned as an accepted misclassification bound; a wrapper-mode test added for `_e2e` only, with the `_regression` sibling's absence of one recorded as PRE-EXISTING and NOT fixed; capture-all subprocess output accepted per the sibling's precedent; and the deliberate absence of an Important-rules entry, since the two that exist are there because those checks GATE. **Phase 5 (instruction + docs)** — the `/devforge:configure` **COMPOSER**, which is the half without which the field renders, is settable, and stays `""` forever (**D1's rejected alternative (c) reached by omission**): three sites in `configure/main.md` — a Phase-2 compose bullet inheriting `test_command`'s *"do NOT invent an ecosystem-default guess"* rule verbatim (a detected runner with no root-invocable script yields `""`, and a dependency alone never composes `npx playwright test`), a Phase-3 echo-template line, and the setter-mapping row — plus the count reconciliation. ⚠ **The counts were COUNTED from the live tuples and never incremented**, which is what the plan's Trap 6 demands: 30 fields populated by that command (24 detected + 6 prompted) out of a **31**-field `FIELD_SCHEMA`, `configure.yaml`'s canonical state at 31, and **39** `project-config.json` keys = 31 + 5 from `init.yaml` + 3 derived; **plan 89's D6 `REGRESSION_GATE` note was UPDATED IN PLACE, never removed** (23 → 24 detection-derived, 29 → 30 populated, 30 → 31 schema), with its `regression_gate` guidance and its no-Phase-4-prompt instruction verbatim. ⚠ **Two build findings from the composer, recorded and NOT repaired:** `read-manifests` folds a single-list manifest into `dependencies` and leaves `dev_dependencies` empty, so a rule reading only `dev_dependencies` would have found nothing on real installs — the shipped bullet reads the same union `build_tool_hint` and `framework_hint` already read; and **there is no route from a set `e2e_command` back to `""` except `reset`**, a family-wide property of the scalar setters recorded as an open question about the composer's overwrite semantics rather than fixed here. **`src/CLAUDE.md`** gained ONE three-sentence `### Verification` note (feature-level, ADVISORY, silent when unconfigured) under plan 08's trim discipline, with the `### Always` list **counted live at SIXTEEN and left byte-unchanged — this plan added no item**. **`src/devforge/storage-rules.md` was read and deliberately NOT edited**, recorded as a finding rather than a silence: that file mirrors the task-file header fields plus the Done-When skeleton (counted live at five standing lines, plan 89's fifth intact), and plan 86's `Regression net:` and Two-hats prose lanes are equally absent from it, so an e2e mention would have been the first of its kind. **The 89/90 coordination rule was honoured on the READ, not on a pre-computed diff:** all three shared surfaces were opened live and plan 89's edits confirmed intact after this plan's Phase 3 — `qa-reviewer.md`'s Approach step 4 and Rule 3 survived byte-identical beside this plan's step-6 edit, both agent files' fenced `yaml` meta-blocks are byte-unchanged, the `### Always` list stayed at sixteen, and the Done-When skeleton stayed at five — and plan 89's **OQ-3 resolved to arm (i)**, so `src/agents/qa-engineer.md` never joined the shared set and this plan was its only editor. **Plans 34, 44 and 87 were CHECKED and none is touched — the no-op is deliberate**: they are the ADVISORY/WARN family D3's stance joins, and nothing in this build falsifies a sentence in any of them. Plans **66** and **86** each gained one dated `## Dependencies + related` bullet: 66 because this lane reuses its declaration SHAPE (sub-question → conditional `plan.md` subsection → dedicated `qa-engineer` task) and deliberately does NOT reuse its gate, 86 because its F3 no-gate declaration precedent is what D2 and D4 rest on. ⚠ **THE HONEST BOUNDS, unchanged by ratification and by the build: v1 GATES NOTHING** (no status reaches the verdict; the fold adds one advisory line and never a blocker), **NOTHING CHECKS THE DECLARATION** (no gate, no validator — a declared scenario covered by no task produces no error), **NOTHING CHECKS THE PLACEMENT RULE** (a misplaced spec surfaces as a confusing red run, not an error), **the lane is SILENT by default**, **the run is LOCAL and makes no CI claim**, and **the cost line carries no number.** **Phase 6 = user-driven consumer e2e HARD GATE, NOT run**, three known-answer anchors: the declared happy path on an install with a real Playwright suite (an `### E2E Scenarios` subsection, a `qa-engineer` task naming those scenarios, the agent running the suite during that task, and PHASE 4.5 reporting **`e2e-clean`** — **also recording where the e2e spec files LANDED and whether any later `verify-touched` run picked one up, which is the only place D5's runner collision can be observed, and the wall-clock delta as a NUMBER**);
