- `91-FEATURE-DIR-IDENTITY-AND-PROVENANCE-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE (build) 2026-09-03 — Phases 1, 1b, 2, 3, 4 and 5 BUILT, Phase 5's Verify MET 2026-08-31, and the last residual left by the 2026-08-31 ruling CLOSED 2026-09-03 (`70656d4`). Phase 6 consumer e2e DEFERRED TO POST-RELEASE by maintainer decision 2026-09-03 — user-driven HARD GATE, NOT run.** ⚠ **That deferral is a TIMING decision and NOT a waiver**: the maintainer runs Phase 6 after the release rather than before the build is closed, so *"done"* here means built and build-verified, **never that Phase 6 passed** — nothing in this plan is consumer-validated, and a deferral is not a pass. Phase 1 landed **2026-08-28** (18 commits), Phase 1b **2026-08-29** (7 commits), then Phase 2 (2 commits, `0dae1e2` / `2560b1a`), Phase 3 (12 commits, `b681bae`…`5ebe5f4`), Phase 4 (3 commits, `4c63ae4` / `153f3a9` / `7e986a1`) and Phase 5 (beginning `42467be`, plus the closure commits its own record names), all on `develop-2.0-init`. ⚠ **Phases 2–5 carry a DATELESS `BUILT` marker in the plan file and that is deliberate, not an omission** — those four were recorded on 2026-08-31 from a commit list without reading the commits' own dates, so all that is established is the window **after Phase 1b (2026-08-29) and no later than 2026-08-31**; a session that opens `git log` should date them and replace the markers, and nobody should date them by guess. **Every built phase is BUILD-VERIFIED ONLY — NOT consumer-validated**: no install has been run against the new layout, the ticket gate or the provenance stamp, so no shipped behaviour here may be read as confirmed by use. Created 2026-08-26 on `develop-2.0-init`; the document carries no private-client identifiers. **Origin: a maintainer design conversation on 2026-08-26 — no incident, and none claimed.** Two problems were raised together and are deliberately kept separate throughout: *identity* (a feature directory's name carries no meaning outside this repo — `NNN` is a repo-local counter, and the framework already scrapes `[A-Z]+-[0-9]+` out of a source branch in wrapper mode but never asks for one) and *volume* (a flat `specs/` accumulates unboundedly — ~200 sibling directories at a realistic ~8 features/month over two years). A third consequence drove F3: provenance is git-only, so an artifact pasted into a PR, a ticket comment or Obsidian carries nothing saying who ran the command that produced it. **The rejected alternative and the invariant it produced, recorded so they are not re-litigated:** keeping `specs/` flat and having `/devforge:finalize` move completed features into `specs/archive/` was rejected on the maintainer's own objection — *features that die mid-pipeline never reach `/devforge:finalize`, so an archive driven by a terminal command collects the completed work (already orderly) and leaves the abandoned work (the actual mess) in place* — generalized into the rule binding every decision below: **placement is decided at creation time, by a mechanism that already has to run, and never changes afterwards.** Intake allocation is such a mechanism; a finalize-time move is not, **so this plan has no archive step and one must not be re-proposed without answering that objection.** **Three changes, in a ratified order (D1).** **F1** — the feature-directory path becomes OPAQUE: the helper authors the layout and command prose receives a path instead of composing one. **F2** — identity becomes the ticket, the layout becomes `specs/<YYYY>/<MM>/<leaf>/`, and `REQUIRE_TICKET` gates intake. **F3** — a `Run by:` provenance line on the four artifacts that travel outside the repository. F1 came first so the prose sweep is paid **once** rather than twice; ⚠ that is the whole argument for the ordering and **it is NOT a claim that F1 made the layout switch cheap** — under either ordering Phase 3 still had to teach six resolvers a variable-depth walk returning both shapes. **Phase 0 CLOSED 2026-08-28**, and how it closed matters: the ratifying act was a **single one-word blanket directive** at the end of a session in which the recommendations were presented and argued, and **every item — D1–D9 plus OQ-1–OQ-8 — ratified AS RECOMMENDED, with D1 = (a)**. The close record names three specific ways that is weaker than an item-by-item ratification and preserves the pre-close text unrewritten so the record of what was skipped survives. **The two missing deliberations were both PERFORMED on 2026-08-29 and both decisions UPHELD, so none is outstanding — but they stand on unequal footings and that difference is the most important thing this entry carries.** **D4 stands narrowly on its own merits**: it is the one place this plan deliberately breaks half of plan 75's tripwire — `REQUIRE_TICKET=true` **is** a new unnumbered hard-fail validator, firing at intake, the user's very first pipeline action — and it survives because it is opt-in with a `false` default (OQ-1), because intake is the cheapest possible failure point, and because **no PHASE-3.5 `verify-*` gate number is added**, so the tripwire's first half holds completely. Its strongest objection is recorded as accepted, not answered: **the rule buys discipline, not verification** — the framework has no tracker integration, so `PROJ-0000` satisfies it with fake traceability — and the deliberation created two obligations Phase 2 discharged (say so in the consumer's own words; name both routes out of the refusal). **D6 stands on a WEAKER footing: it is ENTAILED by D2 and D3, not argued on its merits.** Retiring `NNN` never came up in the originating conversation and was not argued in the ratifying session either, over what the plan itself calls its single largest correctness risk. It holds because once the leaf is the ticket and nothing else (D3) inside a `YYYY/MM` tree (D2), `NNN` has nowhere left to live — **so reversing D6 alone is incoherent; it would mean reopening the layout, which is the part that actually was argued.** Its case-against survives the decision: two live shapes forever is a permanent tax **worse than the plan-68-D3 precedent it leans on** (there the legacy dirs were *inert*; here both shapes stay live in the same install with no end date), and **coexistence is FORCED, not preferred** — the only alternative is migrating existing installs, which that precedent rejects and nobody requested. All three reversal windows are now SPENT: D1's closed when Phase 1 landed, D4's when Phase 2 began, D6's when Phase 3 began. **What each phase built.** **Phase 1** — the resolution accessor (`specs_root_for` / `iter_feature_dirs` / `find_feature_dirs_with` in `_shared/feature_alloc.py`), carrying the **two-arm variable-depth walk from the start** even though only the legacy arm could return anything then, with all six depth-1 resolvers migrated onto it in the same commit so the depth assumption lives in one function instead of six; `allocate_feature_dir` gained `relative_path`, which the build made **canonical for path ARGUMENTS and for anything written into an artifact**, demoting the absolute `path` to user-facing messages because it leaks the local filesystem layout into committed text; and the prose sweep across 26 command-spec files. **Result: 388 `specs/` occurrences → 69 across 17 files**, every survivor in one of four named classes — (a) a bare `specs/` naming no child, (b) the discovery-glob class, (c) a transcription of a literal the helper composes, (d) `specs/` meaning the artifact root. ⚠ **Its Verify was AMENDED rather than met as written**: the clause *"nothing that instructs the model to compose a path"* was unmet by design, the sweep having found a class the plan did not anticipate — globs the **orchestrator runs itself** — deferred to Phase 1b. The sweep's defect harvest found nine pre-existing defects, and **three of them share one cause**: `/devforge:review`, `/devforge:spec-check` and `/devforge:summarize` each emitted `specs/specs/<dir>/file` because a token named `<feature>` was defined as the full path and authors kept prepending the root anyway — **the strongest argument for the `<feature_dir>` spelling, and the lesson is about the NAME, not about the three sites.** **Phase 1b** — the discovery-glob class, closed by **ONE shared verb on `artifact_helper find-feature-artifacts`** rather than the per-consumer verbs the plan scoped, and the refinement is what makes it safe: `artifact_helper` is **nobody's producing helper**, so it preserves the decoupling property every seed-reading block was written for (each says to parse matched JSON inline so the block stays valid even if the producing command is removed), which a verb on a producing helper would have destroyed. Eleven command specs migrated (`audit`, `discover`, `finalize`, `fix`, `plan`, `research`, `review`, `spec-check`, `specify`, `summarize`, `verify` — ⚠ **not** `breakdown` and `grill`, which call `artifact_helper` for `commit-artifacts` only, so that helper has **thirteen** command-spec callers while this verb has eleven). **Result: 69 → 45 occurrences across 14 files.** ⚠ **Exactly ONE orchestrator-run glob deliberately survives** — `/devforge:specify` Phase 0.5's `<feature_dir>/*-seed.json` — and the non-migration is the CORRECT outcome, not a miss: the verb walks every feature directory and carries no narrowing flag, while that block exists precisely to scope its lookup to ONE resolved directory and **forbids** globbing `specs/*/` project-wide; routing it through the verb would perform, via a helper, the exact enumeration the block forbids, converting a mechanical guarantee into a model-obeyed filter. It is not a Phase-3 hazard — rooted at the opaque `<feature_dir>` token, it carries no depth assumption to break. ⚠ **Phase 1b also fixed a REGRESSION Phase 1 introduced, and the lesson generalizes past this plan.** A prose-only token substitution changed a Python classifier's input from relative to absolute: `source_origin_for_path` classifies on `p.startswith("specs/")`, so `record-input-read` began tagging `context` instead of `research`/`discover`, falsifying three sentences in `specify/main.md` and meaning **Phase 3's discover pre-seed could never have fired**. **THREE full green suites did not notice, because nothing covered that classification at all** — a green suite proves only that what is covered did not break. It was fixed **at the cause** (the classifier was wrong on its own terms; the bug predates the sweep, which merely started exercising it), not by putting the composed path back, which would have hidden the defect and undone the thing Phase 1 exists to remove. **Phase 2** — `REQUIRE_TICKET` joined `_configure/_render.py`'s emitted key order with a `set-require-ticket` setter and a `FIELD_DEFAULTS` baseline of `"false"`; the reader `read_require_ticket` fails **OPEN** on every unreadable/absent/malformed case (the deliberate match for "opt-in imposes nothing when off"); `normalize_ticket` validates `TICKET_RE = ^[A-Z]+-[0-9]+$`. **Lowercase is REJECTED, not folded, and that is a decision** — OQ-2's *"normalized at allocation"* would also be satisfied by auto-uppercasing, but OQ-2 lists lowercase among the inputs that must **fail cleanly**, and rejection closes the macOS case-collision hazard **by construction** (only one canonical spelling can ever pass, so no second spelling survives to collide) while coercion would bake a value the operator never typed into a directory name and a git branch. The `/devforge:configure` question ships OQ-1's wrapper preference as a **`(Recommended)` marker that moves with `INIT_JSON.workspace_mode`**, not as a pre-selected answer — the strongest form that question shape allows, recorded as what it is. ⚠ **The attach-mode no-op (OQ-5) is STRUCTURAL, not tested**: `allocate_feature_dir` is fresh-allocation-only and an attach-mode re-entry never calls it, so read that as "no code path exists to test", never as "the no-op was verified". **Phase 3** — the layout switch, and the largest risk in the plan. `allocate_feature_dir` composes `specs/<YYYY>/<MM>/<leaf>/` (its result gained `relative_path` / `ticket` / `year` / `month` / `leaf`, while `number` / `formatted_number` / `dirname` are **ABSENT, not emitted empty**); `decide_branch_action` emits `spec/<TICKET>`; `next_spec_number` is no longer reached and `SPEC_NUMBER_DIR_RE` is demoted to legacy-read; the six resolvers read both shapes by inheriting Phase 1's accessor rather than re-implementing it; `_specify/_render.py`'s `.format()` templates are **demoted to a legacy arm rather than deleted**, which is the correct outcome under read-both-write-one; the pre-commit hook's `-maxdepth 2` became `-maxdepth 4` (⚠ under the new layout a `design-manifest.json` sits at depth 4, so the hook **had silently stopped gating** — a gate that disarms itself); and D6's one obligation was discharged, the **nine "lowest-numbered feature" sites re-stated** to describe what resolution actually does on a mixed install (legacy dirs drain in `NNN` order first, then bucketed dirs in allocation-date order, because `_feature_sort_key`'s stable re-sort ties every bucketed leaf at `2**31`) rather than deleting the phrase — `grep -rn 'lowest-numbered' src/` now returns 5 lines across 3 files, all task-level, and **task numbering inside `tasks/` was never in scope**. **Phase 3 surfaced FOUR defects the plan did not predict, three of them silent stops rather than failures** — the hook above; `/devforge:specify` would have written specs into an unrelated FRESH directory because `import-handoff` matched `SPEC_NUMBER_DIR_RE` against the handoff's containing directory name alone (closed by a fourth, **bucketed** Step-4.1 path plus `classify_feature_dir_identity`); `write-design-anchor` and `finalize-handoff` hard-failed, both having composed their output path from `{specs_root}/{spec_number}-{feature_slug}/` (closed by `resolve_bucketed_feature_dir`, which re-derives the SAME directory the prose already picked from `state["source"]["handoff_path"]`); and the cold arm could not resolve its own directory, never having set that field (closed by a second, narrower writer, `record-handoff-path`). **Seven refusals to fabricate an identity are recorded together as one discipline applied seven times** — no synthetic `LOCAL-1` ticket, no invented `spec_number`, no ticket smuggled into `feature_slug`, no dead `number`/`dirname` keys emitted empty, no branch composed from nothing, no guessed feature directory, and **no number in a commit label** — because in every case the cheaper path would have produced a plausible-looking value no operator ever supplied, which is D9's *"a fake value is worse than no value"* generalized past the field D9 states it for. **Phase 4** — `Run by:` on the four travelling artifacts (`spec.md` and `research-report.md` are Python-rendered; `plan.md` and `summary.md` are LLM-authored and took prose instructions), with **D8 satisfied by REPLACEMENT** — `grep -rn 'Author.*Claude + User' src/` returns nothing, so the constant that named nobody is gone rather than sitting beside the new line. The shared owner is `_shared/provenance.py`, whose `capture_git_user_name` reads `git config user.name` and **nothing else** — never `user.email` — because D9's bound is that nothing but the configured name reaches the artifact. **OQ-8 option (i) exactly: NO new config key** — the gate reads the SAME `AI_ATTRIBUTION` answer `COMMIT_ATTRIBUTION` already derives from, and ⚠ **it fails CLOSED, the deliberate opposite of `read_require_ticket`'s fail-open**: a missing `REQUIRE_TICKET` must not impose friction nobody asked for, whereas an unreadable config must not let a human name leak into a public committed artifact by default — the plan-87 / plan-78 weighing D9 demanded, resolved toward not leaking. OQ-7 ratified "keep the original", so task 6 was in scope and `render_spec` stopped being a pure function of `state`; for `/devforge:research` the same behaviour rides an explicit optional `--existing-path`, because research state carries no field from which the helper could derive the report's own directory. ⚠ **The TWO build-time records that were open are CLOSED, ruled 2026-08-31, so no decision and no OQ in this plan is outstanding. (1) The D3 divergence is RATIFIED** — the build applies `leaf = ticket if ticket else slug` for both the directory leaf and the branch name, and **that rule was not literally in D3 as it then read**: D3's text said the leaf is the ticket ONLY and was **silent** on the ticketless case. That silence was read as **room, not as a ban**, because OQ-1 ratified a `false` default and Phase 2 shipped and tested a ticketless allocation succeeding — something has to name that directory, and making the ticket structurally mandatory would silently REVERSE OQ-1 and inflict the lockout D4's opt-in exists to prevent. The reading invents no new identity scheme (in particular **no synthetic placeholder ticket**) and reuses a value `allocate_feature_dir` already validates, so it was the narrowest resolution available rather than the preferred one. **The maintainer ruled on 2026-08-31 that the shipped rule STANDS, and D3 is amended IN PLACE to RESTATE the rule rather than carve out an exception**: the leaf is the feature's identifier in whatever form it has — the ticket when the run named one, the validated slug when it did not — with the ticket the **ordinary** form of that identifier and not the only permitted one. ⚠ **The restatement is the point, not a wording preference: an exception clause invites a later session to narrow the rule back; a restated rule does not.** What the ruling weighed and rejected, recorded because a ratification with no reasoning is what this plan spent a session avoiding: **the alternative that preserved D3's letter — ticketless installs keeping the numbered `specs/NNN-slug/` shape — was CONSIDERED AND REJECTED**, because it would make **two WRITE shapes permanent by design** where D6 accepts two READ shapes only as the price of not migrating, and because it would make an install's directory layout depend on a config key, so two installs of the same framework would be structurally different; and **making the ticket structurally mandatory was rejected** for silently reversing OQ-1 and inflicting D4's lockout. **The disclosed cost SURVIVES the amendment and is not argued away**: two ticketless allocations with the same slug in the same `YYYY/MM` bucket collide loudly via the never-overwrite contract, which NARROWS plan 68 OQ-4's original guarantee for that one path. ⚠ **One residual rode out of the ruling, and the two code sites the plan named must still be read APART rather than as a pair**: `_shared/feature_alloc.py:552-577` (the leaf) was **FALSIFIED** — it called the rule *"a candidate for explicit ratification, not asserted as settled"* and *"Phase 3's item this plan's own text left open"* — while `decide_branch_action`'s docstring (the branch) is **NOT falsified**, because **the ruling covered D3 only** and **D5 is unamended** — its text still reads `spec/TICKET` and stays silent on the branch name a ticketless allocation gets, which is the same silence D3 carried until this amendment, and that comment records exactly that silence. ⚠ **THE LEAF RESIDUAL IS CLOSED 2026-09-03 (`70656d4`), and closing it found FOUR carriers of the falsified claim where this plan, this entry and the `CLAUDE.md` index line each named ONE**: `allocate_feature_dir`'s docstring (the named site); the module docstring's "Slug collisions" section (`:229-249`), **which the named site itself cites as *"the full argument"***, so a reader following that cross-reference landed precisely on the stale claim; and two docstrings in `tests/lib/_shared/test_feature_alloc.py` (the module docstring and `test_fresh_allocation_ticketless_uses_slug_as_leaf`'s). ⚠ **Three files — the plan, the `CLAUDE.md` index line and this entry — each named exactly ONE carrier; there were four** — the same lesson Phase 5's residual 3 recorded, arriving a second time: a harvest records the sites it happened to see, and re-visiting a recorded list is not the manoeuvre that finds the class. **Docstring prose only — no logic, signature, constant, assertion or test name changed**; the verify run was `python3 -m pytest tests/lib/_shared/ -q` → 785 passed, 20 subtests passed (a reading with its command attached, not a constant — re-run rather than quote it). ⚠ **Two things were deliberately PRESERVED and must survive any later edit**: the **rejected alternatives stay written at the site** (a ticketless allocation kept under the numbered shape — rejected for making two WRITE shapes permanent by design where D6 accepts two READ shapes only; and a structurally mandatory ticket — rejected for silently reversing OQ-1 and inflicting D4's lockout), and **D5 was NOT widened**, so `decide_branch_action`'s docstring still records D5's silence on the ticketless branch name exactly as described above. **(2) OQ-3's supporting citation was REFUTED and is now CORRECTED** — OQ-3 asked to confirm a local date *"as `set-date` already enforces `YYYY-MM-DD` elsewhere"*, but `cmd_set_date` has **no clock logic at all** (it validates a caller-supplied string) and the value that actually reaches `--date` is composed by `specify/main.md`'s own prose via `date -u`, which is **UTC — the opposite of what OQ-3 cited it for**. That parenthetical is **struck** and OQ-3 now records **UTC** with the true reason: every other "now" in `src/devforge/lib` is `datetime.now(timezone.utc)`, and UTC does not move a feature between buckets when a machine's timezone changes. Allocation buckets on `datetime.now(timezone.utc)`, injectable for deterministic tests. ⚠ **This is a factual correction to a supporting citation, not a re-ratification — the item's answer never moved — and the record that the citation was checked and found wrong is KEPT rather than deleted; do not delete it, and do not cite OQ-3 as evidence for local time.** ⚠ **D3 and OQ-3 ARE now edited; nothing else is** — no other decision, no other OQ, and neither D4's nor D6's deliberation. **Phase 5 is BUILT and its Verify is MET (2026-08-31).** Its first commit swept four files — `src/devforge/storage-rules.md` (which now describes the tree as *"a birth index, never a"* work index, exactly the framing D2's accepted cost requires), `src/CLAUDE.md`, `DEVELOPMENT-STATUS.md` and `README.md`. **All five of its recorded residuals are CLOSED**: `iter_feature_dirs`' docstring (the highest-value one, since this plan sends future sessions to that module as the ground truth for how allocation works), the four user-visible argparse `--help` sites in `plan_helper.py` and `_finalize/_cli.py`, the four command/agent prose sites (`specify/main.md`'s cold-path parenthetical and its approval-summary path, `finalize/main.md`'s `[WIP] Feature docs:` label, and `tech-writer.md`'s `/devforge:finalize` input bullet), `tests/lib/_research/test_render_run_by.py`'s stale `KNOWN GAP` justification, and the `CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]` entry. **The ONE item that still blocked the Verify was then DECIDED, and the decision covered BOTH halves of the pair the plan warned would otherwise diverge** — `assign-spec-number`'s docstring (`_specify/_cmds_phase4_setters.py:79`) and its user-visible argparse help (`_specify/_cli.py:325-327`) now each name `NNN-*` as *the earlier numbered shape*, not today's bucketed `<YYYY>/<MM>/<leaf>` layout. ⚠ **The LABEL is the whole change and no behaviour moved**: the verb still scans for `NNN-*`, still delegates to `next_spec_number`, and still exists for the `/devforge:specify` genuine-fallback path this plan takes no decision on — do not read the label as a claim the verb was retired. **The Verify's grep was re-run and classified in full, which is what makes the verdict checkable rather than asserted**: `grep -rn 'NNN-' src/ scripts/` returns **128 matching lines — 127 in `src/`, 1 in `scripts/`** (⚠ a reading with a method attached, never a constant — re-derive before quoting), in exactly three classes: **47 lines of `bugs/NNN-<slug>.md` numbering** and **11 lines of task-file `NNN-title.md` numbering**, both OUT OF SCOPE by the plan's own Non-goals and OQ-4, and **70 lines of legacy feature-dir reference, each labelled as the earlier shape**. ⚠ **The label is carried FOUR different ways, which is why a same-line filter reports false survivors**: on the same line (the dominant *"legacy specs/NNN-slug/ or Phase-3 specs/YYYY/MM/<leaf>/"* form), on an ADJACENT line (`src/git-hooks/pre-commit-forcing-functions.sh:56`, whose preceding comment states both depths), in an ENCLOSING docstring paragraph (`_shared/feature_alloc.py:348`/`:351`, whose own docstring goes on at `:353` to say *"DEAD as of … Phase 3 (D6) … a fresh allocation is keyed on the ticket-or-slug leaf, never on NNN"*), and under a SECTION HEADING (`src/devforge/storage-rules.md:159`, under `**Numbered shape — read, never created anew:**`). ⚠ **Read MET at exactly its width: it is a grep bar plus this phase's declared scope, and it is NOT consumer validation — a Verify met by grep is not a run, and a grep for one token cannot see a sentence that spells the assumption another way** (the same limit Phase 1b's Verify states for its own class). ⚠ **The `_shared/feature_alloc.py:552-577` residual recorded above sat OUTSIDE this Verify and was NOT closed by it** — it carries no `NNN-` token, so the grep never reached it, which is exactly why the class was missed by a Verify built on that grep; it is closed separately on 2026-09-03, as recorded above. **⚠ THE HONEST BOUNDS, none of which the build strengthened.** **Both directory shapes coexist FOREVER and nothing migrates** — resolution reads both and writes only the bucketed one, so a sentence naming only ONE shape is wrong whichever one it names. **The helper is NOT the sole author of the layout**: `/devforge:specify`'s genuine-fallback arm still creates the directory with a bare `mkdir -p` in command prose, `specify_helper` has no `allocate-feature-dir` verb (that verb exists only on `research_helper` and `discover_helper`), and Phase 3's composition change does not reach that arm — **this plan proposes no verb for it and takes no decision on whether one should exist.** **The ticket gate is discipline, not verification** — nothing confirms a ticket exists. **`/devforge:grill`'s `resolve_target_feature` can no longer distinguish an UNREADABLE `specs/` from an EMPTY one** (the accessor's OSError-means-absent contract swallowed the caller's distinct diagnostic); a MISSING `specs_root` is unaffected, and this was accepted rather than repaired. **Plan 88's cold `/devforge:fix` lane holds no feature directory at all, by decision** — everything here binds the intake-allocated feature path only, and a caller with no feature reference is a supported state, not an error path. **Two findings surfaced by Phase 1b are open and owned by no phase**: nine command specs' blanket *"Every mechanical step is a normal Bash tool call to `<cmd>_helper …`"* claim (already false before this plan, since `artifact_helper commit-artifacts` has always been called from all nine — fixing them is one deliberate repo-wide decision, not a side effect of a layout migration), and `_specify/_schema.py`'s over-precise comment about `_group_for_path`'s key set (already imprecise before this work; the change widened the ways a path reaches the fallback but did not create it). **Non-goals, stated so they are not re-proposed**: tracker integration of any kind; migrating existing `specs/NNN-slug/` directories; a finalize-time archive or any other post-creation move; a per-edit provenance trail (D9 — that is `git log --follow` reimplemented in markdown inside a git-tracked file); stamping provenance on task files, bug files or the twenty other artifacts with header blocks (D7); changing `bugs/NNN-*.md` naming (OQ-4 — **corroborated** by plan 88 having built its cold lane feature-less by decision, so a ticket rule scoped to intake-allocated feature directories and a lane deliberately built without one agree by construction rather than by luck); and any new `verify-*` PHASE-3.5 gate number — **plan 75's tripwire holds everywhere except D4's declared exception.** **Phase 6 = user-driven consumer e2e HARD GATE, NOT run — DEFERRED TO POST-RELEASE by maintainer decision 2026-09-03, a TIMING decision and not a waiver**, five known-answer anchors: `REQUIRE_TICKET=true` with a ticket given → `specs/2026/08/PROJ-123/` plus branch `spec/PROJ-123` · `REQUIRE_TICKET=true` with none → intake refuses and **nothing** is created under `specs/` · `REQUIRE_TICKET=false` → the pre-existing behaviour, unchanged · an install carrying a legacy `specs/NNN-slug/` still resolved by `/devforge:plan` and `/devforge:implement` · `git config user.name` unset → `spec.md` renders with **no** provenance line. **Anchors 1 and 2 are scored as a PAIR** — a rule that satisfies 1 by allocating anything at all fails 2. ⚠ **Phase 3's own lesson is the one to carry into that run**: three green suites missed a live defect during Phase 1, and Phase 3 then surfaced four unpredicted defects of which three were silent stops rather than failures, so **"the suite is green" is evidence about the covered surface only** and a clean Phase 6 would be the first evidence of any kind that this layout works on a real install.
- `92-AGENT-MODEL-AND-EFFORT-CONFIG-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE (build) 2026-09-03 — Phase 0 CLOSED and Phases 1–5 BUILT. Phase 6 consumer e2e DEFERRED — user-driven HARD GATE, NOT run, and BATCHED behind plan 85's wall-clock baseline run per OQ-4.** Commits `2f6e409` (Phase 1) / `7dea6a3` (Phase 2) / `c05e5e7` (Phase 3) on `develop-2.0-init`, with Phases 4 and 5 built in the same pass. **Everything is build-verified and NOT consumer-validated**; no install has been run against the apply verb, the probe, the pin or the advisory line. Created 2026-09-03; the document carries no private-client identifiers. ⚠ **THE EVIDENCE SPLIT IS THE FIRST THING TO CARRY, and no summary may blur it: ONE observed, grep-verified defect PLUS FOUR maintainer directives — there was no consumer incident and none is claimed.** The defect half is a repair; **the directive half is a predicted-gap change in plan 87's class.** **The defect.** `/devforge:configure` Q11 asked three tier-model questions, validated each answer through its own setter, stored them in `configure.yaml` and rendered them into `project-config.json` as `CLAUDE_TIER_THINK` / `_DO` / `_VERIFY` — **and nothing read them.** A grep across `install.sh`, `update.sh`, `src/` and `scripts/` returned hits only inside `_render.py`, which writes them; **no agent file carried a `{{CLAUDE_TIER_*}}` placeholder**, so `substitute-file` never touched a `model:` line and the emitted model was fixed at generation time from a static map. Around it sat three further falsehoods: two shipped comments described the missing override as though it existed (`install_defaults.py`'s *"Wizard's Q10a may override per tier"*, `generate-agents.py`'s *"the post-install config step may OVERWRITE these via key-based regex replacement"*), `q11-tiers.md` recommended `verify = Haiku` while the generator shipped `sonnet` — **so because of the defect every install to date got sonnet** — and `DEVELOPMENT-STATUS.md:104` advertised the feature under three key names (`MODEL_THINK` / `MODEL_DO` / `MODEL_VERIFY`) that **do not exist**, with a think-tier agent list naming three of five. **The four directives (2026-09-03, paraphrased):** offer the model per tier and show current versions at run time so the framework never needs a release to track a model release; also ask the effort level; give a per-command recommendation (the maintainer undecided for `/devforge:plan` — Opus or Fable); and degrade cleanly where Fable is unavailable. **Phase 0 CLOSED 2026-09-03 by a SINGLE BLANKET MAINTAINER DIRECTIVE** — verbatim, in Ukrainian: *"ратифікую все як рекомендовано, починай Phase 1"* — **every item (D1–D9 + OQ-1–OQ-4) ratified AS RECOMMENDED.** ⚠ **No per-item deliberation was supplied, and the close record says so plainly rather than implying thirteen arguments were had** (plan 91's Phase-0 precedent for recording a blanket directive). The four items flagged as needing an explicit pick were answered by that same directive: **D1** = arm (b), the helper verb; **D2** = both halves, `verify = sonnet`, **ratified TOGETHER with D4** (the pairing is recorded at both, because ratifying D4 while recommending Haiku would have shipped the one recommended combination the vendor documents as failing); **D6** = the contract extension ACCEPTED; **OQ-1** = `opus` and `default`. **What shipped.** A new `configure_helper apply-agent-models` rewrites the `model:` / `effort:` frontmatter of every `.claude/agents/*.md` from `CLAUDE_TIER_*` / `CLAUDE_EFFORT_*` — at `/devforge:configure` Phase 5.4 (a fourth sub-step after `render-config` → `prune-agents` → `substitute-templates`) and again in `update.sh` after the three-way merge, under the same `HAS_CONFIG` guard `substitute_placeholders()` uses, **so a configured choice survives an update instead of silently reverting**. It is keyed on a **new emitted `model_tier:` line**, and the reason is mechanical: a consumer install carries only the generated `.claude/agents/*.md` and their raw snapshot — **the `model_tier:`-bearing `src/agents/` sources are never shipped** — so without an emitted tier the verb would have to duplicate the 19-agent roster in Python. A file **without** that line is left untouched, which is the same mechanism that protects a consumer's hand-written agent and that implements D6's pin. The verb is idempotent, two-pass (exit 2 on an unknown tier, an out-of-enum effort, a DUPLICATED `model_tier:` / `model:` / `effort:` key, or a `model_tier:` with no sibling `model:` — **nothing written**), CRLF-safe end to end (it reads `read_bytes().decode()` because `Path.read_text()` silently translates `\r\n` and would have rewritten every line ending), normalizes a legacy stored `Opus` to `opus` at apply time, and prints a per-agent summary. **Model choice is version-free BY CONSTRUCTION and a maintainer test enforces it**: the options are Claude Code's `opus` / `sonnet` / `haiku` / `fable` aliases, which float with Claude Code's own mapping, with a free-text arm for pinning a full model ID and run-time-known IDs offered as **unverified suggestions that reach no file**. The tripwire asserts **both** version shapes absent under `src/` — the API-ID shape `claude-[a-z]+-[0-9]` **and** the display-name shape `\b(opus|sonnet|haiku|fable)[\s-]+[0-9]`, widened at build after the reviewer showed the hyphenated `sonnet-4-5` slipping both drafted patterns — and ⚠ **the gate FIRED ONCE mid-build on a real instance** (a docstring example, `claude-opus-4-7-bedrock`), which is the only evidence this plan has that it catches anything. Effort ships as three enum-restricted `claude_effort_*` fields where the `default` answer **REMOVES** the `effort:` line (Claude Code documents that as inheriting the session level), asked in the SAME AskUserQuestion call as its tier's model question. A **four-alias availability probe** runs before the first question — one one-word subagent dispatch per alias, any error marking it unavailable — so an unavailable alias is not offered, with the pin route still reachable. **`security-reviewer` is pinned to `opus`** via a new OPTIONAL `model_pin` meta field (regex TIGHTENED at build to `^[a-z][a-z-]*$`, no digit, so a version string cannot enter through the one field designed to bypass the tier), because Fable's documented bug-finding gains **exclude** security-focused analysis where cyber classifiers apply and a refusal inside the four-reviewer panel surfaces as a **missing verdict** rather than an error. A one-line **advisory** at the start of eight commands names the tier, the configured model and the session model. **Live counts, COUNTED and never incremented:** `FIELD_SCHEMA` **35** (was 32), `ENUM_FIELDS` **8** (was 5), `FIELD_DEFAULTS` **6** (was 3), `_PROJECT_CONFIG_KEY_ORDER` **43** (was 40); the four count pins renamed to carry the live numbers, and **three PRE-EXISTING stale counts corrected in passing** (`_render.py`, `_cmds_verify.py`, and `_cmds_set.py`'s *"Enum scalar setters (6)"* header, **already wrong at 5** before this plan existed). **Phase 1's Step 0 asked `claude-code-guide` BEFORE any code, and one answer landed in a third state the plan had not anticipated.** Unknown-frontmatter-key handling is **NOT DOCUMENTED either way** — the sub-agents page enumerates the conditions under which a subagent file is SKIPPED and an unknown key is not among them, but nothing states that unknown keys are ignored — so **the STOP arm did not trigger and the bound did not close**; D1's keying rests on that enumerated skip list plus the `applies_to` precedent (an unrecognized key shipped since plan 15 and dispatched on without complaint), and the STOP arm survives **re-pointed at an OBSERVED failure** (agents that stop being listed once `model_tier:` ships). Key ORDER is **not documented as significant**, so positions were chosen and pinned by tests. And `effort:` on a model without effort support is **DOCUMENTED as a SILENT FALLBACK** to the highest supported level at or below the one set — ⚠ **which WEAKENED two of this plan's own sentences and both were corrected in place rather than deleted**: D2 half 2's fourth reason (*"Haiku 4.5 errors on `effort`"*) describes the **API**, not Claude Code, so **THREE reasons hold half 2 up, not four**, and D4's honest bound moved from *"fails at DISPATCH"* to *"degrades silently at dispatch (documented fallback)"*. **⚠ THE HONEST BOUNDS, none of which the build strengthened.** **Effort is NOT validated against the model** — building that table would be the version tracking the plan exists to avoid — so an unsupported level degrades **silently**, and **silent is arguably worse than loud**: a downgrade looks exactly like success and nothing here detects it. **The advisory line ENFORCES NOTHING — BY DECISION, NOT BY LIMITATION.** Command frontmatter DOES accept `model` and `effort` per the current slash-commands documentation (the override applies for the rest of that turn, with the session model resuming at the next prompt, and a model excluded by the organization's allowlist is silently not used), so the command-scoped override is **possible and documented**; the maintainer **deferred it to OQ-5 on 2026-09-03 as a separate decision**, which is why this build prints a recommendation instead of setting anything. ⚠ **A drafting-time claim that it was IMPOSSIBLE — "`model` and `effort` are agent-only, verified against the plugins reference" — was REFUTED by Phase 4's guide pass and is corrected wherever it appeared.** And **its session-model half rests on an OBSERVED, undocumented harness statement**, printing `unknown` without it; Phase 2's guide pass confirmed that how a running session learns its own model is not documented either. **A green probe is NOT an entitlement guarantee** — it observes what this session's build can dispatch, never the organization's plan or its data-retention setting (a zero-data-retention org gets a 400 from Fable, possibly at dispatch rather than at probe). **The think default stays `opus` because nothing measured the alternative**, and the vendor's de-prescription guidance cuts against a deliberately prescriptive command corpus — so **offering Fable is offering a configuration this framework has not tuned for**, and no summary may imply the framework "runs on Fable". **TWO documented environment variables defeat the whole mechanism undetectably**: `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE` makes Claude Code ignore every subagent's `model:` field outright (D6's pin included), and **before Claude Code v2.1.251 `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` came FIRST and overrode the frontmatter too** — so a consumer on an older build with that variable set never sees the emitted line at all. **The framework checks neither and builds no version floor**; rule both out before diagnosing any "apply didn't work" report. ⚠ **ONE OPEN RESIDUAL, found by Phase 3's local reproduction and deliberately NOT repaired**: `git merge-file` on base `model: opus` / current `model: fable` / other `model: sonnet` **CONFLICTS**, so a future change to a tier's **static** default would, for every consumer whose configured model differs from both, leave that agent file's **WHOLE update unapplied — body included** — and its **snapshot stale**, a condition **recurring on every subsequent update** until resolved by hand. **And the failure is camouflaged**: the post-merge apply re-derives `model:` from configuration regardless, so the field reads correct while the stale body is invisible from the summary line, and the **only** signal is the earlier, decoupled `Merge conflicts in <file>` warn. **Treat a static tier default as effectively FROZEN — changing one is a migration, not an edit.** Two candidate repairs are recorded (a migration note on any static-default change; teach the merge that `model:` is consumer-owned) and **neither is built**. **Phase 3 also corrected its own drafted rationale**: the *"an early apply would poison the baseline"* framing is **retired** — every snapshot write sources from the regen or template dirs, never from the live target — and the real hazard is that an early apply pre-mutates the live file's *current* side before the diff, manufacturing a spurious conflict; **the constraint (apply AFTER the snapshot refresh) never moved, only its explanation, and a correct constraint resting on a wrong reason is a latent trap.** **Phase 2's HIGH review finding was a product of PARALLEL EXECUTION and the lesson generalizes**: Phase 3 landed its `update.sh` call while Phase 2 was being written, so a *"a follow-up change adds this same call … until that call lands"* sentence was already false when it reached review — **write forward-looking sentences from the TREE, not from the phase order.** **Phase 5's sweep, as executed**, was widened by Phase 2's build beyond the plan's original list: `DEVELOPMENT-STATUS.md` had a **SECOND** stale site at `:58` beside the `:104` the plan named (a fix landing only `:104` would have left `:58` looking verified); `docs/v2/ARCHITECTURE.md` was edited **NARROWLY on the maintainer's 2026-09-03 pick** — *"fix only the falsified sites"*, the full sweep explicitly NOT chosen — correcting the field/key/enum counts, the `verify=Haiku` recommendation, the Q11 description and the setter table, while **its unrelated stale counts (e.g. *"~32 subcommands"*) stay untouched as an accumulated gap for a separate maintainer item**; and `src/agents-AUTHORING.md:87`'s *"17 agents in four families"* became the live **19**, with **`devils-advocate` and `spec-formalizer` recorded as named by NO family list** — a pre-existing gap **named rather than decided**, since assigning them a family is an editorial call this plan has no mandate to make. Verified no-ops recorded as findings: `README.md` and `src/CLAUDE.md` carry no tier or model vocabulary (plan 08's trim discipline binds the latter); the two Python comments were already rewritten at Phase 1 (`grep -n "Q10a\|may OVERWRITE" scripts/` returns nothing); and `install.sh:298`'s *"35-key ALL-NULL stub"* comment plus `src/devforge/project-config.json` (35 keys against 43 live) were **left alone deliberately** — **functionally harmless and VERIFIED so, not assumed**, since `apply-agent-models`' `.get()` reads a null and an absent key alike — with two recorded options (regenerate the stub from `_PROJECT_CONFIG_KEY_ORDER`, or pin the two equal with a maintainer test in plan 89's style). **Plan 63's carve-out: this plan contributed NO delta** — it changed no `disable-model-invocation` flag and added no command — and the counts read **16 model-invocable / 4 human-typed-only** because of **plan 93**, not this plan; the check was run LIVE (four files carry the flag: the setup chain) per plan 63's own coordination rule. **Plan 15's meta-block contract was EXTENDED, not overridden**, and its plan file carries a dated note saying so: one optional `model_pin` field, and `model_tier` now emitted as its own line **in addition to** the static `model:` it always produced; the field names and their required/optional status are unchanged, and none of plan 15's reasoning was edited. ⚠ **Phase 5 also corrected a claim in this plan's own text**: its *"no AI-attribution trailer"* commit instruction was **false as of this build** — the repo's last eleven commits across two sessions all carry the harness `Co-Authored-By` + `Claude-Session` trailers, and this plan's three commits followed that observed convention. **Non-goals, stated so they are not re-proposed**: pinning any model version in `src/`; validating model × effort compatibility; setting a command's own session model (declined for this plan and deferred to OQ-5 — **not impossible**; command frontmatter does accept `model`/`effort`); Fable prompt de-prescription (D8, with an OBSERVED-underperformance trigger); per-agent routing beyond the single pin; removing the unused `scan` tier (OQ-3 — zero members, left as is); any gate, `verify-*` number or hard-fail validator (**plan 75's tripwire holds in both halves**); back-porting into shipped installs; and any priced or dated model claim in emitted text. **Phase 6 = user-driven consumer e2e HARD GATE, NOT run**, six known-answer anchors: `/devforge:configure` with `think = fable` → **(1a)** `architect.md` carries `model: fable` **and (1b)** `security-reviewer.md` carries `model: opus` · effort `default` → **no `effort:` line**, effort `high` → `effort: high` · an agent-BODY change plus `update.sh` → body merged, `model: fable` intact, apply summary printed, **and whether any agent reported a merge conflict recorded** · a session without Fable → probe line printed, `fable` absent from every option list, pin route reachable · `/devforge:plan` → the advisory line prints (**`unknown` for the session half is a PASS and a finding for D7's bound**) · and the OQ-1 A/B, one feature run twice, recording the wall-clock via `profile_helper` **as a NUMBER**. **Anchors 1a and 1b are scored as a PAIR** — an apply that rewrites everything passes 1a and fails 1b, one that rewrites nothing does the reverse. ⚠ **Anchor 6 is the ONLY evidence that may flip OQ-1's default**, and one run of each arm attributes nothing; ⚠ **a clean Phase 6 would be evidence that the configuration path works, NEVER evidence that Fable helps this pipeline.** ⚠ **AMENDED IN PLACE 2026-09-04 by `94-MODEL-OVERRIDE-AND-NO-DEFAULTS-PLAN.md`, dated at each decision, with no phase re-opened and no build record edited — this plan stays DONE (build).** **D2 half 1 REVERSED**: the static tier default map is deleted from both trees (`install_defaults.py` removed entirely, its only symbol having been that literal) along with the equality pin, every agent emits the constant `model: inherit`, and **half 2 survives only as a LABEL** — `verify = sonnet` is a `(Recommended)` marker beside an option, no longer a value written into any file. **D6 REPLACED**: `model_pin` is removed from the authoring contract (this plan's contract-extension note kept verbatim under a `SUPERSEDED` prefix), the emitter ignores such a declaration with one stderr warning, and `security-reviewer` moved to a fourth tier `model_tier: security` with its own `CLAUDE_TIER_SECURITY` / `CLAUDE_EFFORT_SECURITY` fields and its own Q11.4 — **the very "fifth tier" alternative D6 rejected, now winning not because its surface got cheaper but because the framework may no longer make the choice at all**; the refusal reasoning survives as that question's description. **OQ-5 RESOLVED AFFIRMATIVELY**: the deferred command-scoped override was BUILT — `apply-models` (renamed, alias one release) now writes `model:` / `effort:` into eight commands as well as the agents, so D7's advisory line **survives and gains a job** as the override's only readback, printing `not configured` for a tier nobody answered. **Trap 3 CLOSED BY CONSTRUCTION, with three clauses that must travel together**: the emitted baseline is now the constant `inherit`, so the class cannot recur; it fires ONCE on the release that removes the default, mitigated by a second `update.sh` seat that normalizes the snapshot from configuration before the agent merge (a local reproduction reported zero conflicts, and a `git merge-file` experiment reproduced both the conflict before and the clean merge after); and **the residual is WIDER than a hand-edited file** — any live agent whose `model:` / `effort:` is stale relative to `project-config.json` at merge time still conflicts, so the class is narrowed, not closed. **D1's null-tier rule** becomes `inherit` on an agent and NO `model:` line on a command, and its `update.sh` seat MOVED to after the promoted-command re-emit. **Phase-6 anchors 1b and 3 are RESTATED at the anchor list**: 1b now expects whatever Q11.4 chose (never `opus` by guarantee, and `inherit` on an install that never answered), and 3 additionally checks that a mapped command still carries its `model:` line **after** the re-emit.

Build per the flow."*) — **every item (D1–D7 + OQ-1–OQ-7) ratified AS RECOMMENDED.** ⚠ **No per-item deliberation was supplied and the close record says so plainly** (plans 91 / 92 / 94's precedent). Its five explicit picks were answered by that same directive: **`tickets/` WITH the mandatory disambiguation rule** (the `backlog/` arm not taken); **the six fields and three status values exactly as tabled**, plus the H1 rendering element outside that count; **the conversational-offer amendment RATIFIED**; and **`--body-file` with `-` for stdin and NO inline `--body`**. ⚠ **The fifth — OQ-1's reuse MECHANISM — was NOT answered by the directive**, because that section offered two arms and recommended neither: **the PROMOTE arm was picked by the BUILD ORCHESTRATOR under the blanket delegation**, and the record says so rather than presenting it as a maintainer pick. **The central decision, and its ground is mechanical rather than aesthetic:** merging tickets into `bugs/` — the maintainer's own floated option — was declined because `/devforge:fix`'s cold lane triggers on the literal path shape `bugs/NNN-<slug>.md`, a deliberately mechanical trigger, so an enhancement filed there would be a **syntactically valid cold-fix input** whose remediation bypasses spec, consistency check, plan, grill and decomposition. Closing that would require a type discriminator inside a lane whose own consumer e2e has never run. **What shipped.** `tickets/NNN-<slug>.md` as a document type beside `bugs/` — **its own `NNN` sequence** (a populated `bugs/` never shifts it), tracked by adding nothing anywhere (no gitignore entry, no installer change, no `.gitkeep` — it inherits `bugs/`'s properties by construction). `/devforge:report-ticket` as the **21st promoted command, model-invocable**, mirroring `/devforge:report-bug` section for section over a new `_shared/ticket_file.py` + `_report_ticket/` with a POSIX launcher pair; its description is **37 words, counted**; its frontmatter carries four keys plus `Write` in `allowed-tools`. **The one deliberate divergence from its sibling: the body travels as `--body-file <path>` (or `-` on stdin) and there is no inline body argument at all**, because inside a double-quoted shell argument backticks and `$(...)` are command substitution and a pasted tracker-ticket body routinely contains both — *"quote carefully"* being the unfalsifiable-compliance shape this repo's zero-escape-hatch policy forbids by name. OQ-1's promote arm made `scan_highest_number` and `slugify` public in `bug_file.py` — **two behavior-preserving renames and "nothing else"**, on the single-owner ground that a copied scan lets the two directories' numbering rules drift silently. **Phase 2b registered all three rosters in one commit** (`_PROMOTED`, `DISPOSITIONS` with a reason in `report-bug`'s voice, `HELPER_STEMS`) with five test pins and four prose numerals; **the observed-gate-failure ritual was executed AND independently reproduced by the reviewer**, which is what separates a performed ritual from a recorded one. **Phase 3a existed because Phase 3 could not honour its own bound without it**: `set-verbatim-prompt` accepted only an inline `--value`, `required=True`, so the consumer arm as written would have put a pasted body through a shell argument; it gained an additive `--value-file` (mutually exclusive, exactly one required, `newline=""`, both routes through the same validator) with **the verb name, the state key and inline `--value` behavior byte-unchanged**. **Phase 3** gave `/devforge:research` the ticket-file arm — announce which file was read, H1 title → topic, body → scratch → `--value-file`, ticket ID carried forward — **extended the two rubric forcing sentences without removing a clause**, named *"the ticket file already answers everything"* as a fabrication, and made Step 4.1's ticket-ID question carry a **conditional third authored option** where **pre-offering is not answering**: the user still picks, position carries no weight, and a declined pre-offer allocates per their actual answer. **Live counts, COUNTED and never incremented: 21 promoted / 17 model-invocable / 4 human-typed-only / 13 `READS` / 8 `N/A`.** The human-typed SET is unchanged and no `disable-model-invocation` flag moved. ⚠ **THE HONEST BOUNDS, none of which the build strengthened and none of which may be dropped from a summary.** **The lifecycle is MANUAL-ONLY**: no command flips, fills or deletes a ticket file, and a consuming `/devforge:research` run READS it and writes nothing back — so **a ticket file whose work shipped months ago can read `Open` forever and nothing detects it.** That is the ratified design, with the write-back recorded as a named strengthening path rather than refused. **Nothing links a ticket file to the feature directory** a later run allocates from it. **The `Type` field is unchecked** — nothing prevents a defect from being filed as an enhancement, which lands it outside the cold-fix lane with nothing to say so. **Detection is path-shaped and fallible in both directions**, mitigated only by the run announcing which file it read. **The conversational third arm stays ADVISORY** — model judgment with nothing checking it. And ⚠ **the PRE-EXISTING inline hazard on the ordinary intake path is UNTOUCHED**: an ordinary `/devforge:research "<pasted text>"` still routes its argument inline, only the ticket-file arm uses the file route, and **a summary reporting this plan as having fixed the inline hazard generally has over-claimed.** **Non-goals, stated so they are not re-proposed**: any tracker integration or API call; any change to `bugs/`'s schema, its writer, `/devforge:fix` or `/devforge:report-bug` beyond the one conversational clause; any forward pointer inside `/devforge:report-bug` (plan 88's reading (i) is NOT reopened); **any gate, `verify-*` number or hard-fail validator — plan 75's tripwire holds in both halves**, with Python confined to Phases 1, 2b and 3a; any mechanical lifecycle transition; migrating existing `bugs/` files; `/devforge:verify` triage writing to `tickets/`; and any change to the human-typed carve-out. **Reviewer passes:** Phase 2 SHIP-READY 0/0/2/2 (two fixed, two skipped with reasons recorded — a cosmetic column misalignment and a qualified third compound causing no ambiguity); Phase 2b SHIP-READY with **ZERO** findings, explicitly closing Phase 1's High; Phase 3a one Medium FIXED (an uncovered guard whose adjacent test's overlapping stderr text made it read as covered); Phase 3 0/2/1/1, all FIXED. **Residuals recorded, none this plan's to fix**: five pre-existing bare-"ticket" sentences elsewhere in `storage-rules.md`, now technically at odds with the disambiguation rule this plan added (the rule is prescriptive for new writing and claims nothing about the existing file); `HELPER_STEMS` still cross-checked against `_PROMOTED` by **nothing**, so a future command omitted there fails silently (a three-line test, declined as maintainer-gate scope this plan does not own); and `DEVELOPMENT-STATUS.md`'s claim that `install.sh` ships an empty `bugs/` directory with a `.gitkeep` — the script contains no `bugs` string at all and the directory is created lazily on first write. **Phase 5 = user-driven consumer e2e HARD GATE, NOT run**, five known-answer anchors: capturing an enhancement (correct fields, numbered `001` despite a populated `bugs/`) · capturing pasted tracker text containing a backtick and a `$(` sequence with a ticket ID (**body byte-identical**, ID recorded — the awkward characters are the point, and a mangled body is a finding about the body route rather than a helper bug) · consuming it through `/devforge:research` (**all six rubric dimensions asked separately with the turn count recorded as a NUMBER**, the ID pre-offered and NOT pre-selected, **and the ticket file's hash unchanged across the run, including on the confirmed-save arm**) · `bugs/` and the cold-fix contract untouched (with what `/devforge:fix` does on a `tickets/` path RECORDED as an observation, since a cold-mode entry there would falsify this plan's central argument) · and the three-arm offer observed in conversation. **Anchors 2 and 3 are scored as a PAIR** — a capture that mangles the ID passes the consumer's mechanics and fails the pair. ⚠ **A clean Phase 5 would be evidence that the lane works, NEVER evidence that the missing capture path had cost anything** — there was no incident, and there still is none.

- `96-PR-READING-GUIDE-PLAN.md` — **✅ DONE (build) 2026-09-06 — Phase 0 CLOSED and Phases 1–4 BUILT; plan CLOSED 2026-09-07 by maintainer directive. Phase 5 consumer e2e DEFERRED TO POST-RELEASE by maintainer decision 2026-09-07 — user-driven HARD GATE, NOT run and NOT WAIVED.** The directive, verbatim in Ukrainian: *«ок закривай план як зроблений тест буде після релізу»* (*"ok, close the plan as done; the test will be after the release"*). ⚠ **This is a TIMING decision, not a waiver — the maintainer intends to run it** (the plan-86 / plan-91 pattern), so **"done" here means BUILT and BUILD-VERIFIED and NEVER that Phase 5 passed.** On `develop-2.0-init`; the document carries no private-client identifiers and is committed normally. Commits: `d898b09` draft / `b6f5232` Phase 0 close / `26fedaa` Phase 1 / `c0a9baa` Phase 2 / `46acd27` Phase 3 / `275e32b` Phase 4. **Everything is build-verified and NOT consumer-validated**: no install has rendered either new section or seen the printed PR line. ⚠ **THE EVIDENCE FIRST: ONE maintainer concern raised in conversation on 2026-09-06 — humans tire reading large AI-generated PRs, a GENERAL phenomenon and explicitly NOT a consumer incident with this framework — plus grep-verified structural facts. No incident, none claimed, nothing measured** — a predicted-gap plan in plan 87's class, and **the plan requires this be said wherever it is summarized.** The structural facts, each re-derivable: `summary.md`'s `### Files changed` rendered directory COUNTS only, with nothing telling a reviewer where to start; `has_unverified` was already emitted per task by `parse-completion-notes` and named by NO composition step (plan 89's *"already on the wire, never named by the spec"* pattern, cited as precedent); `read_verification` returned only `ac_list` / `verdict` / `path`, so every advisory `/devforge:verify` records was invisible to `/devforge:summarize`; and **`gh pr create` appeared nowhere in `src/`** — every `gh pr` hit belongs to `/devforge:pr-review`, which CONSUMES an existing PR, so no command created or composed one. **Phase 0 CLOSED 2026-09-06 by a SINGLE BLANKET MAINTAINER DIRECTIVE** («го»), **every item (D1–D4 + OQ-1–OQ-3) ratified AS RECOMMENDED.** ⚠ **No per-item deliberation was supplied and the close record says so plainly** (plans 91 / 92 / 94 / 95 precedent), with the explicit warning that the close must NOT be cited as evidence any individual counter-argument was answered. Ratified arms: **D1 = (a)** instruction-only (the orchestrator reads the labels and titles from task files it already enumerates — no `parse-completion-notes` widening, so no amendment phase was owed); **D2 = (b)** widen `read_verification`, so Phase 1 RAN; **D3** as drafted; **D4 = (i)** print-only, with offer-and-run recorded as the named strengthening arm on the trigger *"the maintainer observes the printed line going unused"*; **OQ-1** yes, widened to BOTH sources; **OQ-2** no; **OQ-3** accepted bound. **What shipped.** Phase 1: three MECHANICALLY PARSED fields on `read_verification` — `e2e_status` (`str`/`None`), `has_scope_creep_advisory`, `has_leftover_artifacts_advisory` — with `ac_list` / `verdict` / `path` byte-unchanged in shape and **NO `reasons` key**, that absence being a ratified constraint whose test asserts it rather than an oversight: returning the raw list would push token-extraction and line-suppression judgment into the orchestrator, contradicting D3's verbatim rule at the seat D3 governs. Phase 2: `### Review guide` (after `### What was built`) and `### Not verified` (after `### Acceptance criteria`), each with an omit-when-empty condition in the same bracket style `### Deviations from plan` already used; PHASE 3's list renumbered six items → EIGHT; a direct task-file read step; `has_unverified` named as CONSUMED; OQ-1's widening at BOTH enumeration sites; and a new **rule 9** pinning that the two new conditional sections orient and quote but never judge. Phase 3: the `gh pr create --title "<message_used>" --body-file <feature_dir>/summary.md` line in `/devforge:finalize` PHASE 4 and in `references/results-and-docs.md`, byte-identical in both, gated on summary-present AND squash-succeeded, with the omit rule forbidding a placeholder or deferred hint; rule 9's *"downstream command pointer"* narrowed to *"downstream forge command pointer"* and the `## What this block is NOT` third bullet amended so the new line does not read as violating the bullet directly above it. **Reviewer passes:** Phase 1 SHIP-READY with 2 Low fixed; **Phase 2 NOT SHIP-READY on first pass — 2 High, 1 Medium, 1 Low, all fixed**; Phase 3 0/0/2/1, all fixed. ⚠ **THREE CORRECTIONS ARE RECORDED IN THE PLAN SO THEY ARE NOT RE-INHERITED, and the first is the one that matters.** **(1) There is NO `### Two-hats partition` heading in a task file.** An earlier revision described the partition as a subsection by that name and **Phase 2 consumed that wrong description once** before instruction-reviewer caught it. `### Two-hats partition (mixed behavior-change + restructuring tasks)` is a heading in `src/commands/breakdown/main.md` — the name of the INSTRUCTION section — and that lane states in its own text that it *"adds NO flag and NO header line"*; the partition exists in task files only as **behavior-changing / behavior-preserving labels ON the `- In <path>:` entries** in `## Change Details`. A consumer greping for the heading finds nothing and would wrongly conclude no task declares a partition. **(2)** `verification.md` carries `**Reasons**:` UNCAPPED under `## Verdict`; the capped-at-4 `**Key reasons**:` block belongs to `render_inline_summary` — the console block, never written to a file and unreachable to `/devforge:summarize` — so D2(b) as first drafted was unbuildable and was retargeted. **(3)** `e2e_status` can NEVER equal `"off"`: `_verdict.py` appends the bullet only when the status is truthy and `!= "off"`, so both the no-suite and `off` cases arrive as `null`, and a render condition of *"neither `e2e-clean` nor `off`"* would have been true for null and printed a literal `None`. ⚠ **THE HONEST BOUNDS, none strengthened by the build and none droppable from a summary.** **The Review guide is UNCHECKED MODEL JUDGMENT** — no gate reads it, no validator parses it, a wrong file-to-decision mapping produces a misleading guide with no error anywhere; the emitted text states the claim as **orientation, not enforcement** (plan 86 F3's honesty pattern) and forbids claiming completeness. **The Not-verified section QUOTES statuses already recorded** by `/devforge:verify` and `/devforge:implement` and adds no item of its own, so **plan 24 D1 is intact — `/devforge:summarize` stays agent-free, verdict-free and findings-free**, and it deliberately does NOT repeat non-passed ACs, which the checklist above it already annotates. **NOTHING executes `gh`** — the line is printed for the user, `/devforge:finalize` checks no `gh` availability and reads no remote state, and it stays TERMINAL. **No back-port into shipped installs** — they arrive via `install.sh` / `update.sh`. **Non-goals, stated so they are not re-proposed**: any gate, `verify-*` number or hard-fail validator (**plan 75's tripwire holds in both halves**; the only Python is Phase 1's parser widening, **a read, never a check**); any change to `/devforge:verify`'s or `/devforge:review`'s ownership of verdict and findings, or to what `verification.md` CONTAINS; an auto-created PR; any new agent, command or helper verb; and any plan-63/93 count delta (16/4 untouched, no `disable-model-invocation` flag moved). **Phase 4 sweep, recorded per site**: `CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]` gained one entry; **`src/CLAUDE.md` was checked at six sites and every one is a VERIFIED NO-OP** — the pipeline chain, both catalog lines, both `### Command Details` entries, and the Artifact Storage tree (which never lists `summary.md`) describe gating, output paths and commit behavior but never `summary.md`'s section shape or finalize's closing block, and a `six sections|Review guide|Not verified|gh pr` grep over that file returns nothing. **Phase 5 = user-driven consumer e2e HARD GATE, NOT run**, three known-answer anchors: a feature with a mixed task carrying a behavior-change/preserve label and an unverified Done-When box → both sections render naming exactly those · a clean feature → **both ABSENT**, section shape identical to before · `/devforge:finalize` with the summary present and the squash succeeded → the `gh pr create --body-file` line verbatim with the real feature dir substituted. **Anchors 1 and 2 are scored as a PAIR** — a section that always renders passes 1 and fails 2. ⚠ **Anchor 2's fixture must ALSO carry no recorded key decisions in `plan.md`**: the Review guide's omit condition keys off `decisions` being empty AND no task carrying a label — **NOT off verification state** — so a clean, fully-verified feature that recorded decisions will legitimately render the guide, and scoring that as a failure would misread correct behavior. Only the Not-verified section keys off verification state. ⚠ **A clean Phase 5 would be evidence the sections render as designed, NEVER evidence that the gap cost anything** — there was no incident, and there still is none.

## Appendix — Where to find what (full cells)

Relocated **verbatim** from CLAUDE.md's `## Where to find what` table on 2026-08-17; CLAUDE.md keeps a trimmed router. These cells are the full subsystem notes; the named plans remain the authority. Same live-ledger rule: amend here when a subsystem changes.


| Topic | Location |
|---|---|
| Forge architecture decisions | `DEVELOPMENT-STATUS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md` |
| Spec sources | `src/commands/`, `src/agents/`, `src/files/` |
| Generators / emitters | `scripts/emitters/`, `scripts/generate*.py`, `scripts/generate.sh`. `scripts/emitters/claude.py` owns the emitted command layout: the `_PROMOTED` tuple (20 command names) → `.claude/commands/devforge/<name>.md` (flat file inside the `devforge/` namespace subdir → `/devforge:<name>`) + references → `.devforge/command-refs/<name>/` (outside the `.claude/commands/` scan, so reference files never surface as phantom `/<name>:references:*` menu entries). Adding a new `src/commands/<name>/` still requires updating `_PROMOTED` by hand — the generalized loop stays commented out. Of the 20, **13 are model-invocable** and **7 are human-typed only** (they keep `disable-model-invocation: true` in their `main.md` frontmatter: `init-forge`, `generate-docs`, `configure`, `constitute`, `grill`, `spec-check`, `fix`). See `63-SKILL-COLLISION-SUPPRESSION-PLAN.md`. **As of 2026-09-03 (plan 93): 16 model-invocable / 4 human-typed-only — the four setup commands.** |
| Runtime helpers (4-command sequence) | `src/devforge/lib/{init_helper,configure_helper,constitute_helper}.py` + `src/devforge/lib/_generate_docs/` |
| Forcing-functions detectors (consumer-side) | `src/devforge/lib/_constitute/_forcing_functions/` (substrate + per-rule `_magic_enum/`, `_cross_layer/`, `_any_leak/`, `_design_tokens/` + `_setters.py` + `_cmds_forcing_functions.py`); helper verbs `constitute_helper {verify-magic-enum, verify-cross-layer-imports, verify-any-leak, verify-design-tokens, set-forcing-functions, list-forcing-functions}` ship via `.devforge/lib/constitute_helper`; opt-in pre-commit hook template at `src/git-hooks/pre-commit-forcing-functions.sh` installed to `.devforge/templates/git-hooks/` by `install.sh`. Distinct from forge-internal `constitute_helper forge-internal:verify-universal-defaults` (maintainer-side drift detector for `src/constitution.md` vs canonical defaults). **Update-time drift wiring (plan 44):** `update.sh` (before the equal-version bail) + `install.sh`'s brownfield "leaving as-is" branch both source `scripts/constitution-drift-check.sh` (`forge_check_constitution_drift`), which runs `verify-universal-defaults` AND the sibling `forge-internal:verify-forcing-function-keys` (diffs consumer `constitute.json.forcing_functions` keys vs `_schema.py::FORCING_FUNCTION_RULES`) against an already-constituted target — WARN-ONLY + fail-soft (never mutates `constitution.md`/`constitute.json`), telling the user to re-run `/constitute`; greenfield (no `.devforge/constitute.json`) silent-skips. Runs the freshly-shipped TEMPLATE helper, not the installed one. See `44-CONSTITUTION-DRIFT-WIRING-PLAN.md`. |
| Persistent memory — path, probe, reads (plan 74) | `src/devforge/lib/_shared/memory.py` — the SINGLE owner of the `.devforge/memory.md` literal, the three-state probe (`absent` / `stub` / `populated`), and the bounded reads (`read_memory_context` derives present+state+excerpt from ONE read; `read_memory_excerpt` is SECTION-AWARE since plan 79 — `## ` sections parsed, the installer title dropped, `## Task Outcomes` dropped by named denylist, content-free sections dropped heading and all, an equal per-section share of the line budget, newest entries surviving a cut, every cut declared by a marker line under the heading; `read_memory_digest` = 5 non-blank lines). The literal previously drifted across 13 helper files, two of which read a path present in no consumer install. `absent`/`stub` are CORRECT states, never faults — the installer ships a stub into every install, which is why a bare existence check would be near-vacuous and the probe is three-state. `MEMORY_RELATIVE_PATH` is a forward-slash LITERAL, not `os.path.join`-derived: it is compared for exact equality by the `/devforge:specify` gate, and a platform-joined value made `phase1-finalize` exit 2 unconditionally on native Windows. `install.sh` preserves an existing `memory.md` across re-install by COPY-aside + restore with a pre-existing-backup check (plan 74 OQ-5). See `74-MEMORY-LANE-INTEGRITY-PLAN.md`. |
| Memory-lane coverage gate (maintainer-side, plan 74) | `scripts/lib/memory_lane.py` (`find_gaps(repo_root)` + the `DISPOSITIONS` constant: all 20 `_PROMOTED` commands, 13 `READS` / 7 `N/A`, each with a recorded reason) + `scripts/verify-memory-lane.py` CLI + `tests/lib/test_memory_lane.py` (the live-`src/` test IS the permanent gate — no CI/Makefile in repo). Four rules: every command has a disposition with a reason; every `READS` command performs a read **AND** a consuming surface NAMES the field (`memory_excerpt` / `memory_digest` / `memory_state`); every `N/A` command carries none of those tokens; no `.claude/memory` literal under `src/`. **Rule 2 is CONJUNCTIVE — do not loosen it to a disjunction**, or it passes the orphaned-payload defect (preflight builds it, nobody reads it back) that was the verified state of seven preflights; a regression test pins that case as a FAIL. Adding a command to `_PROMOTED` without a disposition fails the gate. Declares its own non-coverage: it does NOT verify that a named consumption is SUBSTANTIVE, that the right entries were selected, or that memory content is accurate. Independent of plan 41's agent-reachability gate; neither imports the other. See `74-MEMORY-LANE-INTEGRITY-PLAN.md`. |
| Agent-executor reachability gate (maintainer-side, plan 41) | `scripts/lib/agent_reachability.py` (`find_orphans(repo_root) -> {orphan_agents, unknown_assignments, relay_only}`) + `scripts/verify-agent-reachability.py` CLI (exit non-zero on any violation) + `tests/lib/test_agent_reachability.py` (the live-`src/` test IS the permanent gate — no CI/Makefile in repo). Fails when a `src/agents/*.md` agent has no executor (literal `subagent_type:` in `src/commands/**`, a `/breakdown` Agent-Assignment table row, or a helper ensemble/refuter list parsed via AST incl. dict KEYS), an assignment names an unknown agent, or a roster agent is relay-only (hard-fail; `RELAY_ONLY_ALLOWLIST` escape, empty). Mechanizes type-1 orphaned-agent ONLY — NOT type-2 forward-prose or type-3 finding-inertness (human conventions). Complements (does not fold) plan 38's consumer-runtime `breakdown_helper verify-agent-roster`. See `41-AGENT-EXECUTOR-REACHABILITY-PLAN.md`. |

Then park-once, read-in-place (NOT re-carried through downstream handoffs). **Binding (built-side wiring):** authored at `/breakdown` PHASE 2.5 → `specs/[feature]/design-manifest.json` (SAME filename, NEW shape — the disposition-manifest successor): `{route, pairs:[{anchor_selector, built_testid}]}` (container-floor pair + opt-in pairs); `design_helper validate-binding` (route + ≥1 pair required). **Runtime two-tier check:** the `design-auditor` at `/review` PHASE 2.5 (conditional on `design/reference.html` + a valid binding, fidelity-only scoped dispatch) runs the deterministic engine — an always-on anchor-FREE sanity floor (overflow / clip / font-not-loaded) + an anchor-gated value+geometry fidelity diff (built computed-style/geometry vs the ACTUAL design source read via the intent reader — the reversal of plan 52's value-proxy narrowing). ALL decision logic is Python (`_design/_bag`+`_finding`+`_floor`+`_fidelity`+`_comparator`+`_cmds_compare`, verb `design_helper compare --built-bag [--intent-bag] [--binding] --route`); the JS assets `_design/js/{built,intent}_reader.js` are thin measurement collectors run via `evaluate_script` (verified at e2e, no JS test infra). Findings + coverage verdict (NOT-COVERED / CLEAN / DEFECT, 7 honesty invariants; #7 platform-mismatch outermost, from stack metadata) embed as a distinct `## Design Fidelity` section in `review.md` via `review_helper render-report --design-section` (OUTSIDE the refutation partition — deterministic probe output is not cross-examined); a non-gating VLM screenshot advisory rides along, never gates. `/plan` reads the anchor (light passive). **Static provenance gate (manifest-INDEPENDENT):** `_constitute/_forcing_functions/_design_tokens/` + `constitute_helper verify-design-tokens` (config block `{enabled, token_source_css}`; Checks 1–4: no hardcoded hex/rgb/hsl/named colors, no `var(--x,<literal>)` fallbacks, undefined-token-fails-loud, `:hover`+`:focus-visible`) at `/implement`'s per-task gate + the pre-commit hook; offered by `/constitute` Section 3.5 (`design_token_provenance` rule; an older config may carry an inert legacy `manifest_path`, ignored). **Manifest→binding TRIGGER backstop (plan 42, retargeted):** `breakdown_helper verify-manifest-present` (shared `_validate_manifest_present` composing `_design.validate_binding`) asserts `design/reference.html` present ⇒ a VALID binding present — the 4th `/breakdown` PHASE 3.5 integrity gate (HARD-halt) + `finalize-handoff` chokepoint. **Contract:** constitution §3.8 Design Fidelity (persisted universal section, `*Backed by* verify-design-tokens` = the static half). **Non-file design source (plan 43, absorbed):** the `**Design source**:` frontmatter (`html`/`figma`/`screenshot`/`none`, `specify_helper set-design-source`, `design_helper check-design-source`) is now the human-readable summary of the anchor; a non-file source with no enforceable `design/reference.html` still emits the plan-43 loud non-blocking WARN. **RETIRED (plan 53):** `resolve-reference`/`init-manifest`/`validate-manifest`, `_DataRefCollector`/`resolve_reference`/`ElementRecord`/disposition (MATCH/DEFER-EMPTY/STATIC-PLACEHOLDER/DEVIATE), `_design_tokens` Check 5, the `/implement` design-manifest tripwire; `_reference.py`→`_css_parse.py`. `_design/` is in the `install.sh`+`update.sh` surgical `--only` always-copy set (cross-cutting runtime dep of `/breakdown`+`/review`+`/implement`). **Figma is the fast-follow SIBLING** — a new `kind` (open discriminator) whose intent reader normalizes live MCP node data against the SAME comparator; today `figma`/non-web → NOT-COVERED. Legacy `research/<date>-<slug>/handoff.json` installs are inert history — no reader, no migration (D3) |
