| `/audit` command (standalone adversarial audit) | `src/commands/audit/main.md` (6-phase spec) + `src/commands/audit/references/{adversarial-preamble,mislogic-checklist,best-practices-checklist,report-format,hotspot-scoring,refutation-preamble}.md` + `src/devforge/lib/_audit/` (helper subpackage: schema `findings_schema`/`hotspot_schema`, `_state`, `_preflight`, `_hotspot`, `_scope`, `_consume`, `_validate`, `_consensus`, `_merge`, `_verify`, `_rank`, `_report`, `_inline`, `_cli`) + launcher `src/devforge/lib/audit_helper{,.py}`; tests `tests/lib/_audit/`. Three scope modes (broad / hotspot `--top N` / narrow); **full-spectrum** — one run hunts mislogic + system design + lang/framework best practices + duplication + constitution adherence, each finding carrying a producer-declared `Category` (`findings_schema.CATEGORY_ENUM`) that `_report` buckets by; orchestrator-written scratch chain lives in a run-scoped `${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/forge-audit` working dir (outside the repo, swept by a single `rm -rf` at end of run); only the report `audits/YYYY-MM-DD-audit.md` + `.state.json` + `.gitignore` live under `audits/`. Mode-conditional `--passes N` (broad/hotspot default 2, narrow default 1; explicit value clamped `[1,3]`) multi-pass union mode runs the dispatch/consume/validate loop K times and merges via `merge-passes` (the `_merge.py` tolerant `(file, line ± 3)` union merger, replacing `compute-consensus` at ≥2 passes) — `--passes 1` is byte-identical to single-pass (merger never invoked; `compute-consensus` runs unchanged). **Refutation / cross-examination stage** (PHASE 4.2.5, after consensus/merge, before recurring/rank) — `_verify.py` + four verbs (`route-refutation`, `render-verify-brief`, `consume-verdicts`, `apply-verdicts`): each surviving finding is routed by `route-refutation` to a NON-AUTHOR finder (cross-examination — refuter ≠ author, picked by priority `[code-reviewer, architect, qa-reviewer, security-reviewer]`) that cross-examines it under `references/refutation-preamble.md` and renders a verdict (`confirmed`/`dismissed`/`uncertain`, default-dismiss unless the defect is demonstrated from quoted code). `apply-verdicts` partitions the result into a **confidence-gated report**: the headline (`## Top N Priorities` + `## Findings by File`) draws from confirmed ∪ high-stakes `[CONTESTED]` findings; dismissed + low-stakes uncertain go to the `## Dismissed / Worth a Glance` appendix. D7 high-stakes guard: `security` + `[CONSTITUTION-VIOLATION]` findings the refuter can't resolve are surfaced in the headline flagged `[CONTESTED]` (never buried), and a grounded constitution violation is never silently dismissed. Multi-pass recurrence is now descriptive only — `pass_count`/`[MULTI-PASS:k]` no longer floor confidence (`_merge`) or inflate the rank score (`_rank`). See `10-AUDIT-COMMAND-PORT-PLAN.md` (port) + `11-AUDIT-FULL-SPECTRUM-PLAN.md` (full-spectrum) + `12-AUDIT-MULTI-PASS-UNION-PLAN.md` (multi-pass) + `19-AUDIT-FALSE-POSITIVE-PRECISION-PLAN.md` (refutation / precision) |
| `/grill` command (design-level adversarial review of the plan) | `src/commands/grill/main.md` + `src/commands/grill/references/{anti-relitigation-preamble,design-attack-checklist,refutation-preamble,report-format}.md` + `src/devforge/lib/_grill/` (helper subpackage: `seed_schema`, `_state`, `_preflight`, `_scope`, `_brief`, `_report`, `_cli` — 12 verbs) + launcher `src/devforge/lib/grill_helper{,.py}` + the adversary `src/agents/devils-advocate.md`; reuses the `src/devforge/lib/_shared/` refutation engine (`route_refutation` / `apply_verdicts` / validation — same as `/audit` + `/review`, passing an architect-excluded `priority=`); tests `tests/lib/_grill/`. Standalone, and **since plan 85 (2026-08-26) a REQUIRED run before `/breakdown`** — the design-level mirror of `/review`, run between `/plan` and `/breakdown` to attack the completed `plan.md` before it is decomposed. (`breakdown_helper verify-grill-ran` gates it on the report's PRESENCE plus the recorded `adversary_status` only — never freshness, never the disposition.) Dispatches a single `devils-advocate` adversary (which does its own three-ring CBM blast-radius traversal + self-gated context7 web-verification, since a Python helper can't call the CBM graph) + an architect-excluded refutation pass `[code-reviewer, qa-reviewer, security-reviewer]`. Writes `specs/[feature]/grill.md` (confidence-gated findings + a recommended 4-way disposition PROCEED / REVISE-PLAN / RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM / KILL) and, when the user's PHASE-7 pick matches the recommendation (REVISE-PLAN pick → `target_stage="plan"`; RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM pick → the nearest upstream stage), a backward `specs/[feature]/grill-seed.json` (`ReEntrySeed` schema, `source = "grill"`, `target_stage ∈ {spec, discovery, research, plan}`) consumed additively/conditionally/inertly (D10 helper-free direct JSON read, no-op when absent) by `/research` / `/discover` / `/specify` (RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM → an upstream stage) and `/plan` (REVISE-PLAN → `target_stage="plan"`, plan 36). The USER owns the verdict at the `/breakdown` gate. See `23-ADVERSARIAL-GRILLING-PLAN.md` + `36-GRILL-UNIVERSAL-REENTRY-PLAN.md` |

VERSIONED `memory.md`+`spec-stamps.jsonl` deltas fold into `/finalize`'s PHASE-2 safety-net `commit-artifacts` so they ride the squash. **Code (plan 56 — a 4th, CODE, class; extended by plan 63):** the install-reproducible helper-code dirs `.devforge/lib`/`bin`/`templates` plus `.devforge/command-refs/` (plan 63 — the relocated slash-command reference files) are gitignored (a 4-line CODE block in the same `src/files/devforge.gitignore` template) + recursively untracked (a dir loop in the same `scripts/devforge-state-migrate.sh`), the node_modules model, so the consumer's CBM graph stops indexing forge internals; `.devforge/template/` (SINGULAR) stays tracked (it is `update.sh`'s agent-merge baseline + enumeration source). `lib`/`bin`/`templates` are regenerated by the installers' `cp -R`, `command-refs/` by the `scripts/emitters/claude.py` emit.
